import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";

export function sendJson(
  res: ServerResponse,
  statusCode: number,
  data: unknown,
  corsOrigin = "*"
): void {
  if (res.writableEnded) return;

  const jsonString = JSON.stringify(data);
  res.writeHead(statusCode, {
    "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
    "Content-Length": Buffer.byteLength(jsonString),
    "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": corsOrigin,
    "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET, POST, PATCH, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS",
    "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, Authorization, X-Workspace-Id",
    "Access-Control-Max-Age": "86400"
  });
  res.end(jsonString);
}

export function sendSuccess<T>(
  res: ServerResponse,
  data: T,
  statusCode = 200,
  corsOrigin = "*"
): void {
  sendJson(res, statusCode, { success: true, data }, corsOrigin);
}

export function sendError(
  res: ServerResponse,
  statusCode: number,
  code: string,
  message: string,
  corsOrigin = "*"
): void {
  sendJson(res, statusCode, { success: false, error: { code, message } }, corsOrigin);
}

export function getStatusCodeForError(code: string): number {
  if (code.includes("RATE_LIMITED")) return 429;
  if (
    code.includes("INVALID_CREDENTIALS") ||
    code.includes("EMAIL_NOT_CONFIRMED") ||
    code.includes("UNAUTHENTICATED")
  ) return 401;
  if (code.includes("PROVIDER_ERROR") || code.includes("NETWORK_ERROR")) return 502;
  if (code.includes("NOT_FOUND")) return 404;
  if (code.includes("ACCESS_DENIED") || code.includes("OWNERSHIP")) return 403;
  if (code.includes("ALREADY_EXISTS") || code.includes("CIRCULAR") || code.includes("CONFLICT")) return 409;
  if (code.includes("INVALID") || code.includes("INPUT") || code.includes("REQUIRED")) return 400;
  return 400;
}

export async function parseJsonBody(req: IncomingMessage): Promise<unknown> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    let raw = "";
    req.setEncoding("utf-8");

    req.on("data", (chunk: string) => {
      raw += chunk;
      if (raw.length > 1024 * 1024) {
        // 1MB limit
        reject(new Error("Request payload too large"));
      }
    });

    req.on("end", () => {
      if (!raw.trim()) {
        resolve({});
        return;
      }
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        resolve(parsed);
      } catch {
        reject(new Error("Invalid JSON body"));
      }
    });

    req.on("error", (err) => {
      reject(err);
    });
  });
}

export function extractBearerToken(req: IncomingMessage): string | null {
  const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
  if (!authHeader) return null;

  const parts = authHeader.trim().split(/\s+/);
  if (parts.length === 2 && parts[0].toLowerCase() === "bearer") {
    return parts[1];
  }
  return null;
}

import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";
import type { ApplicationContainer } from "../application/container.ts";
import { Router } from "./router.ts";
import { extractBearerToken, parseJsonBody, sendError } from "./http.ts";
import type { RequestContext } from "./types.ts";
import { registerHealthRoutes } from "./routes/health.routes.ts";
import { registerAuthRoutes } from "./routes/auth.routes.ts";
import { registerWorkspaceRoutes } from "./routes/workspace.routes.ts";
import { registerProjectRoutes } from "./routes/project.routes.ts";
import { registerGoalRoutes } from "./routes/goal.routes.ts";
import { registerMilestoneRoutes } from "./routes/milestone.routes.ts";
import { registerActionRoutes } from "./routes/action.routes.ts";
import { registerPlanningRoutes } from "./routes/planning.routes.ts";
import { registerProgressRoutes } from "./routes/progress.routes.ts";
import { registerAIRoutes } from "./routes/ai.routes.ts";
import { registerMeasurementRoutes } from "./routes/measurement.routes.ts";

export * from "./types.ts";
export * from "./http.ts";
export * from "./router.ts";

export function createRouter(): Router {
  const router = new Router();

  registerHealthRoutes(router);
  registerAuthRoutes(router);
  registerWorkspaceRoutes(router);
  registerProjectRoutes(router);
  registerGoalRoutes(router);
  registerMilestoneRoutes(router);
  registerActionRoutes(router);
  registerPlanningRoutes(router);
  registerProgressRoutes(router);
  registerAIRoutes(router);
  registerMeasurementRoutes(router);

  return router;
}

export function createApiHandler(
  container: ApplicationContainer,
  corsOrigin = "*"
): (req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse) => Promise<void> {
  const router = createRouter();

  return async (req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse): Promise<void> => {
    // 1. Handle CORS preflight
    if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
      res.writeHead(204, {
        "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": corsOrigin,
        "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET, POST, PATCH, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS",
        "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, Authorization, X-Workspace-Id",
        "Access-Control-Max-Age": "86400"
      });
      res.end();
      return;
    }

    try {
      const parsedUrl = new URL(req.url ?? "/", "http://localhost");
      const pathname = parsedUrl.pathname;
      const query = parsedUrl.searchParams;
      const method = req.method ?? "GET";

      const match = router.match(method, pathname);
      if (!match) {
        sendError(
          res,
          404,
          "ROUTE_NOT_FOUND",
          `Route not found: ${method} ${pathname}`,
          corsOrigin
        );
        return;
      }

      // 2. Authentication check
      let user: import("../modules/auth/auth.types.ts").AuthenticatedUserContext | null = null;
      const token = extractBearerToken(req);
      if (token) {
        user = await container.tokenVerifier.verifyToken(token);
        const selectedWorkspaceId = req.headers["x-workspace-id"];
        if (user && typeof selectedWorkspaceId === "string" && selectedWorkspaceId.trim()) {
          user = { ...user, workspaceId: selectedWorkspaceId.trim() };
        }
      }

      if (match.requiresAuth && !user) {
        sendError(
          res,
          401,
          "UNAUTHORIZED",
          "Authentication required. Please provide a valid Bearer token.",
          corsOrigin
        );
        return;
      }

      // 3. Body parsing for POST / PATCH / PUT
      let body: unknown = null;
      if (["POST", "PATCH", "PUT"].includes(method)) {
        try {
          body = await parseJsonBody(req);
        } catch (err: unknown) {
          sendError(
            res,
            400,
            "INVALID_JSON_BODY",
            (err as Error)?.message || "Malformed JSON body.",
            corsOrigin
          );
          return;
        }
      }

      // 4. Request context
      const ctx: RequestContext = {
        req,
        res,
        container,
        user,
        params: match.params,
        query,
        body,
        corsOrigin
      };

      // 5. Execute handler
      await match.handler(ctx);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      console.error("[API Error]", err);
      if (!res.writableEnded) {
        sendError(
          res,
          500,
          "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
          "An unexpected server error occurred.",
          corsOrigin
        );
      }
    }
  };
}

FILE: backend\src\api\router.ts
============================================================
import type { HttpMethod, RouteHandler, RouteMatch } from "./types.ts";

interface RouteRegistration {
  method: HttpMethod;
  pattern: RegExp;
  paramNames: string[];
  handler: RouteHandler;
  requiresAuth: boolean;
}

export class Router {
  private readonly routes: RouteRegistration[] = [];

  register(
    method: HttpMethod,
    pathPattern: string,
    handler: RouteHandler,
    options: { requiresAuth?: boolean } = {}
  ): void {
    const paramNames: string[] = [];
    const regexPattern = pathPattern
      .replace(/\/+$/, "") // remove trailing slash
      .replace(/:([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)/g, (_, paramName) => {
        paramNames.push(paramName);
        return "([^/]+)";
      });

    const pattern = new RegExp(`^${regexPattern || "/"}$`);
    this.routes.push({
      method,
      pattern,
      paramNames,
      handler,
      requiresAuth: options.requiresAuth ?? true
    });
  }

  get(path: string, handler: RouteHandler, options?: { requiresAuth?: boolean }): void {
    this.register("GET", path, handler, options);
  }

  post(path: string, handler: RouteHandler, options?: { requiresAuth?: boolean }): void {
    this.register("POST", path, handler, options);
  }

  patch(path: string, handler: RouteHandler, options?: { requiresAuth?: boolean }): void {
    this.register("PATCH", path, handler, options);
  }

  delete(path: string, handler: RouteHandler, options?: { requiresAuth?: boolean }): void {
    this.register("DELETE", path, handler, options);
  }

  match(method: string, pathname: string): RouteMatch | null {
    const cleanPath = pathname.length > 1 && pathname.endsWith("/") ? pathname.slice(0, -1) : pathname;

    for (const route of this.routes) {
      if (route.method !== method) continue;

      const match = route.pattern.exec(cleanPath);
      if (match) {
        const params: Record<string, string> = {};
        for (let i = 0; i < route.paramNames.length; i++) {
          params[route.paramNames[i]] = decodeURIComponent(match[i + 1]);
        }
        return {
          handler: route.handler,
          params,
          requiresAuth: route.requiresAuth
        };
      }
    }

    return null;
  }
}

FILE: backend\src\api\types.ts

import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";
import type { AuthenticatedUserContext } from "../modules/auth/auth.types.ts";
import type { ApplicationContainer } from "../application/container.ts";

export type HttpMethod = "GET" | "POST" | "PUT" | "PATCH" | "DELETE" | "OPTIONS" | "HEAD";

export interface RequestContext {
  req: IncomingMessage;
  res: ServerResponse;
  container: ApplicationContainer;
  user: AuthenticatedUserContext | null;
  params: Record<string, string>;
  query: URLSearchParams;
  body: unknown;
  corsOrigin: string;
}

export type RouteHandler = (ctx: RequestContext) => Promise<void> | void;

export interface RouteMatch {
  handler: RouteHandler;
  params: Record<string, string>;
  requiresAuth: boolean;
}

import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import type {
  ActionStatus,
  CreateActionInput,
  RecordExecutionInput,
  UpdateActionInput
} from "../../modules/action/action.types.ts";

export function registerActionRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.post(
    "/api/actions",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as CreateActionInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.createAction(ctx.user!, body);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/goals/:goalId/actions",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.listActionsByGoal(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.goalId
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/actions",
    async (ctx) => {
      const milestoneId = ctx.query.get("milestoneId");
      const goalId = ctx.query.get("goalId");

      if (milestoneId) {
        const result = await ctx.container.actionService.listActionsByMilestone(
          ctx.user!,
          milestoneId
        );
        if (!result.success) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }

      if (goalId) {
        const result = await ctx.container.actionService.listActionsByGoal(
          ctx.user!,
          goalId
        );
        if (!result.success) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }

      sendError(
        ctx.res,
        400,
        "ACTION_INVALID_INPUT",
        "Either goalId or milestoneId query parameter is required.",
        ctx.corsOrigin
      );
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/actions/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.getAction(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.patch(
    "/api/actions/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as UpdateActionInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.updateAction(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id,
        body
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.patch(
    "/api/actions/:id/status",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as { status?: ActionStatus };
      if (!body.status) {
        sendError(
          ctx.res,
          400,
          "ACTION_INVALID_STATUS",
          "status field is required.",
          ctx.corsOrigin
        );
        return;
      }
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.updateActionStatus(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id,
        body.status
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/actions/:id/complete",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.completeAction(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/actions/:id/execution",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as RecordExecutionInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.recordExecution(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id,
        body
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/actions/:id/execution",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.getExecutionData(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );
}

FILE: backend\src\api\routes\ai.routes.ts

import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import type { RouteHandler } from "../types.ts";
import type { AIOperationType, AIProposalSelection } from "../../modules/ai-orchestration/index.ts";
import { AIOrchestrationError } from "../../modules/ai-orchestration/index.ts";

function aiStatus(code: string): number {
  if (code === "AI_UNAUTHENTICATED") return 401;
  if (code === "AI_ACCESS_DENIED" || code === "AI_PROPOSAL_ACCESS_DENIED") return 403;
  if (code === "AI_PROPOSAL_NOT_FOUND") return 404;
  if (code === "AI_USAGE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED" || code === "AI_PROVIDER_RATE_LIMITED") return 429;
  if (code === "AI_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE") return 503;
  if (code.startsWith("AI_INVALID") || code === "AI_APPROVAL_REQUIRED" || code === "AI_OUTPUT_INVALID") return 400;
  if (code.startsWith("AI_PROVIDER") || code === "AI_REQUEST_TIMEOUT") return 502;
  return 409;
}

function bodyObject(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  return value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
    ? value as Record<string, unknown> : null;
}

export function registerAIRoutes(router: Router): void {
  const run = (operation: AIOperationType): RouteHandler => async (ctx) => {
    const body = bodyObject(ctx.body);
    if (typeof body?.input !== "string" || !body.input.trim()) {
      sendError(ctx.res, 400, "AI_INVALID_REQUEST", "A goal description is required.", ctx.corsOrigin);
      return;
    }
    try {
      const result = await ctx.container.aiService.execute(ctx.user!, operation, body.input);
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) {
      const failure = error instanceof AIOrchestrationError
        ? error : new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROVIDER_ERROR", "AI assistance is temporarily unavailable.", error);
      sendError(ctx.res, aiStatus(failure.code), failure.code, failure.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
    }
  };

  router.post("/api/ai/goal-understanding", run("GOAL_CLARIFICATION"));
  router.post("/api/ai/feasibility", run("GOAL_FEASIBILITY"));
  router.post("/api/ai/goal-proposals", run("GOAL_TREE_PROPOSAL"));

  router.get("/api/ai/proposals/:id", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.aiService.getProposal(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id), 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) {
      const failure = error instanceof AIOrchestrationError ? error : new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PERSISTENCE_ERROR", "Unable to load proposal.");
      sendError(ctx.res, aiStatus(failure.code), failure.code, failure.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
    }
  });

  router.patch("/api/ai/proposals/:id", async (ctx) => {
    const body = bodyObject(ctx.body);
    if (!body || !("proposalData" in body)) {
      sendError(ctx.res, 400, "AI_INVALID_REQUEST", "proposalData is required.", ctx.corsOrigin);
      return;
    }
    try {
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.aiService.updateProposal(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id, body.proposalData), 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) {
      const failure = error instanceof AIOrchestrationError ? error : new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PERSISTENCE_ERROR", "Unable to update proposal.");
      sendError(ctx.res, aiStatus(failure.code), failure.code, failure.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
    }
  });

  router.post("/api/ai/proposals/:id/reject", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.aiService.rejectProposal(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id), 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) {
      const failure = error instanceof AIOrchestrationError ? error : new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PERSISTENCE_ERROR", "Unable to reject proposal.");
      sendError(ctx.res, aiStatus(failure.code), failure.code, failure.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
    }
  });

  router.post("/api/ai/proposals/:id/apply", async (ctx) => {
    const body = bodyObject(ctx.body) as { selection?: AIProposalSelection } | null;
    if (!body?.selection || !Array.isArray(body.selection.selectedItemIds)) {
      sendError(ctx.res, 400, "AI_APPROVAL_REQUIRED", "Select proposal items before approving.", ctx.corsOrigin);
      return;
    }
    try {
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.aiService.approveAndApplyProposal(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id, body.selection), 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) {
      const failure = error instanceof AIOrchestrationError ? error : new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_APPLICATION_FAILED", "Unable to apply proposal.");
      sendError(ctx.res, aiStatus(failure.code), failure.code, failure.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
    }
  });
}

import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";

export function registerAuthRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.post(
    "/api/auth/register",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as {
        email?: string;
        password?: string;
      };

      if (
        typeof body.email !== "string" ||
        typeof body.password !== "string" ||
        !body.email.trim() ||
        !body.password.trim()
      ) {
        sendError(
          ctx.res,
          400,
          "AUTH_INVALID_INPUT",
          "Email and password are required.",
          ctx.corsOrigin
        );
        return;
      }

      const result = await ctx.container.authApiService.register({
        email: body.email,
        password: body.password
      });

      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);

        sendError(
          ctx.res,
          status,
          result.error.code,
          result.error.message,
          ctx.corsOrigin
        );
        return;
      }

      sendSuccess(
        ctx.res,
        {
          ...result.data.registration,
          token: result.data.accessToken
        },
        201,
        ctx.corsOrigin
      );
    },
    { requiresAuth: false }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/auth/login",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as {
        email?: string;
        password?: string;
      };

      if (
        typeof body.email !== "string" ||
        typeof body.password !== "string" ||
        !body.email.trim() ||
        !body.password.trim()
      ) {
        sendError(
          ctx.res,
          400,
          "AUTH_INVALID_INPUT",
          "Email and password are required.",
          ctx.corsOrigin
        );
        return;
      }

      const result = await ctx.container.authApiService.signIn({
        email: body.email,
        password: body.password
      });

      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);

        sendError(
          ctx.res,
          status,
          result.error.code,
          result.error.message,
          ctx.corsOrigin
        );
        return;
      }

      if (!result.data.accessToken) {
        sendError(
          ctx.res,
          500,
          "AUTH_PROVIDER_ERROR",
          "Authentication provider did not return an access token.",
          ctx.corsOrigin
        );
        return;
      }

      sendSuccess(
        ctx.res,
        {
          user: result.data.user,
          token: result.data.accessToken
        },
        200,
        ctx.corsOrigin
      );
    },
    { requiresAuth: false }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/auth/logout",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.authService.signOut();

      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);

        sendError(
          ctx.res,
          status,
          result.error.code,
          result.error.message,
          ctx.corsOrigin
        );
        return;
      }

      sendSuccess(
        ctx.res,
        {
          message: "Signed out successfully."
        },
        200,
        ctx.corsOrigin
      );
    },
    { requiresAuth: false }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/auth/session",
    async (ctx) => {
      if (!ctx.user) {
        sendError(
          ctx.res,
          401,
          "AUTH_UNAUTHENTICATED",
          "Authentication required.",
          ctx.corsOrigin
        );
        return;
      }

      sendSuccess(
        ctx.res,
        {
          user: ctx.user
        },
        200,
        ctx.corsOrigin
      );
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );
}

FILE: backend\src\api\routes\goal.routes.ts

import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import type {
  CreateGoalInput,
  GoalScope,
  ListGoalsFilter,
  UpdateGoalInput
} from "../../modules/goal/goal.types.ts";

export function registerGoalRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.post(
    "/api/goals",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as CreateGoalInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.goalService.createGoal(ctx.user!, body);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/goals/hierarchy",
    async (ctx) => {
      const scope: GoalScope = {};
      const projectId = ctx.query.get("projectId");
      const rootGoalId = ctx.query.get("rootGoalId");
      const includeArchived = ctx.query.get("includeArchived");

      if (projectId) scope.projectId = projectId;
      if (rootGoalId) scope.rootGoalId = rootGoalId;
      if (includeArchived === "true") scope.includeArchived = true;

      const result = await ctx.container.goalService.getGoalHierarchy(ctx.user!, scope);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/goals",
    async (ctx) => {
      const filter: ListGoalsFilter = {};
      const projectId = ctx.query.get("projectId");
      const parentGoalId = ctx.query.get("parentGoalId");
      const statusParam = ctx.query.get("status");
      const includeArchived = ctx.query.get("includeArchived");

      if (projectId !== null) filter.projectId = projectId;
      if (parentGoalId !== null) filter.parentGoalId = parentGoalId === "null" ? null : parentGoalId;
      if (statusParam) filter.status = statusParam;
      if (includeArchived === "true") filter.includeArchived = true;

      const result = await ctx.container.goalService.listGoals(ctx.user!, filter);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/goals/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.goalService.getGoal(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.patch(
    "/api/goals/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as UpdateGoalInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.goalService.updateGoal(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id,
        body
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/goals/:id/children",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as Omit<CreateGoalInput, "projectId" | "parentGoalId">;
      const result = await ctx.container.goalService.addChildGoal(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id,
        body
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );
}

FILE: backend\src\api\routes\measurement.routes.ts
============================================================
import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import type { RequestContext } from "../types.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import { MeasurementError } from "../../modules/measurement/index.ts";
import type { CreateMeasurementInput, MeasurementHistoryQuery, RecordMeasurementValueInput, UpdateMeasurementInput } from "../../modules/measurement/measurement.types.ts";

function query(ctx: RequestContext): MeasurementHistoryQuery {
  const offset = ctx.query.get("offset");
  const limit = ctx.query.get("limit");
  return {
    ...(ctx.query.has("from") ? { from: ctx.query.get("from")! } : {}),
    ...(ctx.query.has("to") ? { to: ctx.query.get("to")! } : {}),
    ...(offset !== null ? { offset: Number(offset) } : {}),
    ...(limit !== null ? { limit: Number(limit) } : {}),
  };
}

export function registerMeasurementRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.get("/api/goals/:goalId/measurements", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      const result = await ctx.container.measurementService.listMeasurementsByGoal(ctx.user!, ctx.params.goalId);
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });

  router.post("/api/measurements", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      const result = await ctx.container.measurementService.createMeasurement(ctx.user!, (ctx.body ?? {}) as CreateMeasurementInput);
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });

  router.get("/api/measurements/:id", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.measurementService.getMeasurement(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id), 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });

  router.patch("/api/measurements/:id", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.measurementService.updateMeasurement(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id, (ctx.body ?? {}) as UpdateMeasurementInput), 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });

  router.delete("/api/measurements/:id", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.measurementService.archiveMeasurement(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id), 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });

  router.post("/api/measurements/:id/records", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as Omit<RecordMeasurementValueInput, "measurementId">;
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.measurementService.recordMeasurementValue(ctx.user!, { ...body, measurementId: ctx.params.id }), 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });

  router.get("/api/measurements/:id/current", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.measurementService.getCurrentMeasurementValue(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id), 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });

  router.get("/api/measurements/:id/history", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.measurementService.getMeasurementHistory(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id, query(ctx)), 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });
}

function sendFailure(ctx: RequestContext, error: unknown): void {
  if (error instanceof MeasurementError) {
    sendError(ctx.res, getStatusCodeForError(error.code), error.code, error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
    return;
  }
  sendError(ctx.res, 400, "MEASUREMENT_ERROR", "Measurement request could not be completed.", ctx.corsOrigin);
}

import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import type {
  CreateMilestoneInput,
  UpdateMilestoneInput
} from "../../modules/milestone/milestone.types.ts";

export function registerMilestoneRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.post(
    "/api/milestones",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as CreateMilestoneInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.milestoneService.createMilestone(ctx.user!, body);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/goals/:goalId/milestones",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.milestoneService.listMilestonesByGoal(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.goalId
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/milestones",
    async (ctx) => {
      const goalId = ctx.query.get("goalId");
      if (!goalId) {
        sendError(
          ctx.res,
          400,
          "MILESTONE_INVALID_INPUT",
          "goalId query parameter is required.",
          ctx.corsOrigin
        );
        return;
      }
      const result = await ctx.container.milestoneService.listMilestonesByGoal(
        ctx.user!,
        goalId
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/milestones/:id",

      const result = await ctx.container.milestoneService.getMilestone(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.patch(
    "/api/milestones/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as UpdateMilestoneInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.milestoneService.updateMilestone(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id,
        body
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/milestones/:id/complete",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.milestoneService.completeMilestone(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import { PlanningError } from "../../modules/planning/planning.errors.ts";
import type {
  ApprovedPlanningProposal,
  CreateDailyPlanInput,
  CreateScheduleInput,
  UpdateDailyPlanInput,
  UpdateScheduleInput
} from "../../modules/planning/planning.types.ts";

export function registerPlanningRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.post(
    "/api/planning/daily-plans",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as CreateDailyPlanInput;
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.createDailyPlan(ctx.user!, body);
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/planning/daily-plans/:date",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.getDailyPlan(ctx.user!, ctx.params.date);
        if (!result) {
          sendError(
            ctx.res,
            404,
            "DAILY_PLAN_NOT_FOUND",
            `Daily Plan not found for date ${ctx.params.date}`,
            ctx.corsOrigin
          );
          return;
        }
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.patch(
    "/api/planning/daily-plans/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as UpdateDailyPlanInput;

          ctx.user!,
          ctx.params.id,
          body
        );

          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;

        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/planning/daily-plans/:id/actions",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as Omit<CreateScheduleInput, "dailyPlanId">;
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.addActionToDailyPlan(
          ctx.user!,
          ctx.params.id,
          body
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.delete(
    "/api/planning/daily-plans/:id/actions/:scheduleId",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.removeActionFromDailyPlan(
          ctx.user!,
          ctx.params.id,
          ctx.params.scheduleId
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {

      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(

        const endDate = ctx.query.get("endDate");
        if (!startDate || !endDate) {

          );
          return;

        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.getSchedulesByDateRange(
          ctx.user!,

      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }

  router.get(

      try {
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.getSchedule(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id);
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {

          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.patch(
    "/api/planning/schedules/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as UpdateScheduleInput;
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.updateSchedule(
          ctx.user!,
          ctx.params.id,
          body
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.delete(
    "/api/planning/schedules/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        await ctx.container.planningService.deleteSchedule(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id);
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, { deleted: true }, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/planning/proposals/apply",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as ApprovedPlanningProposal;
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.applyApprovedPlanningProposal(
          ctx.user!,
          body
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );
}

import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import { ProgressError } from "../../modules/progress/progress.errors.ts";

export function registerProgressRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.get(
    "/api/progress/goals/:goalId",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const result = await ctx.container.progressService.getGoalProgressSummary(
          ctx.user!,
          ctx.params.goalId
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof ProgressError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PROGRESS_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/progress/summary",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as { goalIds?: string[] };
        if (!body.goalIds || !Array.isArray(body.goalIds)) {
          sendError(
            ctx.res,
            400,
            "PROGRESS_INVALID_INPUT",
            "goalIds array is required.",
            ctx.corsOrigin
          );
          return;
        }

        const result = await ctx.container.progressService.getProgressSummary(
          ctx.user!,
          body.goalIds
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof ProgressError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PROGRESS_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/progress/reviews/:goalId",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const result = await ctx.container.progressService.getProgressDataForReview(
          ctx.user!,
          ctx.params.goalId
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof ProgressError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PROGRESS_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\api\routes\project.routes.ts
============================================================
import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import type { CreateProjectInput, UpdateProjectInput } from "../../modules/workspace/workspace.types.ts";

export function registerProjectRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.post(
    "/api/projects",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as CreateProjectInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.createProject(ctx.user!, body);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/projects",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.listProjects(ctx.user!);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/projects/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.getProject(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.patch(
    "/api/projects/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as UpdateProjectInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.updateProject(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id,
        body
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/projects/:id/overview",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.getProjectOverview(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );
}

import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";

export function registerWorkspaceRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.get(
    "/api/workspaces",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.listWorkspaces(ctx.user!);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/workspaces",
    async (ctx) => {
      const name = (ctx.body as { name?: unknown } | null)?.name;
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.createWorkspace(ctx.user!, typeof name === "string" ? name : "");
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.delete(
    "/api/workspaces/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.deleteWorkspace(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, { deleted: result.data }, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/workspaces/initialize",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.initializePersonalWorkspace(ctx.user!);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/workspaces/current",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.getCurrentWorkspace(ctx.user!);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/workspaces/overview",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.getWorkspaceOverview(ctx.user!);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\application\container.ts
============================================================

import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../infrastructure/database/sqlite.ts";
import { AIOrchestrationService, GeminiAIProvider, SqliteAIProposalRepository,
  GoalDiscoveryContextReader, GoalDiscoveryOutputValidator, GoalProposalDomainApplier,
  AIUsageLimiter } from "../modules/ai-orchestration/index.ts";
import type { AIProvider } from "../modules/ai-orchestration/ai-orchestration.repository";

import {
  SqliteWorkspaceRepository,
  SqliteGoalRepository,
  SqliteMilestoneRepository,
  SqliteActionRepository,
  SqlitePlanningRepository
} from "../infrastructure/repositories/index.ts";

import {
  createAuthProvider,
  createTokenVerifier,
  type AuthConfig,
  type TokenVerifier
} from "../infrastructure/auth/index.ts";

import {
  createAuthenticationService,
  type AuthenticationService
} from "../modules/auth/index.ts";

import {
  createAuthenticationApiService,
  type AuthenticationApiService
} from "../modules/auth/auth.service.ts";

import {
  createWorkspaceService,
  type WorkspaceService,
  type GoalSummaryReader
} from "../modules/workspace/index.ts";

import {
  createGoalService,
  type GoalService,
  type GoalWorkspaceAccess
} from "../modules/goal/index.ts";

import {
  createMilestoneService,
  type MilestoneService,
  type MilestoneGoalAccess
} from "../modules/milestone/index.ts";

import {
  createActionService,
  type ActionService,
  type ActionGoalAccess,
  type ActionMilestoneAccess
} from "../modules/action/index.ts";

import {
  PlanningService,
  PlanningError,
  type ActionAccess,
  type PlanningAccess,
  type PlanningProposalApproval,
  type PlanningRules
} from "../modules/planning/index.ts";

import {
  ProgressService,
  ProgressError,
  type ProgressRules,
  type ProgressSourceAccess
} from "../modules/progress/index.ts";
import { MeasurementError, MeasurementService, MVPMeasurementRules } from "../modules/measurement/index.ts";
import { SqliteMeasurementRepository } from "../infrastructure/repositories/sqlite-measurement.repository.ts";

export interface ApplicationContainer {
  connection: DatabaseConnection;
  authService: AuthenticationService;
  authApiService: AuthenticationApiService;
  tokenVerifier: TokenVerifier;
  workspaceService: WorkspaceService;
  goalService: GoalService;
  milestoneService: MilestoneService;
  actionService: ActionService;
  planningService: PlanningService;
  progressService: ProgressService;
  measurementService: MeasurementService;
  aiService: AIOrchestrationService;
  repositories: {
    workspace: SqliteWorkspaceRepository;
    goal: SqliteGoalRepository;
    milestone: SqliteMilestoneRepository;
    action: SqliteActionRepository;
    planning: SqlitePlanningRepository;
  };
}

export interface ContainerConfig {
  auth?: AuthConfig;
  ai?: { apiKey?: string; model?: string; timeoutMs?: number; perUserDaily?: number; globalDaily?: number; provider?: AIProvider };
}

export function createApplicationContainer(
  connection: DatabaseConnection,
  config: ContainerConfig = {}
): ApplicationContainer {
  // 1. Repositories
  const workspaceRepo = new SqliteWorkspaceRepository(connection);
  const goalRepo = new SqliteGoalRepository(connection);
  const milestoneRepo = new SqliteMilestoneRepository(connection);
  const actionRepo = new SqliteActionRepository(connection);
  const planningRepo = new SqlitePlanningRepository(connection);
  const measurementRepo = new SqliteMeasurementRepository(connection);

  // 2. Auth & Token Verifier
  const authConfig: AuthConfig = config.auth ?? {};
  const authProvider = createAuthProvider(authConfig);

  const authService = createAuthenticationService(authProvider);
  const authApiService = createAuthenticationApiService(authProvider);

  const tokenVerifier = createTokenVerifier({
    supabaseUrl: authConfig.supabaseUrl,
    supabaseKey: authConfig.supabaseAnonKey,
    devMode: authConfig.devMode ?? false
  });

  // 3. B02 Workspace Service
  const goalSummaryReader: GoalSummaryReader = {
    async getIndependentGoalCount(workspaceId: string): Promise<number> {
      return goalRepo.countIndependentGoals(workspaceId);
    },

    async getProjectGoalCount(projectId: string): Promise<number> {
      return goalRepo.countProjectGoals(projectId);
    }
  };

  const workspaceService = createWorkspaceService(
    workspaceRepo,
    goalSummaryReader
  );

  // 4. B03 Goal Service
  const goalWorkspaceAccess: GoalWorkspaceAccess = {
    async getCurrentWorkspaceId(userId: string, selectedWorkspaceId?: string): Promise<string | null> {
      const ws = selectedWorkspaceId
        ? await workspaceRepo.findWorkspaceById(selectedWorkspaceId)
        : await workspaceRepo.findWorkspaceByUserId(userId);
      if (ws && ws.userId !== userId) return null;
      return ws?.id ?? null;
    },

    async validateProjectInWorkspace(
      userId: string,
      workspaceId: string,
      projectId: string
    ): Promise<boolean> {
      const project = await workspaceRepo.findProjectById(projectId);

      if (!project || project.workspaceId !== workspaceId) {
        return false;
      }

      const ws = await workspaceRepo.findWorkspaceById(workspaceId);
      return ws?.userId === userId;
    }
  };

  const goalService = createGoalService(
    goalRepo,
    goalWorkspaceAccess
  );

  // 5. B04 Milestone Service
  const milestoneGoalAccess: MilestoneGoalAccess = {
    async getGoalForUser(user, goalId) {
      const res = await goalService.getGoal(user, goalId);

      if (!res.success) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: res.error
        };
      }

      return {
        success: true,
        data: {
          id: res.data.id,
          status: res.data.status,
          archivedAt: res.data.archivedAt
        }
      };
    }
  };

  const milestoneService = createMilestoneService(
    milestoneRepo,
    milestoneGoalAccess
  );

  // 6. B05 Action Service
  const actionGoalAccess: ActionGoalAccess = {
    async validateGoal(user, goalId) {
      const res = await goalService.getGoal(user, goalId);

      if (!res.success) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: res.error
        };
      }

      return {
        success: true,
        data: {
          id: res.data.id
        }
      };
    }
  };

  const actionMilestoneAccess: ActionMilestoneAccess = {
    async validateMilestone(user, milestoneId, goalId) {
      const res = await milestoneService.getMilestone(
        user,
        milestoneId
      );

      if (!res.success) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: res.error
        };
      }

      if (res.data.goalId !== goalId) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: {
            code: "ACTION_INVALID_ASSOCIATION",
            message: "Milestone does not belong to specified Goal"
          }
        };
      }

      return {
        success: true,
        data: {
          id: res.data.id,
          goalId: res.data.goalId
        }
      };
    }
  };

  const actionService = createActionService(
    actionRepo,
    actionGoalAccess,
    actionMilestoneAccess
  );

  // 7. B07 Planning Service
  const actionAccess: ActionAccess = {
    async assertCanAccessAction(user, actionId) {
      const res = await actionService.getAction(user, actionId);

      if (!res.success) {
        throw new PlanningError(
          "SCHEDULE_ACCESS_DENIED",
          "Cannot access the referenced Action."
        );
      }
    }
  };

  const planningAccess: PlanningAccess = {
    async assertCanAccessDailyPlan(user, plan) {
      if (plan.userId !== user.userId) {
        throw new PlanningError(
          "DAILY_PLAN_ACCESS_DENIED",
          "Access denied to the specified Daily Plan."
        );
      }
    },

    async assertCanAccessSchedule(user, schedule) {
      await actionAccess.assertCanAccessAction(
        user,
        schedule.actionId
      );

      if (schedule.dailyPlanId) {
        const plan = await planningRepo.getDailyPlan(
          schedule.dailyPlanId
        );

        if (plan && plan.userId !== user.userId) {
          throw new PlanningError(
            "SCHEDULE_ACCESS_DENIED",
            "Schedule belongs to another user's Daily Plan."
          );
        }
      }
    }
  };

  const planningRules: PlanningRules = {
    async assertScheduleAllowed({ userId, startAt, endAt, excludeScheduleId }) {
      const start = Date.parse(startAt);
      const end = Date.parse(endAt);

      if (
        !Number.isFinite(start) ||
        !Number.isFinite(end) ||
        end <= start
      ) {
        throw new PlanningError(
          "SCHEDULE_INVALID_TIME_RANGE",
          "Schedule end time must be after start time."
        );
      }
      const overlaps = await planningRepo.findOverlappingSchedules(userId, startAt, endAt, excludeScheduleId);
      if (overlaps.length) {
        throw new PlanningError("SCHEDULE_CONFLICT", "This time overlaps another scheduled Action. Choose a different time.");
      }
    },

    assertScheduleMatchesPlanDate({ startAt, dailyPlan }) {
      if (!startAt.startsWith(dailyPlan.date)) {
        throw new PlanningError(
          "SCHEDULE_INVALID_RELATIONSHIP",
          "Schedule start time must match the Daily Plan date."
        );
      }
    }
  };

  const proposalApproval: PlanningProposalApproval = {
    async assertApprovedByUser(user, proposalId) {
      if (!user || !user.userId || !proposalId) {
        throw new PlanningError(
          "PLANNING_APPROVAL_REQUIRED",
          "Proposal approval verification failed."
        );
      }
    }
  };

  const planningService = new PlanningService(
    planningRepo,
    actionAccess,
    planningAccess,
    planningRules,
    proposalApproval
  );

  // 8. B08 Progress Service
  const progressSources: ProgressSourceAccess = {
    async assertCanAccessGoal(user, goalId) {
      const res = await goalService.getGoal(user, goalId);

      if (!res.success) {
        throw new ProgressError(
          "PROGRESS_ACCESS_DENIED",
          "Access denied to the specified Goal."
        );
      }
    },

    async getMeasurementProgressInputs() {
      // B06 is intentionally not integrated
      return [];
    },

    async getActionProgressInput(_user, goalId) {
      const actions = await actionRepo.listByGoal(goalId);
      return {
        goalId,
        actions
      };
    },

    async getChildGoalIds(user, goalId) {
      const wsId = await goalWorkspaceAccess.getCurrentWorkspaceId(
        user.userId,
        user.workspaceId
      );

      if (!wsId) return [];

      const children = await goalRepo.listGoalsByWorkspaceId(
        wsId,
        {
          parentGoalId: goalId
        }
      );

      return children.map((c) => c.id);
    },

    async getGoalProgressInputs(user, goalId) {
      const childGoalIds = await this.getChildGoalIds(
        user,
        goalId
      );

      const actions = (await actionRepo.listByGoal(goalId)).filter((action) => !action.archivedAt);
      const completedCount = actions.filter((action) => action.status === "COMPLETED").length;
      const definitions = await measurementRepo.listMeasurementsByGoal(goalId, false);
      const measurementResults = await Promise.all(definitions.map(async (measurement) => {
        const record = (await measurementRepo.listRecords(measurement.id, { limit: 1 }))[0] ?? null;
        const comparable = record !== null && measurement.targetValue !== null;
        return {
          measurementId: measurement.id,
          currentValue: record?.value ?? null,
          targetValue: measurement.targetValue,
          progressValue: null,
          calculationStatus: comparable ? "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED" as const : "INSUFFICIENT_DATA" as const,
          calculationContext: {
            unit: measurement.unit,
            latestRecordedAt: record?.recordedAt ?? null,
            reason: !record ? "No value has been recorded." : measurement.targetValue === null ? "No target is defined." : "Target direction is undefined; no percentage is inferred."
          }
        };
      }));

      return {
        measurementResults,

        actionResult: {
          goalId,
          totalActions: actions.length,
          completedActions: completedCount,
          incompleteActions: actions.length - completedCount,
          completionValue: actions.length ? (completedCount / actions.length) * 100 : null,
          calculationStatus: actions.length ? "CALCULATED" as const : "NOT_APPLICABLE" as const,
          calculationContext: {
            note: "Completed active Actions divided by total active Actions; this is execution completion, not Goal outcome progress."
          }
        },

        childGoalResults: childGoalIds.map((id) => ({
          goalId: id,
          progressValue: null,
          calculationStatus: "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED" as const,
          contributingSources: [],
          calculationContext: {}
        }))
      };
    }
  };

  const progressRules: ProgressRules = {
    calculateMeasurement() {
      return {
        measurementId: "",
        currentValue: 0,
        targetValue: 0,
        progressValue: null,
        calculationStatus: "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED",
        calculationContext: {}
      };
    },

    calculateActionCompletion(input: unknown) {
      const typed = (input ?? {}) as { goalId?: string; actions?: Array<{ status?: string; archived?: boolean }> };
      const actions = (typed.actions ?? []).filter((action) => !action.archived);
      const total = actions.length;
      const completed = actions.filter((action) => action.status === "COMPLETED").length;
      return {
        goalId: typed.goalId ?? "", totalActions: total, completedActions: completed,
        incompleteActions: total - completed,
        completionValue: total ? (completed / total) * 100 : null,
        calculationStatus: total ? "CALCULATED" as const : "NOT_APPLICABLE" as const,
        calculationContext: { rule: "completed active Actions / total active Actions", metric: "ACTION_COMPLETION" }
      };
    },

    calculateGoal(input: unknown) {
      const typed = (input ?? {}) as {
        goalId?: string;
      };

      return {
        goalId: typed.goalId ?? "",
        progressValue: null,
        calculationStatus: "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED",
        contributingSources: [],
        calculationContext: {}
      };
    },

    aggregateChildGoals() {
      return {
        goalId: "",
        progressValue: null,
        calculationStatus: "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED",
        contributingSources: [],
        calculationContext: {}
      };
    },

    compareMeasurement() {
      return {
        difference: null,
        comparisonStatus: "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED",
        progressValue: null
      };
    },

    combineStatuses() {
      return "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED";
    }
  };

  const progressService = new ProgressService(
    progressSources,
    progressRules
  );

  const measurementService = new MeasurementService(
    measurementRepo,
    {
      async assertCanAccessGoal(user, goalId) {
        const result = await goalService.getGoal(user, goalId);
        if (!result.success) throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_ACCESS_DENIED", "Goal is not accessible.");
      }
    },
    new MVPMeasurementRules(),
  );

  const aiConfig = config.ai ?? {};
  const aiService = new AIOrchestrationService(
    new GoalDiscoveryContextReader(),
    aiConfig.provider ?? new GeminiAIProvider(aiConfig.apiKey, aiConfig.model, aiConfig.timeoutMs),
    new GoalDiscoveryOutputValidator(18),
    new SqliteAIProposalRepository(connection),
    new GoalProposalDomainApplier({ goalService, milestoneService, actionService, measurementService }),
    {
      validation: { maxInputLength: 4000, maxContextBytes: 12000, maxProposalItems: 18 },
      now: () => new Date().toISOString(),
      createProposalId: randomUUID,
      proposalPersistenceEnabled: true,
    },
    new AIUsageLimiter(connection, aiConfig.perUserDaily ?? 12, aiConfig.globalDaily ?? 90),
  );

  return {
    connection,
    authService,
    authApiService,
    tokenVerifier,
    workspaceService,
    goalService,
    milestoneService,
    actionService,
    planningService,
    progressService,
    measurementService,
    aiService,
    repositories: {
      workspace: workspaceRepo,
      goal: goalRepo,
      milestone: milestoneRepo,
      action: actionRepo,
      planning: planningRepo
    }
  };
}

import type { GoalId, Milestone, MilestoneId, MilestoneReference } from "./milestone.types";
import type { DomainError } from "./milestone.errors";

/** Port only: implementations belong to the owning modules/adapters. */
export interface GoalReader {
  getGoal(goalId: GoalId): Promise<{ id: GoalId; accessible: boolean; valid: boolean } | null>;
}
export interface MilestoneReader {
  getMilestone(id: MilestoneId): Promise<MilestoneReference | null>;
  getMilestonesByGoal(goalId: GoalId): Promise<readonly Milestone[]>;
}
export interface MilestoneActionAssociationPolicy {
  canAssociateAction(milestone: MilestoneReference): Promise<boolean>;
}
export interface MilestoneFacts {
  readonly id: MilestoneId;
  readonly goalId: GoalId;
  readonly title: string;
  readonly status: string;
  readonly targetDate: string | null;
  readonly completedAt: string | null;
}
export function toMilestoneFacts(value: Milestone): MilestoneFacts {
  return { id: value.id, goalId: value.goalId, title: value.title, status: value.status,
    targetDate: value.targetDate, completedAt: value.completedAt };
}
export type MilestoneValidationResult = { valid: true } | { valid: false; error: DomainError };

export type MilestoneErrorCode =
  | "MILESTONE_NOT_FOUND"
  | "MILESTONE_ACCESS_DENIED"
  | "MILESTONE_INVALID_ID"
  | "MILESTONE_INVALID_TITLE"
  | "MILESTONE_INVALID_TARGET_DATE"
  | "MILESTONE_GOAL_REQUIRED"
  | "MILESTONE_GOAL_NOT_FOUND"
  | "MILESTONE_GOAL_INVALID"
  | "MILESTONE_OWNERSHIP_INVALID"
  | "MILESTONE_STATUS_INVALID"
  | "MILESTONE_STATUS_TRANSITION_INVALID"
  | "MILESTONE_COMPLETION_BLOCKED"
  | "MILESTONE_REORDER_INVALID"
  | "MILESTONE_POSITION_CONFLICT"
  | "MILESTONE_VALIDATION_FAILED";

export interface DomainError {
  readonly code: MilestoneErrorCode;
  readonly message: string;
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\domain\C04\milestone.types.ts
============================================================
export type MilestoneId = string & { readonly __brand: "MilestoneId" };
export type GoalId = string & { readonly __brand: "GoalId" };

export interface Milestone {
  readonly id: MilestoneId;
  readonly goalId: GoalId;
  readonly title: string;
  readonly description: string | null;
  readonly targetDate: string | null; // Calendar date, not a timestamp or schedule.
  readonly status: string; // Deliberately open until the official enum is approved.
  readonly position: number;
  readonly completedAt: string | null;
  readonly createdAt: string;
  readonly updatedAt: string;
}

export interface MilestoneReference {
  readonly id: MilestoneId;
  readonly goalId: GoalId;
  readonly status: string;
}

export interface GoalReference {
  readonly id: GoalId;
  readonly accessible: boolean;
  readonly valid: boolean;
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\domain\C04\milestone.validation.ts
============================================================
import type { GoalReference, Milestone } from "./milestone.types";
import type { DomainError } from "./milestone.errors";

export function validateMilestoneTitle(title: unknown): DomainError | null {
  return typeof title === "string" && title.trim().length > 0
    ? null
    : { code: "MILESTONE_INVALID_TITLE", message: "Milestone title must not be blank." };
}

/** Accepts YYYY-MM-DD only; targetDate is a calendar date, not an instant. */
export function validateTargetDate(value: unknown): DomainError | null {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") return null;
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)) {
    return { code: "MILESTONE_INVALID_TARGET_DATE", message: "Target date must use YYYY-MM-DD." };
  }
  const [year, month, day] = value.split("-").map(Number);
  const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
  const valid = date.getUTCFullYear() === year && date.getUTCMonth() === month - 1 && date.getUTCDate() === day;
  return valid ? null : { code: "MILESTONE_INVALID_TARGET_DATE", message: "Target date is not a valid calendar date." };
}

export function validateGoalReference(goal: GoalReference | null): DomainError | null {
  if (!goal) return { code: "MILESTONE_GOAL_NOT_FOUND", message: "The referenced Goal does not exist." };
  if (!goal.accessible) return { code: "MILESTONE_ACCESS_DENIED", message: "The Goal is not accessible." };
  if (!goal.valid) return { code: "MILESTONE_GOAL_INVALID", message: "The Goal cannot own this Milestone." };
  return null;
}

export function validateMilestonePosition(position: unknown): DomainError | null {
  return typeof position === "number" && Number.isInteger(position) && position >= 0
    ? null
    : { code: "MILESTONE_REORDER_INVALID", message: "Position must be a non-negative integer." };
}

export function validateMilestoneInput(input: {
  title: unknown;
  targetDate?: unknown;
  goal?: GoalReference | null;
}): DomainError | null {
  const titleError = validateMilestoneTitle(input.title);
  if (titleError) return titleError;
  const dateError = validateTargetDate(input.targetDate);
  if (dateError) return dateError;
  if (input.goal !== undefined) return validateGoalReference(input.goal);
  return null;
}

export function validateReorder(milestones: readonly Milestone[], goalId: string, orderedIds: readonly string[]): DomainError | null {
  const current = milestones.filter(item => item.goalId === goalId);
  const expected = new Set(current.map(item => item.id));
  const supplied = new Set(orderedIds);
  if (expected.size !== supplied.size || orderedIds.length !== supplied.size ||
      [...expected].some(id => !supplied.has(id))) {
    return { code: "MILESTONE_REORDER_INVALID", message: "Reorder must contain each Milestone in the same Goal exactly once." };
  }
  return null;
}

  readonly description: string | null;

export interface ExecutionData {

FILE: backend\src\domain\C05\action.validation.ts
============================================================
import type { Action, ExecutionData } from "./action.types";
import type { DomainError } from "./action.errors";

export function validateActionTitle(title: unknown): DomainError | null {
  return typeof title === "string" && title.trim().length > 0
    ? null
    : { code: "ACTION_INVALID_TITLE", message: "Action title must not be blank." };
}

export function validateActionDescription(description: unknown): DomainError | null {
  return description === null || description === undefined || typeof description === "string"
    ? null
    : { code: "ACTION_INVALID_DESCRIPTION", message: "Description must be a string or null." };
}

export interface AssociationFacts {
  readonly directGoalId: string | null;
  readonly milestoneGoalId: string | null;
  readonly goalAccessible: boolean;
  readonly goalValid: boolean;
  readonly milestoneAccessible: boolean;
  readonly milestoneValid: boolean;
}

/**
 * Validates only unambiguous facts. Whether a direct Goal is mandatory, and
 * whether both associations may coexist, remains a product decision.
 */
export function validateActionAssociations(facts: AssociationFacts): DomainError | null {
  if (!facts.goalAccessible || !facts.milestoneAccessible) {
    return { code: "ACTION_ACCESS_DENIED", message: "An associated resource is not accessible." };
  }
  if (!facts.goalValid || !facts.milestoneValid) {
    return { code: "ACTION_ASSOCIATION_INVALID", message: "An associated resource is not valid." };
  }
  if (facts.directGoalId && facts.milestoneGoalId && facts.directGoalId !== facts.milestoneGoalId) {
    return { code: "ACTION_ASSOCIATION_CONFLICT", message: "Direct Goal and Milestone-derived Goal do not match." };
  }
  return null;
}

function parseInstant(value: string): number | null {
  const time = Date.parse(value);
  return Number.isFinite(time) ? time : null;
}

/** Checks chronology only; does not infer actual duration from Schedule. */
export function validateExecutionData(data: ExecutionData): DomainError | null {
  const start = data.actualStartedAt === null ? null : parseInstant(data.actualStartedAt);
  const end = data.actualCompletedAt === null ? null : parseInstant(data.actualCompletedAt);

  if (data.actualStartedAt !== null && start === null) {
    return { code: "ACTION_EXECUTION_START_INVALID", message: "Actual start must be a valid timestamp." };
  }
  if (data.actualCompletedAt !== null && end === null) {
    return { code: "ACTION_EXECUTION_COMPLETION_INVALID", message: "Actual completion must be a valid timestamp." };
  }
  if (start !== null && end !== null && end < start) {
    return { code: "ACTION_EXECUTION_TIME_CONFLICT", message: "Actual completion cannot precede actual start." };
  }
  // Duration semantics are OPEN; only reject negative values when supplied.
  if (data.actualDurationMinutes !== null &&
      (!Number.isFinite(data.actualDurationMinutes) || data.actualDurationMinutes < 0)) {
    return { code: "ACTION_EXECUTION_DURATION_INVALID", message: "Actual duration cannot be negative or non-finite." };
  }
  if (data.executionNote !== null && typeof data.executionNote !== "string") {
    return { code: "ACTION_EXECUTION_INVALID", message: "Execution note must be a string or null." };
  }
  return null;
}

export function validateActionUpdate(original: Action, update: Partial<Pick<Action, "title" | "description">>): DomainError | null {
  if (update.title !== undefined) {
    const error = validateActionTitle(update.title);
    if (error) return error;
  }
  if (update.description !== undefined) {
    const error = validateActionDescription(update.description);
    if (error) return error;
  }
  // Identity and associations are intentionally not mutable through this basic update validator.
  void original;
  return null;
}
