FILE: backend\src\domain\C06\measurement.validation.ts
============================================================
import {
  Measurement,
  MeasurementGoalFacts,
  MeasurementRecord,
  MeasurementRecordFacts,
} from "./measurement.types";
import { MeasurementError, MeasurementErrorCode, measurementError } from "./measurement.errors";

export type ValidationResult =
  | { valid: true }
  | { valid: false; error: MeasurementError };

const ok: ValidationResult = { valid: true };

function fail(code: MeasurementErrorCode, message: string, field?: string): ValidationResult {
  return { valid: false, error: measurementError(code, message, field) };
}

function validTimestamp(value: string): boolean {
  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0 && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value));
}

export function validateMeasurementName(name: unknown): ValidationResult {
  if (typeof name !== "string" || name.trim().length === 0) {
    return fail("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_NAME", "Measurement name must not be blank.", "name");
  }
  return ok;
}

export function validateMeasurementMetadata(input: {
  description?: unknown;
  purpose?: unknown;
}): ValidationResult {
  if (input.description != null && typeof input.description !== "string") {
    return fail("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_DESCRIPTION", "Description must be a string.", "description");
  }
  if (input.purpose != null && typeof input.purpose !== "string") {
    return fail("MEASUREMENT_VALIDATION_FAILED", "Purpose must be a string.", "purpose");
  }
  return ok;
}

export function validateMeasurementGoal(
  goalId: unknown,
  facts: MeasurementGoalFacts,
): ValidationResult {
  if (typeof goalId !== "string" || goalId.trim() === "") {
    return fail("MEASUREMENT_GOAL_REQUIRED", "A Goal reference is required.", "goalId");
  }
  if (!facts.exists) return fail("MEASUREMENT_GOAL_NOT_FOUND", "The referenced Goal does not exist.", "goalId");
  if (!facts.accessible) return fail("MEASUREMENT_ACCESS_DENIED", "The Goal is not accessible.");
  if (!facts.validForMeasurement) return fail("MEASUREMENT_GOAL_INVALID", "The Goal cannot own this Measurement.");
  return ok;
}

export function validateMeasurementDefinition(
  measurement: Pick<Measurement, "id" | "goalId" | "name" | "createdAt" | "updatedAt"> &
    Partial<Pick<Measurement, "description" | "purpose">>,
  goalFacts: MeasurementGoalFacts,
): ValidationResult {
  if (typeof measurement.id !== "string" || !measurement.id.trim()) {
    return fail("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_ID", "Measurement ID is required.", "id");
  }
  const goal = validateMeasurementGoal(measurement.goalId, goalFacts);
  if (!goal.valid) return goal;
  const name = validateMeasurementName(measurement.name);
  if (!name.valid) return name;
  const metadata = validateMeasurementMetadata(measurement);
  if (!metadata.valid) return metadata;
  if (!validTimestamp(measurement.createdAt) || !validTimestamp(measurement.updatedAt)) {
    return fail("MEASUREMENT_TIMESTAMP_INVALID", "Creation and update timestamps must be valid.", "createdAt/updatedAt");
  }
  return ok;
}

/**
 * Value validation is delegated to an approved type policy. No permanent type
 * system is assumed while the specification keeps value types OPEN.
 */
export type ApprovedValueValidator = (valueType: string, value: unknown) => boolean;

export function validateMeasurementValue(
  valueType: string | null | undefined,
  value: unknown,
  approvedValidator?: ApprovedValueValidator,
): ValidationResult {
  if (!valueType) return fail("MEASUREMENT_VALUE_TYPE_REQUIRED", "An approved value type is required.", "valueType");
  if (!approvedValidator) {
    return fail("MEASUREMENT_VALUE_TYPE_UNSUPPORTED", "No approved value-type validator is configured.", "valueType");
  }
  if (!approvedValidator(valueType, value)) {
    return fail("MEASUREMENT_VALUE_INVALID", "Value does not match the configured value-type policy.", "value");
  }
  return ok;
}

export function validateMeasurementRecord(
  record: MeasurementRecord,
  facts: MeasurementRecordFacts,
): ValidationResult {
  if (!record.id || !record.measurementId) {
    return fail("MEASUREMENT_RECORD_INVALID", "Record and Measurement IDs are required.");
  }
  if (!facts.measurementExists) {
    return fail("MEASUREMENT_NOT_FOUND", "The owning Measurement does not exist.", "measurementId");
  }
  if (!facts.measurementAccessible) {
    return fail("MEASUREMENT_ACCESS_DENIED", "The owning Measurement is not accessible.");
  }
  if (facts.measurementId !== record.measurementId) {
    return fail("MEASUREMENT_RECORD_OWNERSHIP_INVALID", "Record references a different Measurement.", "measurementId");
  }
  if (!validTimestamp(record.recordedAt) || !validTimestamp(record.createdAt)) {
    return fail("MEASUREMENT_TIMESTAMP_INVALID", "Recorded and created timestamps must be valid.");
  }
  return ok;
}

/** No latest-value selection is implemented: ordering semantics remain OPEN. */
export function compareRecordedAt(
  _a: MeasurementRecord,
  _b: MeasurementRecord,
): never {
  throw new Error("Current-value and history ordering policy is OPEN for C06.");
}

FILE: backend\src\domain\C07\planning.validation.ts
============================================================
import {
  DailyPlan, DailyPlanUserFacts, Schedule, ScheduleActionFacts, ScheduleDailyPlanFacts,
} from "./planning.types";
import { PlanningError, PlanningErrorCode, planningError } from "./planning.errors";

export type ValidationResult =
  | { valid: true }
  | { valid: false; error: PlanningError };

const ok: ValidationResult = { valid: true };
function fail(code: PlanningErrorCode, message: string, field?: string): ValidationResult {
  return { valid: false, error: planningError(code, message, field) };
}

export function isCalendarDate(value: unknown): value is string {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)) {
    return false;
  }

  const [year, month, day] = value.split("-").map(Number);
  const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));

  return (
    date.getUTCFullYear() === year &&
    date.getUTCMonth() === month - 1 &&
    date.getUTCDate() === day
  );
}

function isValidInstant(value: unknown): value is string {
  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() !== "" && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value));
}

export function validateDailyPlan(plan: DailyPlan, facts: DailyPlanUserFacts): ValidationResult {
  if (!plan.id) return fail("DAILY_PLAN_INVALID_ID", "Daily Plan ID is required.", "id");
  if (!plan.userId) return fail("DAILY_PLAN_USER_REQUIRED", "User ID is required.", "userId");
  if (!facts.userExists) return fail("DAILY_PLAN_OWNER_INVALID", "Owning User does not exist.", "userId");
  if (!facts.accessible) return fail("DAILY_PLAN_ACCESS_DENIED", "User context is not accessible.");
  if (!isCalendarDate(plan.date)) return fail("DAILY_PLAN_DATE_INVALID", "Date must be a real YYYY-MM-DD calendar date.", "date");
  if (!isValidInstant(plan.createdAt) || !isValidInstant(plan.updatedAt)) {
    return fail("PLANNING_VALIDATION_FAILED", "Daily Plan timestamps must be valid.");
  }
  return ok;
}

export function validateScheduleAction(actionId: unknown, facts: ScheduleActionFacts): ValidationResult {
  if (typeof actionId !== "string" || !actionId.trim()) {
    return fail("SCHEDULE_ACTION_REQUIRED", "An Action reference is required.", "actionId");
  }
  if (!facts.actionExists) return fail("SCHEDULE_ACTION_NOT_FOUND", "Referenced Action does not exist.", "actionId");
  if (!facts.accessible) return fail("SCHEDULE_ACCESS_DENIED", "Referenced Action is not accessible.");
  return ok;
}

export function validateScheduleDailyPlan(
  dailyPlanId: string | null | undefined,
  facts?: ScheduleDailyPlanFacts,
): ValidationResult {
  // Daily Plan association is OPEN/optional; absence is not rejected here.
  if (dailyPlanId == null) return ok;
  if (!facts || !facts.exists) return fail("SCHEDULE_DAILY_PLAN_NOT_FOUND", "Referenced Daily Plan does not exist.");
  if (!facts.belongsToUser) return fail("SCHEDULE_DAILY_PLAN_INVALID", "Daily Plan does not belong to the scheduling User.");
  return ok;
}

export function validateScheduleTimeRange(startAt: unknown, endAt: unknown): ValidationResult {
  if (!isValidInstant(startAt)) return fail("SCHEDULE_START_INVALID", "Start must be a valid timestamp.", "startAt");
  if (!isValidInstant(endAt)) return fail("SCHEDULE_END_INVALID", "End must be a valid timestamp.", "endAt");
  if (Date.parse(endAt) <= Date.parse(startAt)) {
    return fail("SCHEDULE_TIME_RANGE_INVALID", "End must be after start.", "endAt");
  }
  return ok;
}

export function validateSchedule(
  schedule: Schedule,
  actionFacts: ScheduleActionFacts,
  dailyPlanFacts?: ScheduleDailyPlanFacts,
): ValidationResult {
  if (!schedule.id) return fail("SCHEDULE_INVALID_ID", "Schedule ID is required.", "id");
  const action = validateScheduleAction(schedule.actionId, actionFacts);
  if (!action.valid) return action;
  const plan = validateScheduleDailyPlan(schedule.dailyPlanId, dailyPlanFacts);
  if (!plan.valid) return plan;
  const range = validateScheduleTimeRange(schedule.startAt, schedule.endAt);
  if (!range.valid) return range;
  if (!isValidInstant(schedule.createdAt) || !isValidInstant(schedule.updatedAt)) {
    return fail("PLANNING_VALIDATION_FAILED", "Schedule timestamps must be valid.");
  }
  return ok;
}

/** Reports overlaps only. It deliberately does not decide warning vs rejection. */
export function detectScheduleOverlap(a: Pick<Schedule, "startAt" | "endAt">, b: Pick<Schedule, "startAt" | "endAt">): boolean {
  if (!validateScheduleTimeRange(a.startAt, a.endAt).valid ||
      !validateScheduleTimeRange(b.startAt, b.endAt).valid) return false;
  return Date.parse(a.startAt) < Date.parse(b.endAt) &&
    Date.parse(b.startAt) < Date.parse(a.endAt);
}

export function validateApprovedPlanningProposal(
  proposal: { approved: boolean; schedules: readonly unknown[] },
): ValidationResult {
  if (!proposal || !Array.isArray(proposal.schedules) || proposal.schedules.length === 0) {
    return fail("PLANNING_PROPOSAL_INVALID", "Proposal must contain at least one Schedule item.");
  }
  if (proposal.approved !== true) {
    return fail("PLANNING_PROPOSAL_NOT_APPROVED", "Proposal must be approved by the User before application.");
  }
  return ok;
}

FILE: backend\src\domain\C08\progress.validation.ts
============================================================
import {
  ProgressCalculationResult, ProgressSourceFacts, ProgressSourceReference,
} from "./progress.types";
import { ProgressError, ProgressErrorCode, progressError } from "./progress.errors";

export type ValidationResult =
  | { valid: true }
  | { valid: false; error: ProgressError };

const ok: ValidationResult = { valid: true };

function fail(code: ProgressErrorCode, message: string, source?: string): ValidationResult {
  return { valid: false, error: progressError(code, message, source) };
}

function validTimestamp(value: unknown): boolean {
  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() !== "" && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value));
}

/** Preserve all four availability meanings; missing is never coerced to zero. */
export function resolveProgressAvailability(facts: ProgressSourceFacts): ProgressCalculationResult["availability"] {
  if (!facts.applicable) return "NOT_APPLICABLE";
  if (!facts.available) return "INSUFFICIENT_DATA";
  if (!facts.valid) return "INVALID_SOURCE";
  return "AVAILABLE";
}

export function validateSourceReferences(
  references: readonly ProgressSourceReference[],
): ValidationResult {
  for (const ref of references) {
    if (!ref.module?.trim() || !ref.resourceType?.trim() || !ref.resourceId?.trim()) {
      return fail("PROGRESS_SOURCE_INVALID", "Every source reference requires module, resource type and resource ID.");
    }
    if (ref.sourceTimestamp != null && !validTimestamp(ref.sourceTimestamp)) {
      return fail("PROGRESS_SOURCE_INVALID", "Source timestamp is invalid.", ref.resourceId);
    }
  }
  return ok;
}

export function validateProgressResult(result: ProgressCalculationResult): ValidationResult {
  if (!result.scopeType?.trim() || !result.scopeId?.trim() || !result.metricType?.trim()) {
    return fail("PROGRESS_SCOPE_INVALID", "Scope type, scope ID and metric type are required.");
  }
  if (!validTimestamp(result.calculatedAt)) {
    return fail("PROGRESS_VALIDATION_FAILED", "Calculation timestamp is invalid.");
  }
  if (result.availability === "AVAILABLE") {
    if (typeof result.value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(result.value)) {
      return fail("PROGRESS_RESULT_UNAVAILABLE", "An AVAILABLE result requires a finite numeric value.");
    }
  } else if (result.value !== undefined && result.value !== null) {
    return fail("PROGRESS_VALIDATION_FAILED", "Unavailable results must not contain a fabricated numeric value.");
  }
  return validateSourceReferences(result.sourceReferences);
}

/**
 * No Progress formula is implemented here. Formula, weighting, eligibility,
 * normalization, precision, boundaries and aggregation remain Product Owner decisions.
 */
export function requireApprovedCalculationRule(
  approved: boolean,
): ValidationResult {
  return approved
    ? ok
    : fail("PROGRESS_RULE_UNAVAILABLE", "No approved calculation rule is available.");
}

/** Guard against non-finite source numbers without deciding formula semantics. */
export function validateFiniteSourceNumber(value: unknown, source?: string): ValidationResult {
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)
    ? ok
    : fail("PROGRESS_SOURCE_INVALID", "Numeric source value must be finite.", source);
}

FILE: backend\src\domain\C09\review.validation.ts
============================================================
import {
  Review, ReviewPeriod, ReviewScopeFacts, ReviewSourceStatus, ReviewUserFacts,
} from "./review.types";
import { ReviewError, ReviewErrorCode, reviewError } from "./review.errors";

export type ValidationResult =
  | { valid: true }
  | { valid: false; error: ReviewError };

const ok: ValidationResult = { valid: true };
function fail(code: ReviewErrorCode, message: string, field?: string): ValidationResult {
  return { valid: false, error: reviewError(code, message, field) };
}

function validTimestamp(value: unknown): boolean {
  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() !== "" && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value));
}

export function validateReviewPeriod(period: ReviewPeriod): ValidationResult {
  if (!period || !validTimestamp(period.start) || !validTimestamp(period.end)) {
    return fail("REVIEW_PERIOD_INVALID", "Review period start and end must be valid timestamps.");
  }
  if (Date.parse(period.end) < Date.parse(period.start)) {
    return fail("REVIEW_PERIOD_RANGE_INVALID", "Review period end must not be before start.");
  }
  return ok;
}

export function validateReviewScope(
  scope: Review["scope"],
  facts: ReviewScopeFacts,
): ValidationResult {
  if (!scope || !scope.type?.trim()) return fail("REVIEW_SCOPE_REQUIRED", "Review scope is required.");
  if (!scope.id?.trim()) return fail("REVIEW_SCOPE_INVALID", "A scope ID is required for this scope.", "scope.id");
  if (!facts.exists) return fail("REVIEW_SCOPE_NOT_FOUND", "Referenced scope does not exist.");
  if (!facts.accessible) return fail("REVIEW_SCOPE_ACCESS_DENIED", "Referenced scope is not accessible.");
  if (!facts.validForReview) return fail("REVIEW_SCOPE_INVALID", "Referenced scope is not valid for Review.");
  return ok;
}

export function validateReviewOwnership(userId: unknown, facts: ReviewUserFacts): ValidationResult {
  if (typeof userId !== "string" || !userId.trim()) {
    return fail("REVIEW_OWNER_REQUIRED", "Review owner is required.", "userId");
  }
  if (!facts.userExists) return fail("REVIEW_OWNERSHIP_INVALID", "Review owner does not exist.");
  if (!facts.accessible) return fail("REVIEW_ACCESS_DENIED", "Review owner context is not accessible.");
  return ok;
}

export function validateReview(review: Review, userFacts: ReviewUserFacts, scopeFacts: ReviewScopeFacts): ValidationResult {
  if (!review.id) return fail("REVIEW_INVALID_ID", "Review ID is required.", "id");
  const owner = validateReviewOwnership(review.userId, userFacts);
  if (!owner.valid) return owner;
  const scope = validateReviewScope(review.scope, scopeFacts);
  if (!scope.valid) return scope;
  const period = validateReviewPeriod(review.period);
  if (!period.valid) return period;
  if (!validTimestamp(review.createdAt) || !validTimestamp(review.updatedAt)) {
    return fail("REVIEW_VALIDATION_FAILED", "Review timestamps must be valid.");
  }
  return ok;
}

/** Preserve source availability categories; no absent source is converted to zero. */
export function validateReviewSourceStatuses(statuses: readonly ReviewSourceStatus[]): ValidationResult {
  for (const status of statuses) {
    if (!status.source?.trim()) return fail("REVIEW_SOURCE_INVALID", "Source identifier is required.");
    if (!["AVAILABLE", "PARTIALLY_AVAILABLE", "MISSING", "NOT_APPLICABLE", "INVALID"].includes(status.availability)) {
      return fail("REVIEW_SOURCE_INVALID", "Unknown source availability state.", status.source);
    }
  }
  return ok;
}

export function validateReviewNote(note: {
  id: string; reviewId: string; content: string; createdAt: string; updatedAt: string;
}, owningReviewId: string): ValidationResult {
  if (!note.id || !note.reviewId || typeof note.content !== "string" || !note.content.trim()) {
    return fail("REVIEW_NOTE_INVALID", "Note ID, Review ID and non-empty content are required.");
  }
  if (note.reviewId !== owningReviewId) {
    return fail("REVIEW_NOTE_ACCESS_DENIED", "Note does not belong to the resolved Review.");
  }
  if (!validTimestamp(note.createdAt) || !validTimestamp(note.updatedAt)) {
    return fail("REVIEW_NOTE_INVALID", "Note timestamps must be valid.");
  }
  return ok;
}

/** C09 consumes authoritative Progress; it must not calculate or approximate it. */
export function requireAuthoritativeProgressSource(sourceModule: string): ValidationResult {
  return sourceModule === "C08"
    ? ok
    : fail("REVIEW_PROGRESS_SOURCE_INVALID", "Progress shown in Review must come from C08.");
}

export const C03_ERROR_CODES = [
  "GOAL_NOT_FOUND",
  "GOAL_INVALID_ID",
  "GOAL_INVALID_TITLE",
  "GOAL_INVALID_DESCRIPTION",
  "GOAL_INVALID_TARGET_DATE",
  "GOAL_SCOPE_INVALID",
  "GOAL_SCOPE_ACCESS_DENIED",
  "GOAL_PARENT_NOT_FOUND",
  "GOAL_PARENT_INVALID",
  "GOAL_SELF_PARENTING",
  "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE",
  "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID",
  "GOAL_OWNERSHIP_INVALID",
  "GOAL_SCOPE_POLICY_UNRESOLVED",
  "GOAL_SCOPE_MOVEMENT_INVALID",
  "GOAL_SCOPE_MOVEMENT_BLOCKED",
  "GOAL_DEPENDENCY_CONFLICT",
] as const;

export type C03ErrorCode = (typeof C03_ERROR_CODES)[number];

export class C03DomainError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly code: C03ErrorCode,
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "C03DomainError";
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\domain\goal-hierarchy\goal.hierarchy.ts
============================================================
import type {
  C03ValidationResult,
  Goal,
  GoalId,
  GoalReader,
} from "./goal.types";

export type HierarchyError =
  | "GOAL_NOT_FOUND"
  | "GOAL_PARENT_NOT_FOUND"
  | "GOAL_SELF_PARENTING"
  | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE"
  | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID"
  | "GOAL_SCOPE_POLICY_UNRESOLVED";

function sameWorkspace(parent: Goal, child: Goal): boolean {
  return parent.workspaceId === child.workspaceId;
}

/**
 * Enforces confirmed Workspace boundary. Different Project scopes within the
 * same Workspace remain blocked because GOAL-006 is OPEN.
 */
export function validateParentRelationship(
  childGoalId: GoalId,
  proposedParentGoalId: GoalId | null,
  reader: GoalReader,
): C03ValidationResult<HierarchyError> {
  const child = reader.getGoal(childGoalId);
  if (!child) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_NOT_FOUND" };
  if (proposedParentGoalId === null) return { valid: true };
  if (proposedParentGoalId === childGoalId) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_SELF_PARENTING" };
  }

  const parent = reader.getGoal(proposedParentGoalId);
  if (!parent) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_PARENT_NOT_FOUND" };
  if (!sameWorkspace(parent, child)) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID" };
  }
  if (parent.projectId !== child.projectId) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_SCOPE_POLICY_UNRESOLVED" };
  }

  const visited = new Set<GoalId>([childGoalId]);
  let cursor: Goal | null = parent;

  while (cursor) {
    if (visited.has(cursor.id)) {
      return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE" };
    }
    visited.add(cursor.id);
    if (cursor.parentGoalId === null) break;
    cursor = reader.getGoal(cursor.parentGoalId);
    if (!cursor) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID" };
  }

  return { valid: true };
}

export function getGoalAncestors(
  goalId: GoalId,
  reader: GoalReader,
): C03ValidationResult<"GOAL_NOT_FOUND" | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID" | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE"> & {
  readonly goals?: readonly Goal[];
} {
  const start = reader.getGoal(goalId);
  if (!start) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_NOT_FOUND" };

  const ancestors: Goal[] = [];
  const visited = new Set<GoalId>([goalId]);
  let parentId = start.parentGoalId;

  while (parentId !== null) {
    if (visited.has(parentId)) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE" };
    visited.add(parentId);
    const parent = reader.getGoal(parentId);
    if (!parent) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID" };
    ancestors.push(parent);
    parentId = parent.parentGoalId;
  }

  return { valid: true, goals: ancestors };
}

export function getGoalDescendants(
  goalId: GoalId,
  reader: GoalReader,
): C03ValidationResult<"GOAL_NOT_FOUND" | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID" | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE"> & {
  readonly goals?: readonly Goal[];
} {
  const root = reader.getGoal(goalId);
  if (!root) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_NOT_FOUND" };

  const all = reader.getGoals();
  const childrenByParent = new Map<GoalId, Goal[]>();
  for (const goal of all) {
    if (goal.parentGoalId === null) continue;
    const list = childrenByParent.get(goal.parentGoalId) ?? [];
    list.push(goal);
    childrenByParent.set(goal.parentGoalId, list);
  }

  const descendants: Goal[] = [];
  const visited = new Set<GoalId>([goalId]);
  const stack = [...(childrenByParent.get(goalId) ?? [])].reverse();

  while (stack.length > 0) {
    const current = stack.pop()!;
    if (visited.has(current.id)) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE" };
    visited.add(current.id);
    descendants.push(current);
    const children = childrenByParent.get(current.id) ?? [];
    for (let i = children.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) stack.push(children[i]);
  }

  // A dangling parent reference is invalid when encountered in the traversed tree.
  for (const goal of descendants) {
    if (goal.parentGoalId && !reader.getGoal(goal.parentGoalId)) {
      return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID" };
    }
  }
  return { valid: true, goals: descendants };
}

export interface GoalTreeNode {
  readonly goal: Goal;
  readonly children: readonly GoalTreeNode[];
}

export function buildGoalTree(
  rootGoalId: GoalId,
  reader: GoalReader,
): C03ValidationResult<"GOAL_NOT_FOUND" | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID" | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE"> & {
  readonly tree?: GoalTreeNode;
} {
  const root = reader.getGoal(rootGoalId);
  if (!root) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_NOT_FOUND" };

  const all = reader.getGoals();
  const childrenByParent = new Map<GoalId, Goal[]>();
  for (const goal of all) {
    if (goal.parentGoalId === null) continue;
    const children = childrenByParent.get(goal.parentGoalId) ?? [];
    children.push(goal);
    childrenByParent.set(goal.parentGoalId, children);
  }

  const visiting = new Set<GoalId>();
  const build = (goal: Goal): GoalTreeNode | null => {
    if (visiting.has(goal.id)) return null;
    visiting.add(goal.id);
    const children: GoalTreeNode[] = [];
    for (const child of childrenByParent.get(goal.id) ?? []) {
      const node = build(child);
      if (!node) return null;
      children.push(node);
    }
    visiting.delete(goal.id);
    return { goal, children };
  };

  const tree = build(root);
  if (!tree) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE" };
  return { valid: true, tree };
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\domain\goal-hierarchy\goal.movement.ts
============================================================
import type {
  Goal,
  GoalId,
  GoalOwnershipValidator,
  GoalReader,
  GoalScope,
  GoalScopeValidator,
  UserId,
  C03ValidationResult,
} from "./goal.types";
import { validateGoalScope } from "./goal.validation";
import { validateParentRelationship } from "./goal.hierarchy";

export interface GoalScopeMovementRequest {
  readonly callerUserId: UserId;
  readonly goalId: GoalId;
  readonly destinationScope: GoalScope;
  readonly proposedParentGoalId?: GoalId | null;
}

export type GoalScopeMovementError =
  | "GOAL_NOT_FOUND"
  | "GOAL_OWNERSHIP_INVALID"
  | "GOAL_SCOPE_INVALID"
  | "GOAL_SCOPE_ACCESS_DENIED"
  | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID"
  | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE"
  | "GOAL_SELF_PARENTING"
  | "GOAL_PARENT_NOT_FOUND"
  | "GOAL_SCOPE_POLICY_UNRESOLVED"
  | "GOAL_SCOPE_MOVEMENT_BLOCKED"
  | "GOAL_DEPENDENCY_CONFLICT";

export interface GoalMovementDependencyReader {
  hasBlockingDependencies(goalId: GoalId): boolean;
}

/**
 * Validation contract only. This function never mutates Goal data and does not
 * claim transaction/atomicity guarantees. The Application Layer/D01 must own
 * the eventual movement transaction or recovery strategy.
 */
export function validateGoalScopeMovement(
  request: GoalScopeMovementRequest,
  goals: GoalReader,
  ownership: GoalOwnershipValidator,
  scopes: GoalScopeValidator,
  dependencies: GoalMovementDependencyReader,
): C03ValidationResult<GoalScopeMovementError> {
  const goal: Goal | null = goals.getGoal(request.goalId);
  if (!goal) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_NOT_FOUND" };

  const ownerResult = ownership.validateGoalOwnership(request.callerUserId, request.goalId);
  if (!ownerResult.valid) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_OWNERSHIP_INVALID" };

  const destination = validateGoalScope(request.callerUserId, request.destinationScope, scopes);
  if (!destination.valid) return destination;

  if (request.proposedParentGoalId !== undefined) {
    // Validate hierarchy against the proposed destination scope, not the current scope.
    const destinationAwareReader: GoalReader = {
      getGoal: (id) => {
        const found = goals.getGoal(id);
        if (!found || found.id !== request.goalId) return found;
        return {
          ...found,
          workspaceId: request.destinationScope.workspaceId,
          projectId: request.destinationScope.projectId,
        };
      },
      getGoals: () => goals.getGoals().map((found) => found.id === request.goalId
        ? { ...found, workspaceId: request.destinationScope.workspaceId, projectId: request.destinationScope.projectId }
        : found),
    };
    const hierarchy = validateParentRelationship(
      request.goalId,
      request.proposedParentGoalId,
      destinationAwareReader,
    );
    if (!hierarchy.valid) return hierarchy;
  }

  if (dependencies.hasBlockingDependencies(request.goalId)) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_DEPENDENCY_CONFLICT" };
  }

  return { valid: true };
}

/**
 * C03 — Goal & Hierarchy domain contracts.
 * Identifier formats remain opaque until D03 standardizes shared identifiers.
 */
export type GoalId = string;
export type UserId = string;
export type WorkspaceId = string;
export type ProjectId = string;

export type GoalStatus = string;
/**
 * The specification has not approved GoalStatus values. This alias deliberately
 * does not publish an enum or imply lifecycle transitions.
 */

export interface GoalScope {
  readonly workspaceId: WorkspaceId;
  /** null = independent Workspace Goal; non-null = Project-owned Goal. */
  readonly projectId: ProjectId | null;
}

export interface Goal {
  readonly id: GoalId;
  readonly workspaceId: WorkspaceId;
  readonly projectId: ProjectId | null;
  readonly parentGoalId: GoalId | null;
  readonly title: string;
  readonly description: string | null;
  /** Calendar date (YYYY-MM-DD), not a timestamp or Action Schedule. */
  readonly targetDate: string | null;
  /** Status remains opaque until GOAL-001 is approved. */
  readonly status?: GoalStatus;
  readonly createdAt: string;
  readonly updatedAt: string;
}

export interface GoalReader {
  getGoal(id: GoalId): Goal | null;
  getGoals(): readonly Goal[];
}

export interface GoalScopeValidator {
  validate(
    callerUserId: UserId,
    scope: GoalScope,
  ): { readonly valid: true } | { readonly valid: false; readonly code: string };
}

export interface GoalOwnershipValidator {
  validateGoalOwnership(
    callerUserId: UserId,
    goalId: GoalId,
  ): { readonly valid: true } | { readonly valid: false; readonly code: string };
}

export type C03ValidationResult<TCode extends string> =
  | { readonly valid: true }
  | { readonly valid: false; readonly code: TCode };

FILE: backend\src\domain\goal-hierarchy\goal.validation.ts

import type {
  C03ValidationResult,
  Goal,
  GoalId,
  GoalScope,
  GoalScopeValidator,
} from "./goal.types";

export type GoalAttributeError =
  | "GOAL_INVALID_ID"
  | "GOAL_INVALID_TITLE"
  | "GOAL_INVALID_DESCRIPTION"
  | "GOAL_INVALID_TARGET_DATE";

const DATE_ONLY = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

function isRealCalendarDate(value: string): boolean {
  if (!DATE_ONLY.test(value)) return false;
  const [year, month, day] = value.split("-").map(Number);
  const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
  return date.getUTCFullYear() === year
    && date.getUTCMonth() === month - 1
    && date.getUTCDate() === day;
}

/** Only constraints explicitly supported by C03 are enforced. Length limits remain OPEN. */
export function validateGoalAttributes(input: {
  readonly id: GoalId;
  readonly title: string;
  readonly description?: string | null;
  readonly targetDate?: string | null;
}): C03ValidationResult<GoalAttributeError> {
  if (typeof input.id !== "string" || input.id.trim().length === 0) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_ID" };
  }
  if (typeof input.title !== "string" || input.title.trim().length === 0) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_TITLE" };
  }
  if (input.description !== undefined && input.description !== null
      && typeof input.description !== "string") {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_DESCRIPTION" };
  }
  if (input.targetDate !== undefined && input.targetDate !== null
      && !isRealCalendarDate(input.targetDate)) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_TARGET_DATE" };
  }
  return { valid: true };
}

export function toGoalScope(goal: Pick<Goal, "workspaceId" | "projectId">): GoalScope {
  return { workspaceId: goal.workspaceId, projectId: goal.projectId };
}

/** Delegates Workspace/Project truth to C02; C03 does not duplicate those rules. */
export function validateGoalScope(
  callerUserId: string,
  scope: GoalScope,
  validator: GoalScopeValidator,
): C03ValidationResult<"GOAL_SCOPE_INVALID" | "GOAL_SCOPE_ACCESS_DENIED"> {
  if (!scope || typeof scope.workspaceId !== "string" || scope.workspaceId.trim() === ""
      || (scope.projectId !== null && (typeof scope.projectId !== "string" || scope.projectId.trim() === ""))) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_SCOPE_INVALID" };
  }
  const result = validator.validate(callerUserId, scope);
  if (result.valid) return { valid: true };
  return {
    valid: false,
    code: result.code.includes("ACCESS_DENIED") ? "GOAL_SCOPE_ACCESS_DENIED" : "GOAL_SCOPE_INVALID",
  };
}

/**
 * C01 — Application identity contracts.
 * Provider-independent. UserId is deliberately not constrained to UUID:
 * the specification does not approve a concrete identifier format.
 */
declare const userIdBrand: unique symbol;

export type UserId = string & { readonly [userIdBrand]: "UserId" };

export type IdentityErrorCode =
  | "IDENTITY_REQUIRED"
  | "IDENTITY_INVALID"
  | "IDENTITY_NOT_FOUND"
  | "IDENTITY_INACTIVE";

export type IdentityValidationResult =
  | { valid: true; userId: UserId }
  | { valid: false; code: IdentityErrorCode };

export function parseUserId(value: unknown): IdentityValidationResult {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") {
    return { valid: false, code: "IDENTITY_REQUIRED" };
  }

  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.trim().length === 0 || value !== value.trim()) {
    return { valid: false, code: "IDENTITY_INVALID" };
  }

  return { valid: true, userId: value as UserId };
}

FILE: backend\src\domain\identity-ownership\ownership.policy.ts
============================================================
import { parseUserId } from "./identity.types";
import type {
  AccessDecision,
  OwnershipGraphReader,
  OwnershipOperation,
  ResourceReference,
} from "./ownership.types";
import { resolveResourceOwner } from "./ownership.resolver";

/**
 * C01 returns the ownership decision only. The Application Layer remains
 * responsible for enforcing it in request handling.
 */
export function checkResourceOwnershipAccess(
  callerUserId: unknown,
  resource: ResourceReference,
  _operation: OwnershipOperation,
  graph: OwnershipGraphReader,
): AccessDecision {
  const identity = parseUserId(callerUserId);
  if (!identity.valid) return { allowed: false, code: "RESOURCE_ACCESS_DENIED" };

  const resolution = resolveResourceOwner(resource, graph);
  if (resolution.status !== "RESOLVED") {
    return {
      allowed: false,
      code: resolution.status === "OWNERSHIP_INCONSISTENT"
        ? "OWNERSHIP_INCONSISTENT"
        : "RESOURCE_ACCESS_DENIED",
    };
  }

  if (resolution.ownerUserId !== identity.userId) {
    return { allowed: false, code: "RESOURCE_ACCESS_DENIED" };
  }

  return { allowed: true, ownerUserId: resolution.ownerUserId };
}

/** Ordinary updates cannot change ownership. No transfer workflow is provided. */
export function validateOwnerUnchanged(
  currentOwnerUserId: unknown,
  proposedOwnerUserId: unknown,
): { valid: true } | { valid: false; code: "OWNERSHIP_MODIFICATION_FORBIDDEN" | "OWNERSHIP_REQUIRED" } {
  const current = parseUserId(currentOwnerUserId);
  const proposed = parseUserId(proposedOwnerUserId);

  if (!current.valid || !proposed.valid) {
    return { valid: false, code: "OWNERSHIP_REQUIRED" };
  }

  return current.userId === proposed.userId
    ? { valid: true }
    : { valid: false, code: "OWNERSHIP_MODIFICATION_FORBIDDEN" };
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\domain\identity-ownership\ownership.resolver.ts
============================================================
import { parseUserId, type UserId } from "./identity.types";
import type {
  OwnershipGraphReader,
  OwnershipNode,
  OwnershipResolution,
  ResourceReference,
} from "./ownership.types";

/**
 * Resolves all authoritative ownership paths. No parent means unresolved
 * unless a direct owner is present. Cycles and conflicting owners fail closed.
 */
export function resolveResourceOwner(
  ref: ResourceReference,
  graph: OwnershipGraphReader,
): OwnershipResolution {
  const visiting = new Set<string>();

  const visit = (currentRef: ResourceReference): OwnershipResolution => {
    const key = `${currentRef.resourceType}:${currentRef.resourceId}`;
    if (visiting.has(key)) return { status: "OWNERSHIP_INCONSISTENT" };

    const node: OwnershipNode | undefined = graph.getOwnershipNode(currentRef);
    if (!node) return { status: "RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND" };

    visiting.add(key);
    const resolvedOwners: UserId[] = [];

    if (node.directOwnerUserId !== undefined && node.directOwnerUserId !== null) {
      const directOwner = parseUserId(node.directOwnerUserId);
      if (!directOwner.valid) {
        visiting.delete(key);
        return { status: "OWNERSHIP_UNRESOLVED" };
      }
      resolvedOwners.push(directOwner.userId);
    }

    const parents = node.parentResources ?? [];
    for (const parentRef of parents) {
      const parentResult = visit(parentRef);
      if (parentResult.status !== "RESOLVED") {
        visiting.delete(key);
        return parentResult;
      }
      resolvedOwners.push(parentResult.ownerUserId);
    }

    visiting.delete(key);

    if (resolvedOwners.length === 0) {
      return { status: "OWNERSHIP_UNRESOLVED" };
    }

    const firstOwner = resolvedOwners[0];
    if (resolvedOwners.some((owner) => owner !== firstOwner)) {
      return { status: "OWNERSHIP_INCONSISTENT" };
    }

    return { status: "RESOLVED", ownerUserId: firstOwner };
  };

  return visit(ref);
}

FILE: backend\src\domain\identity-ownership\ownership.types.ts
============================================================
import type { UserId } from "./identity.types";

/** Resource identity is independent of database and transport identifiers. */
export interface ResourceReference {
  readonly resourceType: string;
  readonly resourceId: string;
}

/**
 * Authoritative ownership graph node supplied by the owning module/repository.
 * A node may have direct ownership, inherited ownership, or both.
 * When both exist, every applicable path must resolve to the same user.
 *
 * parentResources may contain multiple references. This deliberately avoids
 * deciding the still-open Action and Schedule relationship cardinalities.
 */
export interface OwnershipNode {
  readonly ref: ResourceReference;
  readonly directOwnerUserId?: UserId | null;
  readonly parentResources?: readonly ResourceReference[];
}

export interface OwnershipGraphReader {
  getOwnershipNode(ref: ResourceReference): OwnershipNode | undefined;
}

export type OwnershipResolutionStatus =
  | "RESOLVED"
  | "RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND"
  | "OWNERSHIP_UNRESOLVED"
  | "OWNERSHIP_INCONSISTENT";

export type OwnershipResolution =
  | { status: "RESOLVED"; ownerUserId: UserId }
  | { status: Exclude<OwnershipResolutionStatus, "RESOLVED"> };

export type OwnershipErrorCode =
  | "OWNERSHIP_REQUIRED"
  | "OWNERSHIP_UNRESOLVED"
  | "OWNERSHIP_INCONSISTENT"
  | "OWNERSHIP_MISMATCH"
  | "OWNERSHIP_MODIFICATION_FORBIDDEN"
  | "RESOURCE_ACCESS_DENIED"
  | "RELATED_RESOURCE_ACCESS_DENIED"
  | "CROSS_SCOPE_REFERENCE_INVALID"
  | "OWNERSHIP_VALIDATION_FAILED";

export type OwnershipOperation = "READ" | "UPDATE" | "DELETE" | "ASSOCIATE";

export type OwnershipValidationResult =
  | { valid: true; ownerUserId: UserId }
  | { valid: false; code: OwnershipErrorCode };

export type AccessDecision =
  | { allowed: true; ownerUserId: UserId }
  | { allowed: false; code: OwnershipErrorCode };

FILE: backend\src\domain\identity-ownership\ownership.validation.ts
============================================================
import { parseUserId } from "./identity.types";
import type { OwnershipGraphReader, OwnershipValidationResult, ResourceReference } from "./ownership.types";
import { resolveResourceOwner } from "./ownership.resolver";

export function validateResourceOwnership(
  callerUserId: unknown,
  resource: ResourceReference,
  graph: OwnershipGraphReader,
): OwnershipValidationResult {
  const identity = parseUserId(callerUserId);
  if (!identity.valid) return { valid: false, code: identity.code };

  const resolution = resolveResourceOwner(resource, graph);
  if (resolution.status === "RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND") {
    // Do not reveal whether a protected resource exists to an unauthorized caller.
    return { valid: false, code: "RESOURCE_ACCESS_DENIED" };
  }
  if (resolution.status === "OWNERSHIP_UNRESOLVED") {
    return { valid: false, code: "OWNERSHIP_UNRESOLVED" };
  }
  if (resolution.status === "OWNERSHIP_INCONSISTENT") {
    return { valid: false, code: "OWNERSHIP_INCONSISTENT" };
  }
  if (resolution.ownerUserId !== identity.userId) {
    return { valid: false, code: "OWNERSHIP_MISMATCH" };
  }

  return { valid: true, ownerUserId: resolution.ownerUserId };
}

export function validateRelatedResourceOwnership(
  callerUserId: unknown,
  source: ResourceReference,
  related: ResourceReference,
  graph: OwnershipGraphReader,
): OwnershipValidationResult {
  const sourceResult = validateResourceOwnership(callerUserId, source, graph);
  if (!sourceResult.valid) return sourceResult;

  const relatedResult = validateResourceOwnership(callerUserId, related, graph);
  if (!relatedResult.valid) {
    return { valid: false, code: "RELATED_RESOURCE_ACCESS_DENIED" };
  }

  if (sourceResult.ownerUserId !== relatedResult.ownerUserId) {
    return { valid: false, code: "CROSS_SCOPE_REFERENCE_INVALID" };
  }

  return sourceResult;
}

import type { GoalId, GoalScope, UserId, WorkspaceReader, ProjectReader, C02ValidationResult } from "./workspace-project.types";
import { validateGoalScope } from "./goal-scope.validation";

export interface GoalScopeMovementRequest {
  readonly callerUserId: UserId;
  readonly goalId: GoalId;
  readonly sourceScope: GoalScope;
  readonly destinationScope: GoalScope;
}

export type GoalScopeMovementError =
  | "SOURCE_SCOPE_INVALID"
  | "DESTINATION_SCOPE_INVALID"
  | "DESTINATION_ACCESS_DENIED"
  | "HIERARCHY_SCOPE_INVALID"
  | "DEPENDENT_RESOURCE_CONFLICT";

/**
 * C02 scope preflight contract only. C03 must supply hierarchy/dependent-resource checks and
 * own the actual Goal mutation. This function deliberately performs no movement or transaction.
 */
export function validateGoalScopeMovementScopes(
  request: GoalScopeMovementRequest,
  projects: ProjectReader,
  workspaces: WorkspaceReader,
): C02ValidationResult<GoalScopeMovementError> {
  const source = validateGoalScope(request.callerUserId, request.sourceScope, projects, workspaces);
  if (!source.valid) return { valid: false, code: "SOURCE_SCOPE_INVALID" };

  const destination = validateGoalScope(request.callerUserId, request.destinationScope, projects, workspaces);
  if (!destination.valid) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      code: destination.code === "GOAL_SCOPE_ACCESS_DENIED" ? "DESTINATION_ACCESS_DENIED" : "DESTINATION_SCOPE_INVALID",
    };
  }
  return { valid: true };
}

FILE: backend\src\domain\workspace-project\goal-scope.validation.ts
============================================================
import type { C02ValidationResult, GoalScope, ProjectReader, UserId, WorkspaceReader } from "./workspace-project.types";
import { validateWorkspaceOwnership } from "./workspace.validation";
import { validateProjectScope } from "./project.validation";

export type GoalScopeError =
  | "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND"
  | "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND"
  | "GOAL_PROJECT_WORKSPACE_MISMATCH"
  | "GOAL_SCOPE_INVALID"
  | "GOAL_SCOPE_ACCESS_DENIED";

/** Validates organizational scope only. It does not read, create, or mutate a C03 Goal entity. */
export function validateGoalScope(
  callerUserId: UserId,
  scope: GoalScope,
  projects: ProjectReader,
  workspaces: WorkspaceReader,
): C02ValidationResult<GoalScopeError> {
  if (!scope || !scope.workspaceId || (scope.projectId !== null && !scope.projectId)) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_SCOPE_INVALID" };
  }

  const workspaceResult = validateWorkspaceOwnership(callerUserId, scope.workspaceId, workspaces);
  if (!workspaceResult.valid) {
    if (workspaceResult.code === "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND") return { valid: false, code: "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND" };
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_SCOPE_ACCESS_DENIED" };
  }

  if (scope.projectId === null) return { valid: true };
  const projectResult = validateProjectScope(callerUserId, scope.projectId, scope.workspaceId, projects, workspaces);
  if (!projectResult.valid) {
    if (projectResult.code === "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND") return { valid: false, code: "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND" };
    if (projectResult.code === "PROJECT_WORKSPACE_MISMATCH") return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_PROJECT_WORKSPACE_MISMATCH" };
    if (projectResult.code === "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND") return { valid: false, code: "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND" };
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_SCOPE_ACCESS_DENIED" };
  }
  return { valid: true };
}

import type { ProjectId, ProjectReader, WorkspaceReader, ProjectOwnerResolution } from "./workspace-project.types";

/** Project ownership is inherited only through its authoritative Workspace relationship. */
export function resolveProjectOwner(
  projectId: ProjectId,
  projects: ProjectReader,
  workspaces: WorkspaceReader,
): ProjectOwnerResolution {
  const project = projects.getProject(projectId);
  if (!project) return { status: "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND" };
  if (!project.workspaceId) return { status: "OWNERSHIP_UNRESOLVED" };

  const workspace = workspaces.getWorkspace(project.workspaceId);
  if (!workspace) return { status: "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND" };
  if (!workspace.ownerUserId) return { status: "OWNERSHIP_UNRESOLVED" };

  return {
    status: "RESOLVED",
    projectId: project.id,
    workspaceId: workspace.id,
    ownerUserId: workspace.ownerUserId,
  };
}

FILE: backend\src\domain\workspace-project\project.validation.ts
============================================================
import type { C02ValidationResult, ProjectId, ProjectReader, UserId, WorkspaceId, WorkspaceReader } from "./workspace-project.types";
import { resolveProjectOwner } from "./project.resolver";

export type ProjectScopeError =
  | "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND"
  | "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND"
  | "PROJECT_WORKSPACE_MISMATCH"
  | "PROJECT_ACCESS_DENIED";

export function validateProjectScope(
  callerUserId: UserId,
  projectId: ProjectId,
  expectedWorkspaceId: WorkspaceId,
  projects: ProjectReader,
  workspaces: WorkspaceReader,
): C02ValidationResult<ProjectScopeError> {
  const project = projects.getProject(projectId);
  if (!project) return { valid: false, code: "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND" };
  if (project.workspaceId !== expectedWorkspaceId) return { valid: false, code: "PROJECT_WORKSPACE_MISMATCH" };

  const resolution = resolveProjectOwner(projectId, projects, workspaces);
  if (resolution.status === "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND") return { valid: false, code: "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND" };
  if (resolution.status !== "RESOLVED") return { valid: false, code: "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND" };
  if (resolution.ownerUserId !== callerUserId) return { valid: false, code: "PROJECT_ACCESS_DENIED" };
  return { valid: true };
}

/** Only a minimal non-empty check is applied; max length/character rules remain OPEN in the spec. */
export function validateProjectName(name: string): C02ValidationResult<"PROJECT_INVALID_NAME"> {
  return typeof name === "string" && name.trim().length > 0
    ? { valid: true }
    : { valid: false, code: "PROJECT_INVALID_NAME" };
}

export const C02_ERROR_CODES = [
  "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND",
  "WORKSPACE_ACCESS_DENIED",
  "WORKSPACE_OWNER_INVALID",
  "WORKSPACE_ALREADY_EXISTS",
  "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND",
  "PROJECT_ACCESS_DENIED",
  "PROJECT_WORKSPACE_REQUIRED",
  "PROJECT_WORKSPACE_MISMATCH",
  "PROJECT_INVALID_NAME",
  "GOAL_SCOPE_INVALID",
  "GOAL_PROJECT_NOT_FOUND",
  "GOAL_PROJECT_WORKSPACE_MISMATCH",
  "GOAL_SCOPE_ACCESS_DENIED",
  "SOURCE_SCOPE_INVALID",
  "DESTINATION_SCOPE_INVALID",
  "DESTINATION_ACCESS_DENIED",
  "HIERARCHY_SCOPE_INVALID",
  "DEPENDENT_RESOURCE_CONFLICT",
] as const;

export type C02ErrorCode = (typeof C02_ERROR_CODES)[number];

export class C02DomainError extends Error {
  constructor(public readonly code: C02ErrorCode, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "C02DomainError";
  }
}

/** C02 domain identifiers. Keep as strings until D03 centralizes shared branded IDs. */
export type UserId = string;

export type ProjectId = string;
export type GoalId = string;

  readonly id: WorkspaceId;
  readonly ownerUserId: UserId;
  readonly createdAt: string;
  readonly updatedAt: string;
}

export interface Project {
  readonly id: ProjectId;
  readonly workspaceId: WorkspaceId;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly description?: string;
  readonly createdAt: string;
  readonly updatedAt: string;
}

export interface GoalScope {
  readonly workspaceId: WorkspaceId;
  /** null means independent Workspace scope; otherwise Project scope. */
  readonly projectId: ProjectId | null;
}

export interface WorkspaceReader {
  getWorkspace(id: WorkspaceId): Workspace | null;
  getPersonalWorkspaceByOwner(ownerUserId: UserId): Workspace | null;
}

export interface ProjectReader {
  getProject(id: ProjectId): Project | null;
}

  | { readonly valid: true }
  | { readonly valid: false; readonly code: TCode };

  | { readonly status: "RESOLVED"; readonly projectId: ProjectId; readonly workspaceId: WorkspaceId; readonly ownerUserId: UserId }
  | { readonly status: "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND" }
  | { readonly status: "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND" }
  | { readonly status: "OWNERSHIP_UNRESOLVED" }
  | { readonly status: "OWNERSHIP_INCONSISTENT" };

FILE: backend\src\domain\workspace-project\workspace.validation.ts

import type { UserId, Workspace, WorkspaceId, WorkspaceReader, C02ValidationResult } from "./workspace-project.types";

export type WorkspaceOwnershipError = "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND" | "WORKSPACE_ACCESS_DENIED" | "WORKSPACE_OWNER_INVALID";

export function validateWorkspaceOwnership(
  callerUserId: UserId,
  workspaceId: WorkspaceId,
  reader: WorkspaceReader,
): C02ValidationResult<WorkspaceOwnershipError> {
  const workspace = reader.getWorkspace(workspaceId);
  if (!workspace) return { valid: false, code: "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND" };
  if (!workspace.ownerUserId) return { valid: false, code: "WORKSPACE_OWNER_INVALID" };
  if (workspace.ownerUserId !== callerUserId) return { valid: false, code: "WORKSPACE_ACCESS_DENIED" };
  return { valid: true };
}

export function validatePersonalWorkspaceUniqueness(
  ownerUserId: UserId,
  reader: WorkspaceReader,
): C02ValidationResult<"WORKSPACE_ALREADY_EXISTS"> {
  return reader.getPersonalWorkspaceByOwner(ownerUserId)
    ? { valid: false, code: "WORKSPACE_ALREADY_EXISTS" }
    : { valid: true };
}

/** Structural guard for data returned by persistence; it does not create or mutate a Workspace. */
export function isValidWorkspace(workspace: Workspace | null): workspace is Workspace {
  return !!workspace && !!workspace.id && !!workspace.ownerUserId && !!workspace.createdAt && !!workspace.updatedAt;
}

import type { AuthProvider } from "../../modules/auth/auth.provider.ts";
import { createSupabaseAuthProvider } from "../../modules/auth/supabase.provider.ts";
import { createLocalDevAuthProvider } from "./local-dev.provider.ts";

export * from "./token-verifier.ts";
export * from "./local-dev.provider.ts";

export interface AuthConfig {
  supabaseUrl?: string;
  supabaseAnonKey?: string;
  devMode?: boolean;
}

export function createAuthProvider(
  config: AuthConfig
): AuthProvider {
  const hasSupabaseConfig =
    Boolean(config.supabaseUrl?.trim()) &&
    Boolean(config.supabaseAnonKey?.trim());

  // Explicit local development mode without Supabase configuration.
  if (config.devMode === true && !hasSupabaseConfig) {
    return createLocalDevAuthProvider();
  }

  // Supabase is required when local development mode is disabled.
  if (!hasSupabaseConfig) {
    throw new Error(
      "Supabase Auth configuration is required. " +
      "Set SUPABASE_URL and SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, " +
      "or explicitly enable AUTH_DEV_MODE=true for local development."
    );
  }

  try {
    return createSupabaseAuthProvider(
      config.supabaseUrl!,
      config.supabaseAnonKey!
    );
  } catch {
    throw new Error(
      "Failed to initialize Supabase Auth provider."
    );
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\infrastructure\auth\local-dev.provider.ts
============================================================

import {
  randomUUID,
  randomBytes,
  scrypt,
  timingSafeEqual
} from "node:crypto";

import type {
  AuthProvider,
  ProviderAuthEvent,
  ProviderResult,
  ProviderSession,
  ProviderUser
} from "../../modules/auth/auth.provider.ts";

interface StoredDevUser {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  passwordHash: string;
}

const PASSWORD_KEY_LENGTH = 64;

function derivePasswordHash(
  password: string,
  salt: string
): Promise<Buffer> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    scrypt(password, salt, PASSWORD_KEY_LENGTH, (error, derivedKey) => {
      if (error) {
        reject(error);
        return;
      }

      resolve(derivedKey as Buffer);
    });
  });
}

async function hashPassword(password: string): Promise<string> {
  const salt = randomBytes(16).toString("hex");
  const hash = await derivePasswordHash(password, salt);

  return `${salt}:${hash.toString("hex")}`;
}

async function verifyPassword(
  password: string,
  storedHash: string
): Promise<boolean> {
  const [salt, originalHash] = storedHash.split(":");

  if (!salt || !originalHash || !/^[a-f0-9]+$/i.test(originalHash)) {
    return false;
  }

  const originalBuffer = Buffer.from(originalHash, "hex");

  if (originalBuffer.length !== PASSWORD_KEY_LENGTH) {
    return false;
  }

  const derivedHash = await derivePasswordHash(password, salt);

  return timingSafeEqual(originalBuffer, derivedHash);
}

export class LocalDevAuthProvider implements AuthProvider {
  private readonly users = new Map<string, StoredDevUser>();

  private currentSession: ProviderSession | null = null;

  private readonly listeners = new Set<
    (
      event: ProviderAuthEvent,
      session: ProviderSession | null
    ) => void
  >();

  async register(
    email: string,
    password: string
  ): Promise<
    ProviderResult<{
      user: ProviderUser | null;
      session: ProviderSession | null;
    }>
  > {
    const normalizedEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();

    for (const user of this.users.values()) {
      if (user.email === normalizedEmail) {
        return {
          data: { user: null, session: null },
          error: new Error("User already exists")
        };
      }
    }

    const id = `dev-user-${randomUUID()}`;
    const passwordHash = await hashPassword(password);

    const storedUser: StoredDevUser = {
      id,
      email: normalizedEmail,
      passwordHash
    };

    this.users.set(id, storedUser);

    const providerUser: ProviderUser = {
      id,
      email: normalizedEmail
    };

    const session: ProviderSession = {
      user: providerUser,
      accessToken: `dev-token:${id}:${normalizedEmail}`
    };

    this.currentSession = session;
    this.notify("SIGNED_IN", session);

    return {
      data: { user: providerUser, session },
      error: null
    };
  }

  async signIn(
    email: string,
    password: string
  ): Promise<
    ProviderResult<{
      user: ProviderUser | null;
      session: ProviderSession | null;
    }>
  > {
    const normalizedEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();

    let found: StoredDevUser | null = null;

    for (const user of this.users.values()) {
      if (user.email === normalizedEmail) {
        found = user;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (!found || !(await verifyPassword(password, found.passwordHash))) {
      return {
        data: { user: null, session: null },
        error: new Error("Invalid credentials")
      };
    }

    const providerUser: ProviderUser = {
      id: found.id,
      email: found.email
    };

    const session: ProviderSession = {
      user: providerUser,
      accessToken: `dev-token:${found.id}:${found.email}`
    };

    this.currentSession = session;
    this.notify("SIGNED_IN", session);

    return {
      data: { user: providerUser, session },
      error: null
    };
  }

  async signOut(): Promise<ProviderResult<null>> {
    this.currentSession = null;
    this.notify("SIGNED_OUT", null);

    return {
      data: null,
      error: null
    };
  }

  async getSession(): Promise<
    ProviderResult<{ session: ProviderSession | null }>
  > {
    return {
      data: { session: this.currentSession },
      error: null
    };
  }

  onAuthStateChange(
    callback: (
      event: ProviderAuthEvent,
      session: ProviderSession | null
    ) => void
  ): () => void {
    this.listeners.add(callback);

    return () => {
      this.listeners.delete(callback);
    };
  }

  private notify(
    event: ProviderAuthEvent,
    session: ProviderSession | null
  ): void {
    for (const listener of this.listeners) {
      try {
        listener(event, session);
      } catch {
        // Ignore callback errors.
      }
    }
  }
}

export function createLocalDevAuthProvider(): AuthProvider {
  return new LocalDevAuthProvider();
}

import type { AuthenticatedUserContext } from "../../modules/auth/auth.types.ts";

export interface TokenVerifier {
  verifyToken(token: string): Promise<AuthenticatedUserContext | null>;
}

export interface TokenVerifierOptions {
  supabaseUrl?: string;
  supabaseKey?: string;
  devMode?: boolean;
}

export class SupabaseOrDevTokenVerifier implements TokenVerifier {
  private readonly options: TokenVerifierOptions;

  constructor(options: TokenVerifierOptions) {
    this.options = options;
  }

  async verifyToken(
    token: string
  ): Promise<AuthenticatedUserContext | null> {
    const cleanToken = token.trim();

    if (!cleanToken) return null;

    const isDevToken =
      cleanToken.startsWith("dev:") ||
      cleanToken.startsWith("dev-token:") ||
      cleanToken.startsWith("mock-token:");

    // Development tokens are accepted only when explicitly enabled.
    if (isDevToken) {
      if (this.options.devMode !== true) return null;

      return this.parseDevToken(cleanToken);
    }

    // Supabase access tokens must always be verified with Supabase,
    // including when the application is running in development mode.
    if (!this.options.supabaseUrl || !this.options.supabaseKey) {
      return null;
    }

    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        `${this.options.supabaseUrl}/auth/v1/user`,
        {
          method: "GET",
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${cleanToken}`,
            apikey: this.options.supabaseKey
          }
        }
      );

      if (!response.ok) return null;

      const data = (await response.json()) as {
        id?: string;
        email?: string;
      };

      if (!data?.id) return null;

      return {
        userId: data.id,
        email: data.email ?? null,
        isAuthenticated: true
      };
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  private parseDevToken(
    token: string
  ): AuthenticatedUserContext | null {
    // Format: dev-token:<userId>:<email>
    if (token.startsWith("dev-token:")) {
      const parts = token.slice("dev-token:".length).split(":");
      const userId = parts[0]?.trim();
      const email = parts[1]?.trim();

      if (!userId) return null;

      return {
        userId,
        email: email || `${userId}@example.com`,
        isAuthenticated: true
      };
    }

    // Format: mock-token:<userId>
    if (token.startsWith("mock-token:")) {
      const userId = token.slice("mock-token:".length).trim();

      if (!userId) return null;

      return {
        userId,
        email: `${userId}@example.com`,
        isAuthenticated: true
      };
    }

    // Format: dev:<userId>
    if (token.startsWith("dev:")) {
      const userId = token.slice("dev:".length).trim();

      if (!userId) return null;

      return {
        userId,
        email: `${userId}@example.com`,
        isAuthenticated: true
      };
    }

    return null;
  }
}

export function createTokenVerifier(
  options: TokenVerifierOptions
): TokenVerifier {
  return new SupabaseOrDevTokenVerifier(options);
}

/**
 * FLOW-UP — SQLite Database Schema & Migrations
 * MVP temporary database configuration.
 *
 * Enforces:
 * - Foreign keys enabled
 * - Multiple owner-scoped workspaces per user
 * - Project workspace foreign keys
 * - Goal hierarchy relational integrity
 * - Milestone/Action/Schedule constraints
 */

export const SCHEMA_SQL = `
-- Workspaces are user-owned containers; a user may have multiple.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS workspaces (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_workspaces_user_id ON workspaces(user_id, created_at);

-- Projects table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projects (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  workspace_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES workspaces(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

-- Goals table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS goals (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  workspace_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES workspaces(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  project_id TEXT REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  parent_goal_id TEXT REFERENCES goals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  target_date TEXT,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  archived_at TEXT
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_goals_workspace_id ON goals(workspace_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_goals_project_id ON goals(project_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_goals_parent_goal_id ON goals(parent_goal_id);

-- Milestones table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS milestones (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  goal_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES goals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  target_date TEXT,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  completed_at TEXT
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_milestones_goal_id ON milestones(goal_id);

-- Actions table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS actions (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  goal_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES goals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  milestone_id TEXT REFERENCES milestones(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'TODO',
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  completed_at TEXT,
  archived_at TEXT
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_actions_goal_id ON actions(goal_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_actions_milestone_id ON actions(milestone_id);

-- Action executions table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS action_executions (
  action_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES actions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  actual_started_at TEXT,
  actual_completed_at TEXT,
  actual_duration_minutes INTEGER,
  execution_note TEXT,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

-- Daily Plans table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS daily_plans (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  date TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE(user_id, date)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_daily_plans_user_date ON daily_plans(user_id, date);

-- Schedules table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schedules (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  action_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES actions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  daily_plan_id TEXT REFERENCES daily_plans(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  start_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  end_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_schedules_action_id ON schedules(action_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_schedules_daily_plan_id ON schedules(daily_plan_id);

-- B06 Measurement definitions and immutable value history.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS measurements (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  goal_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES goals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  label TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  value_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  unit TEXT,
  target_value TEXT,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  archived_at TEXT
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_measurements_goal_id ON measurements(goal_id);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS measurement_records (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  measurement_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES measurements(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  value_json TEXT NOT NULL,
  recorded_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  note TEXT
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_measurement_records_measurement_recorded
  ON measurement_records(measurement_id, recorded_at, created_at);

-- B10 owns these AI workflow records; domain tables remain owned by their modules.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ai_proposals (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  operation_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  proposal_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL,
  proposal_data TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ai_proposals_user_created
  ON ai_proposals(user_id, created_at);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ai_usage (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ai_usage_user_created
  ON ai_usage(user_id, created_at);
`;

FILE: backend\src\infrastructure\database\sqlite.ts

import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { SCHEMA_SQL } from "./schema.ts";

export interface DatabaseConnection {
  readonly db: DatabaseSync;
  runInTransaction<T>(fn: () => T): T;
  close(): void;
}

export interface SqliteOptions {
  dbPath?: string;
}

/**
 * Initializes SQLite database connection with foreign keys enabled
 * and executes initial schema migrations.
 */
export function createSqliteDatabase(options: SqliteOptions = {}): DatabaseConnection {
  const dbPath = options.dbPath ?? ":memory:";

  if (dbPath !== ":memory:") {
    const dir = path.dirname(path.resolve(dbPath));
    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  const db = new DatabaseSync(dbPath);

  // Enable foreign keys
  db.exec("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;");

  if (dbPath !== ":memory:") {
    try {
      db.exec("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;");
    } catch {
      // WAL may not be supported on all environments, ignore if fails
    }
  }

  // Initialize schema
  db.exec(SCHEMA_SQL);
  migrateMultipleWorkspaces(db);

  return {
    get db() {
      return db;
    },
    runInTransaction<T>(fn: () => T): T {
      db.exec("BEGIN IMMEDIATE TRANSACTION;");
      try {
        const result = fn();
        db.exec("COMMIT;");
        return result;
      } catch (error) {
        try {
          db.exec("ROLLBACK;");
        } catch {
          // ignore rollback failure if transaction already ended
        }
        throw error;
      }
    },
    close() {
      db.close();
    }
  };
}

/** Remove the legacy UNIQUE(user_id) constraint without changing existing workspace IDs or data. */
function migrateMultipleWorkspaces(db: DatabaseSync): void {
  const indexes = db.prepare("PRAGMA index_list('workspaces')").all() as Array<{ name: string; unique: number; origin: string }>;
  const hasLegacyUserUniqueness = indexes.some((index) => {
    if (index.unique !== 1 || index.origin !== "u") return false;
    const columns = db.prepare(`PRAGMA index_info('${index.name.replaceAll("'", "''")}')`).all() as Array<{ name: string }>;
    return columns.length === 1 && columns[0]?.name === "user_id";
  });
  if (!hasLegacyUserUniqueness) return;

  db.exec("PRAGMA foreign_keys = OFF;");
  try {
    db.exec(`
      BEGIN IMMEDIATE;
      CREATE TABLE workspaces_migration (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        name TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      );
      INSERT INTO workspaces_migration (id, user_id, name, created_at, updated_at)
        SELECT id, user_id, name, created_at, updated_at FROM workspaces;
      DROP TABLE workspaces;
      ALTER TABLE workspaces_migration RENAME TO workspaces;
      CREATE INDEX idx_workspaces_user_id ON workspaces(user_id, created_at);
      COMMIT;
    `);
  } catch (error) {
    try { db.exec("ROLLBACK;"); } catch { /* transaction may already have rolled back */ }
    throw error;
  } finally {
    db.exec("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;");
  }
}

import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../database/sqlite.ts";
import type {
  Action,
  ActionExecutionData,
  ActionStatus,
  RecordExecutionInput,
  UpdateActionInput
} from "../../modules/action/action.types.ts";
import type { ActionRepository } from "../../modules/action/action.repository.ts";

interface ActionRow {
  id: string;
  goal_id: string;
  milestone_id: string | null;
  title: string;
  description: string | null;
  status: ActionStatus;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
  completed_at: string | null;
  archived_at: string | null;
}

interface ActionExecutionRow {
  action_id: string;
  actual_started_at: string | null;
  actual_completed_at: string | null;
  actual_duration_minutes: number | null;
  execution_note: string | null;
  updated_at: string;
}

function mapAction(row: ActionRow): Action {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    goalId: row.goal_id,
    milestoneId: row.milestone_id,
    title: row.title,
    description: row.description,
    status: row.status,
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at,
    completedAt: row.completed_at,
    archivedAt: row.archived_at
  };
}

function mapExecution(row: ActionExecutionRow): ActionExecutionData {
  return {
    actionId: row.action_id,
    actualStartedAt: row.actual_started_at,
    actualCompletedAt: row.actual_completed_at,
    actualDurationMinutes: row.actual_duration_minutes,
    executionNote: row.execution_note
  };
}

export class SqliteActionRepository implements ActionRepository {
  private readonly connection: DatabaseConnection;

  constructor(connection: DatabaseConnection) {
    this.connection = connection;
  }

  async create(input: {
    goalId: string;
    milestoneId: string | null;
    title: string;
    description: string | null;
    status: "TODO";
  }): Promise<Action> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const description = input.description?.trim() ? input.description.trim() : null;
    const milestoneId = input.milestoneId ?? null;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO actions (
        id, goal_id, milestone_id, title, description, status,
        created_at, updated_at, completed_at, archived_at
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NULL, NULL)`
    );

    stmt.run(
      id,
      input.goalId,
      milestoneId,
      input.title.trim(),
      description,
      input.status,
      now,
      now
    );

    return {
      id,
      goalId: input.goalId,
      milestoneId,
      title: input.title.trim(),
      description,
      status: input.status,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
      completedAt: null,
      archivedAt: null
    };
  }

  async findById(actionId: string): Promise<Action | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, goal_id, milestone_id, title, description, status,
              created_at, updated_at, completed_at, archived_at
       FROM actions WHERE id = ?`
    );
    const row = stmt.get(actionId) as ActionRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapAction(row) : null;
  }

  async listByGoal(goalId: string): Promise<Action[]> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, goal_id, milestone_id, title, description, status,
              created_at, updated_at, completed_at, archived_at
       FROM actions WHERE goal_id = ? ORDER BY created_at ASC`
    );
    const rows = stmt.all(goalId) as unknown as ActionRow[];
    return rows.map(mapAction);
  }

  async listByMilestone(milestoneId: string): Promise<Action[]> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, goal_id, milestone_id, title, description, status,
              created_at, updated_at, completed_at, archived_at
       FROM actions WHERE milestone_id = ? ORDER BY created_at ASC`
    );
    const rows = stmt.all(milestoneId) as unknown as ActionRow[];
    return rows.map(mapAction);
  }

  async update(actionId: string, input: UpdateActionInput): Promise<Action> {
    const existing = await this.findById(actionId);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Action not found: ${actionId}`);
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const title = input.title !== undefined ? input.title.trim() : existing.title;
    const description =
      input.description !== undefined
        ? input.description === null || input.description.trim() === ""
          ? null
          : input.description.trim()
        : existing.description;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "UPDATE actions SET title = ?, description = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?"
    );
    stmt.run(title, description, now, actionId);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      goalId: existing.goalId,
      milestoneId: existing.milestoneId,
      title,
      description,
      status: existing.status,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      completedAt: existing.completedAt,
      archivedAt: existing.archivedAt
    };
  }

  async updateStatus(
    actionId: string,
    status: ActionStatus,
    completedAt: string | null
  ): Promise<Action> {
    const existing = await this.findById(actionId);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Action not found: ${actionId}`);
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "UPDATE actions SET status = ?, completed_at = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?"
    );
    stmt.run(status, completedAt, now, actionId);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      goalId: existing.goalId,
      milestoneId: existing.milestoneId,
      title: existing.title,
      description: existing.description,
      status,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      completedAt,
      archivedAt: existing.archivedAt
    };
  }

  async saveExecution(
    actionId: string,
    input: RecordExecutionInput
  ): Promise<ActionExecutionData> {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const actualStartedAt = input.actualStartedAt ?? null;
    const actualCompletedAt = input.actualCompletedAt ?? null;
    const actualDurationMinutes = input.actualDurationMinutes ?? null;
    const executionNote = input.executionNote?.trim() ? input.executionNote.trim() : null;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO action_executions (
        action_id, actual_started_at, actual_completed_at, actual_duration_minutes, execution_note, updated_at
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      ON CONFLICT(action_id) DO UPDATE SET
        actual_started_at = excluded.actual_started_at,
        actual_completed_at = excluded.actual_completed_at,
        actual_duration_minutes = excluded.actual_duration_minutes,
        execution_note = excluded.execution_note,
        updated_at = excluded.updated_at`
    );

    stmt.run(
      actionId,
      actualStartedAt,
      actualCompletedAt,
      actualDurationMinutes,
      executionNote,
      now
    );

    return {
      actionId,
      actualStartedAt,
      actualCompletedAt,
      actualDurationMinutes,
      executionNote
    };
  }

  async getExecution(actionId: string): Promise<ActionExecutionData | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT action_id, actual_started_at, actual_completed_at, actual_duration_minutes, execution_note, updated_at
       FROM action_executions WHERE action_id = ?`
    );
    const row = stmt.get(actionId) as ActionExecutionRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapExecution(row) : null;
  }
}
