import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../database/sqlite.ts";
import type {
  CreateGoalInput,
  Goal,
  ListGoalsFilter,
  UpdateGoalInput
} from "../../modules/goal/goal.types.ts";
import type { GoalRepository } from "../../modules/goal/goal.repository.ts";

interface GoalRow {
  id: string;
  workspace_id: string;
  project_id: string | null;
  parent_goal_id: string | null;
  title: string;
  description: string | null;
  target_date: string | null;
  status: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
  archived_at: string | null;
}

function mapGoal(row: GoalRow): Goal {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    workspaceId: row.workspace_id,
    projectId: row.project_id,
    parentGoalId: row.parent_goal_id,
    title: row.title,
    description: row.description,
    targetDate: row.target_date,
    status: row.status,
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at,
    archivedAt: row.archived_at
  };
}

export class SqliteGoalRepository implements GoalRepository {
  private readonly connection: DatabaseConnection;

  constructor(connection: DatabaseConnection) {
    this.connection = connection;
  }

  async createGoal(workspaceId: string, input: CreateGoalInput): Promise<Goal> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const projectId = input.projectId ?? null;
    const parentGoalId = input.parentGoalId ?? null;
    const description = input.description?.trim() ? input.description.trim() : null;
    const targetDate = input.targetDate?.trim() ? input.targetDate.trim() : null;
    const status = "ACTIVE";

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO goals (
        id, workspace_id, project_id, parent_goal_id, title, description,
        target_date, status, created_at, updated_at, archived_at
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NULL)`
    );

    stmt.run(
      id,
      workspaceId,
      projectId,
      parentGoalId,
      input.title.trim(),
      description,
      targetDate,
      status,
      now,
      now
    );

    return {
      id,
      workspaceId,
      projectId,
      parentGoalId,
      title: input.title.trim(),
      description,
      targetDate,
      status,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
      archivedAt: null
    };
  }

  async findGoalById(goalId: string): Promise<Goal | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, workspace_id, project_id, parent_goal_id, title, description,
              target_date, status, created_at, updated_at, archived_at
       FROM goals WHERE id = ?`
    );
    const row = stmt.get(goalId) as GoalRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapGoal(row) : null;
  }

  async listGoalsByWorkspaceId(
    workspaceId: string,
    filter?: ListGoalsFilter
  ): Promise<Goal[]> {
    let sql = `SELECT id, workspace_id, project_id, parent_goal_id, title, description,
                      target_date, status, created_at, updated_at, archived_at
               FROM goals WHERE workspace_id = ?`;
    const params: (string | null)[] = [workspaceId];

    if (filter) {
      if (filter.projectId !== undefined) {
        if (filter.projectId === null) {
          sql += " AND project_id IS NULL";
        } else {
          sql += " AND project_id = ?";
          params.push(filter.projectId);
        }
      }

      if (filter.parentGoalId !== undefined) {
        if (filter.parentGoalId === null) {
          sql += " AND parent_goal_id IS NULL";
        } else {
          sql += " AND parent_goal_id = ?";
          params.push(filter.parentGoalId);
        }
      }

      if (filter.status !== undefined) {
        sql += " AND status = ?";
        params.push(filter.status);
      }

      if (!filter.includeArchived) {
        sql += " AND archived_at IS NULL";
      }
    } else {
      sql += " AND archived_at IS NULL";
    }

    sql += " ORDER BY created_at ASC";

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(sql);
    const rows = stmt.all(...params) as unknown as GoalRow[];
    return rows.map(mapGoal);
  }

  async updateGoal(goalId: string, input: UpdateGoalInput): Promise<Goal> {
    const existing = await this.findGoalById(goalId);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Goal not found: ${goalId}`);
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const title = input.title !== undefined ? input.title.trim() : existing.title;
    const description =
      input.description !== undefined
        ? input.description === null || input.description.trim() === ""
          ? null
          : input.description.trim()
        : existing.description;
    const targetDate =
      input.targetDate !== undefined
        ? input.targetDate === null || input.targetDate.trim() === ""
          ? null
          : input.targetDate.trim()
        : existing.targetDate;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `UPDATE goals SET title = ?, description = ?, target_date = ?, updated_at = ?
       WHERE id = ?`
    );
    stmt.run(title, description, targetDate, now, goalId);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      workspaceId: existing.workspaceId,
      projectId: existing.projectId,
      parentGoalId: existing.parentGoalId,
      title,
      description,
      targetDate,
      status: existing.status,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      archivedAt: existing.archivedAt
    };
  }

  async findDescendantIds(goalId: string): Promise<string[]> {
    const sql = `
      WITH RECURSIVE descendants(id) AS (
        SELECT id FROM goals WHERE parent_goal_id = ?
        UNION ALL
        SELECT g.id FROM goals g JOIN descendants d ON g.parent_goal_id = d.id
      )
      SELECT id FROM descendants;
    `;
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(sql);
    const rows = stmt.all(goalId) as unknown as { id: string }[];
    return rows.map((r) => r.id);
  }

  async countIndependentGoals(workspaceId: string): Promise<number> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM goals WHERE workspace_id = ? AND project_id IS NULL AND archived_at IS NULL"
    );
    const row = stmt.get(workspaceId) as { count: number } | undefined;
    return row ? Number(row.count) : 0;
  }

  async countProjectGoals(projectId: string): Promise<number> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM goals WHERE project_id = ? AND archived_at IS NULL"
    );
    const row = stmt.get(projectId) as { count: number } | undefined;
    return row ? Number(row.count) : 0;
  }
}

import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../database/sqlite.ts";
import type { MeasurementRepository } from "../../modules/measurement/measurement.repository.ts";
import type { Measurement, MeasurementHistoryQuery, MeasurementRecord } from "../../modules/measurement/measurement.types.ts";

interface MeasurementRow {
  id: string; goal_id: string; label: string; description: string | null; value_type: string;
  unit: string | null; target_value: string | null; created_at: string; updated_at: string; archived_at: string | null;
}
interface RecordRow {
  id: string; measurement_id: string; value_json: string; recorded_at: string;
  created_at: string; note: string | null;
}

function mapMeasurement(row: MeasurementRow): Measurement {
  return {
    id: row.id, goalId: row.goal_id, label: row.label, description: row.description,
    valueType: row.value_type, unit: row.unit,
    targetValue: row.target_value === null ? null : JSON.parse(row.target_value) as Measurement["targetValue"],
    createdAt: row.created_at, updatedAt: row.updated_at, archivedAt: row.archived_at,
  };
}
function mapRecord(row: RecordRow): MeasurementRecord {
  return {
    id: row.id, measurementId: row.measurement_id,
    value: JSON.parse(row.value_json) as MeasurementRecord["value"],
    recordedAt: row.recorded_at, createdAt: row.created_at, note: row.note,
  };
}

export class SqliteMeasurementRepository implements MeasurementRepository {
  constructor(private readonly connection: DatabaseConnection) {}

  async createMeasurement(input: Omit<Measurement, "id">): Promise<Measurement> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    this.connection.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO measurements
      (id, goal_id, label, description, value_type, unit, target_value, created_at, updated_at, archived_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`)
      .run(id, input.goalId, input.label, input.description, input.valueType, input.unit,
        input.targetValue === null ? null : JSON.stringify(input.targetValue),
        input.createdAt, input.updatedAt, input.archivedAt);
    return { ...input, id };
  }

  async getMeasurement(id: string): Promise<Measurement | null> {
    const row = this.connection.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM measurements WHERE id = ?").get(id) as MeasurementRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapMeasurement(row) : null;
  }

  async listMeasurementsByGoal(goalId: string, includeArchived: boolean): Promise<Measurement[]> {
    const rows = this.connection.db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM measurements WHERE goal_id = ? ${includeArchived ? "" : "AND archived_at IS NULL"} ORDER BY created_at, id`)
      .all(goalId) as unknown as MeasurementRow[];
    return rows.map(mapMeasurement);
  }

  async updateMeasurement(id: string, patch: Partial<Pick<Measurement, "label" | "description" | "unit" | "targetValue" | "updatedAt">>): Promise<Measurement> {
    const current = await this.getMeasurement(id);
    if (!current) throw new Error("Measurement not found during update.");
    const next = { ...current, ...patch };
    this.connection.db.prepare(`UPDATE measurements SET label = ?, description = ?, unit = ?, target_value = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`)
      .run(next.label, next.description, next.unit, next.targetValue === null ? null : JSON.stringify(next.targetValue), next.updatedAt, id);
    return next;
  }

  async archiveMeasurement(id: string, archivedAt: string): Promise<Measurement> {
    this.connection.db.prepare("UPDATE measurements SET archived_at = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?")
      .run(archivedAt, archivedAt, id);
    const result = await this.getMeasurement(id);
    if (!result) throw new Error("Measurement not found during archive.");
    return result;
  }

  async createRecord(input: Omit<MeasurementRecord, "id">): Promise<MeasurementRecord> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    this.connection.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO measurement_records
      (id, measurement_id, value_json, recorded_at, created_at, note) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`)
      .run(id, input.measurementId, JSON.stringify(input.value), input.recordedAt, input.createdAt, input.note);
    return { ...input, id };
  }

  async listRecords(measurementId: string, query: MeasurementHistoryQuery): Promise<MeasurementRecord[]> {
    const where = ["measurement_id = ?"];
    const params: (string | number)[] = [measurementId];
    if (query.from) { where.push("recorded_at >= ?"); params.push(query.from); }
    if (query.to) { where.push("recorded_at <= ?"); params.push(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(query.to) ? `${query.to}T23:59:59.999Z` : query.to); }
    let sql = `SELECT * FROM measurement_records WHERE ${where.join(" AND ")} ORDER BY recorded_at DESC, created_at DESC, id DESC`;
    if (query.limit !== undefined) { sql += " LIMIT ?"; params.push(query.limit); }
    if (query.offset !== undefined) { if (query.limit === undefined) sql += " LIMIT -1"; sql += " OFFSET ?"; params.push(query.offset); }
    return (this.connection.db.prepare(sql).all(...params) as unknown as RecordRow[]).map(mapRecord);
  }
}

import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../database/sqlite.ts";
import type {
  Milestone,
  MilestoneStatus,
  UpdateMilestoneInput
} from "../../modules/milestone/milestone.types.ts";
import type { MilestoneRepository } from "../../modules/milestone/milestone.repository.ts";

interface MilestoneRow {
  id: string;
  goal_id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string | null;
  target_date: string | null;
  status: MilestoneStatus;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
  completed_at: string | null;
}

function mapMilestone(row: MilestoneRow): Milestone {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    goalId: row.goal_id,
    title: row.title,
    description: row.description,
    targetDate: row.target_date,
    status: row.status,
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at,
    completedAt: row.completed_at
  };
}

export class SqliteMilestoneRepository implements MilestoneRepository {
  private readonly connection: DatabaseConnection;

  constructor(connection: DatabaseConnection) {
    this.connection = connection;
  }

  async createMilestone(input: {
    goalId: string;
    title: string;
    description: string | null;
    targetDate: string | null;
  }): Promise<Milestone> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const status: MilestoneStatus = "PENDING";
    const description = input.description?.trim() ? input.description.trim() : null;
    const targetDate = input.targetDate?.trim() ? input.targetDate.trim() : null;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO milestones (
        id, goal_id, title, description, target_date, status, created_at, updated_at, completed_at
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NULL)`
    );

    stmt.run(
      id,
      input.goalId,
      input.title.trim(),
      description,
      targetDate,
      status,
      now,
      now
    );

    return {
      id,
      goalId: input.goalId,
      title: input.title.trim(),
      description,
      targetDate,
      status,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
      completedAt: null
    };
  }

  async findMilestoneById(milestoneId: string): Promise<Milestone | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, goal_id, title, description, target_date, status, created_at, updated_at, completed_at
       FROM milestones WHERE id = ?`
    );
    const row = stmt.get(milestoneId) as MilestoneRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapMilestone(row) : null;
  }

  async listMilestonesByGoal(goalId: string): Promise<Milestone[]> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, goal_id, title, description, target_date, status, created_at, updated_at, completed_at
       FROM milestones WHERE goal_id = ? ORDER BY created_at ASC`
    );
    const rows = stmt.all(goalId) as unknown as MilestoneRow[];
    return rows.map(mapMilestone);
  }

  async updateMilestone(
    milestoneId: string,
    input: UpdateMilestoneInput
  ): Promise<Milestone> {
    const existing = await this.findMilestoneById(milestoneId);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Milestone not found: ${milestoneId}`);
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const title = input.title !== undefined ? input.title.trim() : existing.title;
    const description =
      input.description !== undefined
        ? input.description === null || input.description.trim() === ""
          ? null
          : input.description.trim()
        : existing.description;
    const targetDate =
      input.targetDate !== undefined
        ? input.targetDate === null || input.targetDate.trim() === ""
          ? null
          : input.targetDate.trim()
        : existing.targetDate;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `UPDATE milestones SET title = ?, description = ?, target_date = ?, updated_at = ?
       WHERE id = ?`
    );
    stmt.run(title, description, targetDate, now, milestoneId);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      goalId: existing.goalId,
      title,
      description,
      targetDate,
      status: existing.status,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      completedAt: existing.completedAt
    };
  }

  async completeMilestone(
    milestoneId: string,
    completedAt: string
  ): Promise<Milestone> {
    const existing = await this.findMilestoneById(milestoneId);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Milestone not found: ${milestoneId}`);
    }

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `UPDATE milestones SET status = 'COMPLETED', completed_at = ?, updated_at = ?
       WHERE id = ?`
    );
    stmt.run(completedAt, completedAt, milestoneId);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      goalId: existing.goalId,
      title: existing.title,
      description: existing.description,
      targetDate: existing.targetDate,
      status: "COMPLETED",
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: completedAt,
      completedAt
    };
  }
}

import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../database/sqlite.ts";
import type {
  DailyPlan,
  DailyPlanId,
  PlanningDateRange,
  ScheduleEntry,
  ScheduleId
} from "../../modules/planning/planning.types.ts";
import type { PlanningRepository } from "../../modules/planning/planning.repository.ts";

interface DailyPlanRow {
  id: string;
  user_id: string;
  date: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

interface ScheduleRow {
  id: string;
  action_id: string;
  daily_plan_id: string | null;
  start_at: string;
  end_at: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

function mapDailyPlan(row: DailyPlanRow): DailyPlan {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    userId: row.user_id,
    date: row.date,
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at
  };
}

function mapSchedule(row: ScheduleRow): ScheduleEntry {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    actionId: row.action_id,
    dailyPlanId: row.daily_plan_id,
    startAt: row.start_at,
    endAt: row.end_at,
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at
  };
}

export class SqlitePlanningRepository implements PlanningRepository {
  private readonly connection: DatabaseConnection;

  constructor(connection: DatabaseConnection) {
    this.connection = connection;
  }

  async createDailyPlan(input: Omit<DailyPlan, "id">): Promise<DailyPlan> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO daily_plans (id, user_id, date, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"
    );
    stmt.run(id, input.userId, input.date, input.createdAt, input.updatedAt);

    return {
      id,
      userId: input.userId,
      date: input.date,
      createdAt: input.createdAt,
      updatedAt: input.updatedAt
    };
  }

  async getDailyPlan(id: DailyPlanId): Promise<DailyPlan | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "SELECT id, user_id, date, created_at, updated_at FROM daily_plans WHERE id = ?"
    );
    const row = stmt.get(id) as DailyPlanRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapDailyPlan(row) : null;
  }

  async findDailyPlanByUserAndDate(userId: string, date: string): Promise<DailyPlan | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "SELECT id, user_id, date, created_at, updated_at FROM daily_plans WHERE user_id = ? AND date = ?"
    );
    const row = stmt.get(userId, date) as DailyPlanRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapDailyPlan(row) : null;
  }

  async updateDailyPlan(
    id: DailyPlanId,
    patch: Partial<Pick<DailyPlan, "date" | "updatedAt">>
  ): Promise<DailyPlan> {
    const existing = await this.getDailyPlan(id);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Daily Plan not found: ${id}`);
    }

    const date = patch.date ?? existing.date;
    const updatedAt = patch.updatedAt ?? new Date().toISOString();

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "UPDATE daily_plans SET date = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?"
    );
    stmt.run(date, updatedAt, id);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      userId: existing.userId,
      date,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt
    };
  }

  async listSchedulesByDailyPlan(dailyPlanId: DailyPlanId): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, action_id, daily_plan_id, start_at, end_at, created_at, updated_at
       FROM schedules WHERE daily_plan_id = ? ORDER BY start_at ASC`
    );
    const rows = stmt.all(dailyPlanId) as unknown as ScheduleRow[];
    return rows.map(mapSchedule);
  }

  async createSchedule(input: Omit<ScheduleEntry, "id">): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO schedules (id, action_id, daily_plan_id, start_at, end_at, created_at, updated_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
    );
    stmt.run(
      id,
      input.actionId,
      input.dailyPlanId,
      input.startAt,
      input.endAt,
      input.createdAt,
      input.updatedAt
    );

    return {
      id,
      actionId: input.actionId,
      dailyPlanId: input.dailyPlanId,
      startAt: input.startAt,
      endAt: input.endAt,
      createdAt: input.createdAt,
      updatedAt: input.updatedAt
    };
  }

  async getSchedule(id: ScheduleId): Promise<ScheduleEntry | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, action_id, daily_plan_id, start_at, end_at, created_at, updated_at
       FROM schedules WHERE id = ?`
    );
    const row = stmt.get(id) as ScheduleRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapSchedule(row) : null;
  }

  async updateSchedule(
    id: ScheduleId,
    patch: Partial<Pick<ScheduleEntry, "startAt" | "endAt" | "dailyPlanId" | "updatedAt">>
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    const existing = await this.getSchedule(id);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Schedule not found: ${id}`);
    }

    const startAt = patch.startAt ?? existing.startAt;
    const endAt = patch.endAt ?? existing.endAt;
    const dailyPlanId = patch.dailyPlanId === undefined ? existing.dailyPlanId : patch.dailyPlanId;
    const updatedAt = patch.updatedAt ?? new Date().toISOString();

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `UPDATE schedules SET start_at = ?, end_at = ?, daily_plan_id = ?, updated_at = ?
       WHERE id = ?`
    );
    stmt.run(startAt, endAt, dailyPlanId, updatedAt, id);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      actionId: existing.actionId,
      dailyPlanId,
      startAt,
      endAt,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt
    };
  }

  async deleteSchedule(id: ScheduleId): Promise<void> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare("DELETE FROM schedules WHERE id = ?");
    stmt.run(id);
  }

  async listSchedulesByDateRange(
    userId: string,
    range: PlanningDateRange,
    workspaceId?: string
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]> {
    const startRange = range.startDate.includes("T") ? range.startDate : `${range.startDate}T00:00:00.000Z`;
    const endRange = range.endDate.includes("T") ? range.endDate : `${range.endDate}T23:59:59.999Z`;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT DISTINCT s.id, s.action_id, s.daily_plan_id, s.start_at, s.end_at, s.created_at, s.updated_at
       FROM schedules s
       JOIN actions a ON s.action_id = a.id
       JOIN goals g ON a.goal_id = g.id
       JOIN workspaces w ON g.workspace_id = w.id
       WHERE w.user_id = ?
         AND (? IS NULL OR w.id = ?)
         AND julianday(s.start_at) <= julianday(?)
         AND julianday(s.end_at) > julianday(?)
       ORDER BY s.start_at ASC`
    );
    const rows = stmt.all(userId, workspaceId ?? null, workspaceId ?? null, endRange, startRange) as unknown as ScheduleRow[];
    return rows.map(mapSchedule);
  }

  async findOverlappingSchedules(
    userId: string,
    startAt: string,
    endAt: string,
    excludeScheduleId?: ScheduleId,
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT DISTINCT s.id, s.action_id, s.daily_plan_id, s.start_at, s.end_at, s.created_at, s.updated_at
       FROM schedules s
       JOIN actions a ON s.action_id = a.id
       JOIN goals g ON a.goal_id = g.id
       JOIN workspaces w ON g.workspace_id = w.id
       WHERE w.user_id = ?
         AND julianday(s.start_at) < julianday(?)
         AND julianday(s.end_at) > julianday(?)
         AND (? IS NULL OR s.id <> ?)
       ORDER BY s.start_at ASC`
    );
    const rows = stmt.all(userId, endAt, startAt, excludeScheduleId ?? null, excludeScheduleId ?? null) as unknown as ScheduleRow[];
    return rows.map(mapSchedule);
  }
}

FILE: backend\src\infrastructure\repositories\sqlite-workspace.repository.ts
============================================================
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../database/sqlite.ts";
import type {
  CreateProjectInput,
  Project,
  UpdateProjectInput,
  Workspace
} from "../../modules/workspace/workspace.types.ts";
import type { WorkspaceRepository } from "../../modules/workspace/workspace.repository.ts";

interface WorkspaceRow {
  id: string;
  user_id: string;
  name: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

interface ProjectRow {
  id: string;
  workspace_id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string | null;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

function mapWorkspace(row: WorkspaceRow): Workspace {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    userId: row.user_id,
    name: row.name,
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at
  };
}

function mapProject(row: ProjectRow): Project {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    workspaceId: row.workspace_id,
    name: row.name,
    description: row.description,
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at
  };
}

export class SqliteWorkspaceRepository implements WorkspaceRepository {
  private readonly connection: DatabaseConnection;

  constructor(connection: DatabaseConnection) {
    this.connection = connection;
  }

  async getOrCreatePersonalWorkspace(userId: string): Promise<Workspace> {
    return this.connection.runInTransaction(() => {
      const selectStmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
        "SELECT id, user_id, name, created_at, updated_at FROM workspaces WHERE user_id = ?"
      );
      const existing = selectStmt.get(userId) as WorkspaceRow | undefined;
      if (existing) {
        return mapWorkspace(existing);
      }

      const id = randomUUID();
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const insertStmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
        "INSERT INTO workspaces (id, user_id, name, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"
      );

      try {
        insertStmt.run(id, userId, "Personal Workspace", now, now);
        return {
          id,
          userId,
          name: "Personal Workspace",
          createdAt: now,
          updatedAt: now
        };
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        // Handle race condition where another request created it
        const retry = selectStmt.get(userId) as WorkspaceRow | undefined;
        if (retry) {
          return mapWorkspace(retry);
        }
        throw err;
      }
    });
  }

  async findWorkspaceByUserId(userId: string): Promise<Workspace | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "SELECT id, user_id, name, created_at, updated_at FROM workspaces WHERE user_id = ? ORDER BY created_at ASC, id ASC LIMIT 1"
    );
    const row = stmt.get(userId) as WorkspaceRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapWorkspace(row) : null;
  }

  async listWorkspacesByUserId(userId: string): Promise<Workspace[]> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "SELECT id, user_id, name, created_at, updated_at FROM workspaces WHERE user_id = ? ORDER BY created_at ASC, id ASC"
    );
    return (stmt.all(userId) as unknown as WorkspaceRow[]).map(mapWorkspace);
  }

  async createWorkspace(userId: string, name: string): Promise<Workspace> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    this.connection.db.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO workspaces (id, user_id, name, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"
    ).run(id, userId, name.trim(), now, now);
    return { id, userId, name: name.trim(), createdAt: now, updatedAt: now };
  }

  async deleteWorkspace(workspaceId: string, userId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const result = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "DELETE FROM workspaces WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?"
    ).run(workspaceId, userId);
    return Number(result.changes) > 0;
  }

  async findWorkspaceById(workspaceId: string): Promise<Workspace | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "SELECT id, user_id, name, created_at, updated_at FROM workspaces WHERE id = ?"
    );
    const row = stmt.get(workspaceId) as WorkspaceRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapWorkspace(row) : null;
  }

  async createProject(workspaceId: string, input: CreateProjectInput): Promise<Project> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const description = input.description?.trim() ? input.description.trim() : null;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO projects (id, workspace_id, name, description, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"
    );
    stmt.run(id, workspaceId, input.name.trim(), description, now, now);

    return {
      id,
      workspaceId,
      name: input.name.trim(),
      description,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now
    };
  }

  async findProjectById(projectId: string): Promise<Project | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "SELECT id, workspace_id, name, description, created_at, updated_at FROM projects WHERE id = ?"
    );
    const row = stmt.get(projectId) as ProjectRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapProject(row) : null;
  }

  async listProjectsByWorkspaceId(workspaceId: string): Promise<Project[]> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "SELECT id, workspace_id, name, description, created_at, updated_at FROM projects WHERE workspace_id = ? ORDER BY created_at ASC"
    );
    const rows = stmt.all(workspaceId) as unknown as ProjectRow[];
    return rows.map(mapProject);
  }

  async updateProject(projectId: string, input: UpdateProjectInput): Promise<Project> {
    const existing = await this.findProjectById(projectId);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Project not found: ${projectId}`);
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const name = input.name !== undefined ? input.name.trim() : existing.name;
    const description =
      input.description !== undefined
        ? input.description === null || input.description.trim() === ""
          ? null
          : input.description.trim()
        : existing.description;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "UPDATE projects SET name = ?, description = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?"
    );
    stmt.run(name, description, now, projectId);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      workspaceId: existing.workspaceId,
      name,
      description,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now
    };
  }
}

export function createActionError(code: ActionErrorCode) {

import type {
  Action,
  ActionExecutionData,
  ActionStatus,
  CreateActionInput,
  RecordExecutionInput,
  UpdateActionInput
} from "./action.types";

export interface ActionRepository {
  create(input: {
    goalId: string;
    milestoneId: string | null;
    title: string;
    description: string | null;
    status: "TODO";
  }): Promise<Action>;
  findById(actionId: string): Promise<Action | null>;
  listByGoal(goalId: string): Promise<Action[]>;
  listByMilestone(milestoneId: string): Promise<Action[]>;
  update(actionId: string, input: UpdateActionInput): Promise<Action>;
  updateStatus(
    actionId: string,
    status: ActionStatus,
    completedAt: string | null
  ): Promise<Action>;
  saveExecution(
    actionId: string,
    input: RecordExecutionInput
  ): Promise<ActionExecutionData>;
  getExecution(actionId: string): Promise<ActionExecutionData | null>;
}

/** Adapter boundary to B03. Implement using B03's public application service. */
export interface ActionGoalAccess {
  validateGoal(user: { userId: string; email: string | null; isAuthenticated: true },
    goalId: string): Promise<
      | { success: true; data: { id: string } }
      | { success: false; error: { code: string; message: string } }
    >;
}

/** Adapter boundary to B04. No direct access to B04 storage is allowed. */
export interface ActionMilestoneAccess {
  validateMilestone(user: { userId: string; email: string | null; isAuthenticated: true },
    milestoneId: string, goalId: string): Promise<
      | { success: true; data: { id: string; goalId: string } }
      | { success: false; error: { code: string; message: string } }
    >;
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\modules\action\action.service.ts
============================================================
import { createActionError } from "./action.errors";
import type {
  Action,
  ActionErrorCode,
  ActionResult,
  ActionStatus,
  CreateActionInput,
  RecordExecutionInput,
  UpdateActionInput,
  ActionExecutionData
} from "./action.types";
import type {
  ActionGoalAccess,
  ActionMilestoneAccess,
  ActionRepository
} from "./action.repository";
import {
  validateCreateActionInput,
  validateExecutionInput,
  validateUpdateActionInput
} from "./action.validation";

export interface ActionUser {
  userId: string;
  email: string | null;
  isAuthenticated: true;
}

function ok<T>(data: T): ActionResult<T> {
  return { success: true, data };
}
function fail<T>(code: ActionErrorCode): ActionResult<T> {
  return { success: false, error: createActionError(code) };
}
function authenticated(user: ActionUser | null | undefined): user is ActionUser {
  return Boolean(user?.isAuthenticated && user.userId?.trim());
}

export interface ActionService {
  createAction(user: ActionUser, input: CreateActionInput): Promise<ActionResult<Action>>;
  getAction(user: ActionUser, actionId: string): Promise<ActionResult<Action>>;
  listActionsByGoal(user: ActionUser, goalId: string): Promise<ActionResult<Action[]>>;
  listActionsByMilestone(user: ActionUser, milestoneId: string): Promise<ActionResult<Action[]>>;
  updateAction(user: ActionUser, actionId: string, input: UpdateActionInput): Promise<ActionResult<Action>>;
  updateActionStatus(user: ActionUser, actionId: string, status: ActionStatus): Promise<ActionResult<Action>>;
  completeAction(user: ActionUser, actionId: string, input?: { executionNote?: string }): Promise<ActionResult<Action>>;
  recordExecution(user: ActionUser, actionId: string, input: RecordExecutionInput): Promise<ActionResult<ActionExecutionData>>;
  getExecutionData(user: ActionUser, actionId: string): Promise<ActionResult<ActionExecutionData | null>>;
}

export function createActionService(
  repository: ActionRepository,
  goalAccess: ActionGoalAccess,
  milestoneAccess: ActionMilestoneAccess,
  now: () => string = () => new Date().toISOString()
): ActionService {
  async function checkGoal(user: ActionUser, goalId: string): Promise<ActionResult<true>> {
    try {
      const result = await goalAccess.validateGoal(user, goalId);
      if (result.success) return ok(true);
      return fail(result.error.code.includes("NOT_FOUND")
        ? "ACTION_GOAL_NOT_FOUND" : "ACTION_GOAL_ACCESS_DENIED");
    } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
  }

  async function checkMilestone(
    user: ActionUser, milestoneId: string, goalId: string
  ): Promise<ActionResult<true>> {
    try {
      const result = await milestoneAccess.validateMilestone(user, milestoneId, goalId);
      if (result.success) {
        if (result.data.goalId !== goalId) return fail("ACTION_INVALID_ASSOCIATION");
        return ok(true);
      }
      return fail(result.error.code.includes("NOT_FOUND")
        ? "ACTION_MILESTONE_NOT_FOUND" : "ACTION_MILESTONE_ACCESS_DENIED");
    } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
  }

  async function resolve(user: ActionUser, actionId: string): Promise<ActionResult<Action>> {
    if (!authenticated(user)) return fail("ACTION_UNAUTHENTICATED");
    if (!actionId?.trim()) return fail("ACTION_NOT_FOUND");
    try {
      const action = await repository.findById(actionId);
      if (!action) return fail("ACTION_NOT_FOUND");
      const goal = await checkGoal(user, action.goalId);
      if (!goal.success) return goal;
      if (action.milestoneId) {
        const milestone = await checkMilestone(user, action.milestoneId, action.goalId);
        if (!milestone.success) return milestone;
      }
      return ok(action);
    } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
  }

  return {
    async createAction(user, input) {
      if (!authenticated(user)) return fail("ACTION_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      const validation = validateCreateActionInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) return fail(validation.code);
      const goal = await checkGoal(user, input.goalId);
      if (!goal.success) return goal;
      if (input.milestoneId) {
        const milestone = await checkMilestone(user, input.milestoneId, input.goalId);
        if (!milestone.success) return milestone;
      }
      try {
        const created = await repository.create({
          goalId: input.goalId,
          milestoneId: input.milestoneId ?? null,
          title: input.title.trim(),
          description: input.description?.trim() || null,
          status: "TODO"
        });
        if (created.goalId !== input.goalId ||
            created.milestoneId !== (input.milestoneId ?? null)) {
          return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
        }
        return ok(created);
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    },

    async getAction(user, actionId) {
      return resolve(user, actionId);
    },

    async listActionsByGoal(user, goalId) {
      if (!authenticated(user)) return fail("ACTION_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      const goal = await checkGoal(user, goalId);
      if (!goal.success) return goal;
      try {
        const actions = await repository.listByGoal(goalId);
        return ok(actions.filter(action => action.archivedAt === null));
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    },

    async listActionsByMilestone(user, milestoneId) {
      if (!authenticated(user)) return fail("ACTION_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      // Validate the Milestone through B04 first. Goal ID is obtained from B04.
      // This starter's B04 adapter must resolve the owning Goal in its contract.
      try {
        const actions = await repository.listByMilestone(milestoneId);
        const visible: Action[] = [];
        for (const action of actions) {
          if (action.milestoneId !== milestoneId || action.archivedAt !== null) continue;
          const resolved = await resolve(user, action.id);
          if (resolved.success) visible.push(action);
        }
        return ok(visible);
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    },

    async updateAction(user, actionId, input) {
      const existing = await resolve(user, actionId);
      if (!existing.success) return existing;
      const validation = validateUpdateActionInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) return fail(validation.code);
      if (existing.data.status === "COMPLETED") return fail("ACTION_UPDATE_NOT_ALLOWED");
      try {
        const updated = await repository.update(actionId, {
          ...(input.title !== undefined ? { title: input.title.trim() } : {}),
          ...(input.description !== undefined
            ? { description: input.description?.trim() || null } : {})
        });
        if (updated.id !== existing.data.id ||
            updated.goalId !== existing.data.goalId ||
            updated.milestoneId !== existing.data.milestoneId) {
          return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
        }
        return ok(updated);
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    },

    async updateActionStatus(user, actionId, status) {
      const existing = await resolve(user, actionId);
      if (!existing.success) return existing;
      if (!["TODO", "IN_PROGRESS", "COMPLETED"].includes(status)) {
        return fail("ACTION_INVALID_STATUS");
      }
      if (existing.data.status === "COMPLETED") return fail("ACTION_ALREADY_COMPLETED");
      const allowed: Record<ActionStatus, ActionStatus[]> = {
        TODO: ["IN_PROGRESS", "COMPLETED"],
        IN_PROGRESS: ["COMPLETED"],
        COMPLETED: []
      };
      if (!allowed[existing.data.status].includes(status)) {
        return fail("ACTION_INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION");
      }
      // Direct TODO -> COMPLETED remains a proposed transition; C05 must confirm it.
      try {
        const completedAt = status === "COMPLETED" ? now() : null;
        return ok(await repository.updateStatus(actionId, status, completedAt));
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    },

    async completeAction(user, actionId, input = {}) {
      const existing = await resolve(user, actionId);
      if (!existing.success) return existing;
      if (existing.data.status === "COMPLETED") return fail("ACTION_ALREADY_COMPLETED");
      try {
        if (input.executionNote !== undefined) {
          const saved = await repository.saveExecution(actionId, {
            executionNote: input.executionNote
          });
          void saved;
        }
        const completed = await repository.updateStatus(actionId, "COMPLETED", now());
        return ok(completed);
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    },

    async recordExecution(user, actionId, input) {
      const existing = await resolve(user, actionId);
      if (!existing.success) return existing;
      const validation = validateExecutionInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) return fail(validation.code);
      try {
        return ok(await repository.saveExecution(actionId, input));
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    },

    async getExecutionData(user, actionId) {
      const existing = await resolve(user, actionId);
      if (!existing.success) return existing;
      try {
        return ok(await repository.getExecution(actionId));
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    }
  };
}

FILE: backend\src\modules\action\action.validation.ts
============================================================
import type {
  CreateActionInput,
  RecordExecutionInput,
  UpdateActionInput
} from "./action.types";

const MAX_TITLE_LENGTH = 160;
const MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH = 5000;
const MAX_NOTE_LENGTH = 5000;

function validDate(value: unknown): boolean {
  return value === undefined || value === null ||
    (typeof value === "string" &&
      value.trim().length > 0 &&
      !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(value)));
}

export function validateCreateActionInput(input: CreateActionInput) {
  if (
    typeof input.title !== "string" ||
    input.title.trim().length === 0 ||
    input.title.trim().length > MAX_TITLE_LENGTH
  ) return { valid: false as const, code: "ACTION_INVALID_TITLE" as const };

  if (
    typeof input.goalId !== "string" ||
    input.goalId.trim().length === 0 ||
    (input.milestoneId !== undefined && input.milestoneId !== null &&
      (typeof input.milestoneId !== "string" || !input.milestoneId.trim())) ||
    (input.description !== undefined && input.description !== null &&
      (typeof input.description !== "string" ||
        input.description.length > MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH))
  ) return { valid: false as const, code: "ACTION_INVALID_INPUT" as const };

  return { valid: true as const };
}

export function validateUpdateActionInput(input: UpdateActionInput) {
  if (Object.keys(input).length === 0) {
    return { valid: false as const, code: "ACTION_INVALID_INPUT" as const };
  }
  if (input.title !== undefined &&
    (typeof input.title !== "string" ||
      !input.title.trim() ||
      input.title.trim().length > MAX_TITLE_LENGTH)) {
    return { valid: false as const, code: "ACTION_INVALID_TITLE" as const };
  }
  if (input.description !== undefined && input.description !== null &&
    (typeof input.description !== "string" ||
      input.description.length > MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH)) {
    return { valid: false as const, code: "ACTION_INVALID_INPUT" as const };
  }
  return { valid: true as const };
}

export function validateExecutionInput(input: RecordExecutionInput) {
  if (
    !validDate(input.actualStartedAt) ||
    !validDate(input.actualCompletedAt) ||
    (input.actualDurationMinutes !== undefined &&
      input.actualDurationMinutes !== null &&
      (!Number.isFinite(input.actualDurationMinutes) ||
        input.actualDurationMinutes < 0)) ||
    (input.executionNote !== undefined && input.executionNote !== null &&
      (typeof input.executionNote !== "string" ||
        input.executionNote.length > MAX_NOTE_LENGTH))
  ) return { valid: false as const, code: "ACTION_INVALID_EXECUTION_DATA" as const };

  if (
    input.actualStartedAt &&
    input.actualCompletedAt &&
    Date.parse(input.actualCompletedAt) < Date.parse(input.actualStartedAt)
  ) return { valid: false as const, code: "ACTION_INVALID_TIME_RANGE" as const };

  if (Object.keys(input).length === 0) {
    return { valid: false as const, code: "ACTION_INVALID_EXECUTION_DATA" as const };
  }
  return { valid: true as const };
}

import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../../infrastructure/database/sqlite.ts";
import type { ApplicationContainer } from "../../application/container.ts";
import type {
  AIContextReader, AIOutputValidator, AIProposalDomainApplier,
} from "./ai-orchestration.repository";
import type {
  AIOutputValidationResult, AIProposal, AIProposalApplicationResult,
  AIProposalSelection, AIOperationType, AIRequestContext,
} from "./ai-orchestration.types";
import { AIOrchestrationError } from "./ai-orchestration.errors";

type ProposalItem = {
  id: string; type: "GOAL" | "MILESTONE" | "ACTION" | "MEASUREMENT"; title: string;
  description: string; targetDate: string | null; parentItemId: string | null; rationale: string;
  valueType?: "NUMBER" | "TEXT" | "BOOLEAN" | null; unit?: string | null; targetValue?: number | string | boolean | null;
};
function record(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  return value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
    ? value as Record<string, unknown> : null;
}
function stringArray(value: unknown): value is string[] {
  return Array.isArray(value) && value.every((item) => typeof item === "string");
}

export class GoalDiscoveryContextReader implements AIContextReader {
  async buildContext(userId: string, operationType: AIOperationType, input: string): Promise<AIRequestContext> {
    return { userId, operationType, userInput: input, constraints: { contextProvided: false } };
  }
}

export class GoalDiscoveryOutputValidator implements AIOutputValidator {
  constructor(private readonly maxItems: number) {}

  async validate(operation: AIOperationType, output: unknown): Promise<AIOutputValidationResult> {
    const value = record(output);
    if (!value) return { valid: false, issues: [{ path: "$", code: "OBJECT_REQUIRED", message: "Expected an object." }] };
    if (operation === "GOAL_CLARIFICATION") {
      const questions = value.questions;
      const valid = typeof value.outcome === "string" && value.outcome.length <= 500
        && [value.successCriteria, value.knownFacts, value.assumptions, value.missingInformation].every(stringArray)
        && Array.isArray(questions) && questions.length <= 8 && questions.every((raw) => {
          const question = record(raw);
          return !!question && typeof question.id === "string" && typeof question.prompt === "string"
            && question.prompt.length <= 500 && typeof question.why === "string"
            && ["TEXT", "NUMBER", "DATE", "CHOICE"].includes(String(question.answerType))
            && typeof question.required === "boolean" && stringArray(question.options);
        });
      return { valid, value: valid ? { ...value, provenance: "NEEDS_CONFIRMATION" } : undefined, issues: valid ? [] : [{ path: "$", code: "CLARIFICATION_SHAPE", message: "Clarification output is incomplete." }] };
    }
    if (operation === "GOAL_FEASIBILITY") {
      const valid = ["FEASIBLE", "CONCERN", "INSUFFICIENT_INFORMATION"].includes(String(value.assessment))
        && typeof value.summary === "string"
        && [value.concerns, value.alternatives, value.assumptions, value.missingInformation].every(stringArray);
      return { valid, value: valid ? { ...value, provenance: "AI_PROPOSED" } : undefined, issues: valid ? [] : [{ path: "$", code: "FEASIBILITY_SHAPE", message: "Feasibility output is incomplete." }] };
    }
    if (operation !== "GOAL_TREE_PROPOSAL") {
      return { valid: false, issues: [{ path: "$", code: "UNSUPPORTED_OPERATION", message: "This workflow is not enabled." }] };
    }
    const items = value.items;
    if (typeof value.summary !== "string" || !Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0 || items.length > this.maxItems) {
      return { valid: false, issues: [{ path: "items", code: "ITEM_COUNT", message: "Proposal item count or summary is invalid." }] };
    }
    const byId = new Map<string, ProposalItem>();
    for (const raw of items) {
      const item = record(raw);
      if (!item || typeof item.id !== "string" || !item.id.trim() || byId.has(item.id)
        || !["GOAL", "MILESTONE", "ACTION", "MEASUREMENT"].includes(String(item.type))
        || typeof item.title !== "string" || !item.title.trim() || item.title.length > 160
        || typeof item.description !== "string" || item.description.length > 1200
        || typeof item.rationale !== "string" || item.rationale.length > 500
        || !(item.targetDate === null || (typeof item.targetDate === "string" && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(item.targetDate)))
        || !(item.parentItemId === null || typeof item.parentItemId === "string")) {
        return { valid: false, issues: [{ path: "items", code: "ITEM_INVALID", message: "Proposal contains an invalid or duplicate item." }] };
      }
      if (item.type === "MEASUREMENT") {
        if (!["NUMBER", "TEXT", "BOOLEAN"].includes(String(item.valueType))
          || !(item.unit === null || (typeof item.unit === "string" && item.unit.length <= 40))
          || !(item.targetValue === null || ["number", "string", "boolean"].includes(typeof item.targetValue))
          || (typeof item.targetValue === "number" && !Number.isFinite(item.targetValue))
          || (item.targetValue !== null && typeof item.targetValue !== "undefined" && typeof item.valueType !== typeof item.targetValue)
          || (item.valueType !== "NUMBER" && item.unit !== null)) {
          return { valid: false, issues: [{ path: `items.${item.id}`, code: "MEASUREMENT_SHAPE", message: "Measurement proposal fields are invalid." }] };
        }
      }
      byId.set(item.id, item as unknown as ProposalItem);
    }
    const roots = [...byId.values()].filter((item) => item.type === "GOAL" && item.parentItemId === null);
    if (roots.length !== 1) return { valid: false, issues: [{ path: "items", code: "ROOT_GOAL", message: "A proposal must contain one root Goal." }] };
    for (const item of byId.values()) {
      if (item.parentItemId === null) {
        if (item.type !== "GOAL") return { valid: false, issues: [{ path: `items.${item.id}`, code: "PARENT_REQUIRED", message: "Milestones and Actions need a parent." }] };
        continue;
      }
      const parent = byId.get(item.parentItemId);
      if (!parent || (item.type === "GOAL" && parent.type !== "GOAL")
        || (item.type === "MILESTONE" && parent.type !== "GOAL")
        || (item.type === "MEASUREMENT" && parent.type !== "GOAL")
        || (item.type === "ACTION" && !["GOAL", "MILESTONE"].includes(parent.type))) {
        return { valid: false, issues: [{ path: `items.${item.id}.parentItemId`, code: "PARENT_INVALID", message: "Proposal references an invalid parent." }] };
      }
    }
    for (const item of byId.values()) {
      const seen = new Set<string>([item.id]);
      let parentId = item.parentItemId;
      while (parentId) {
        if (seen.has(parentId)) return { valid: false, issues: [{ path: `items.${item.id}.parentItemId`, code: "DEPENDENCY_CYCLE", message: "Proposal contains a hierarchy cycle." }] };
        seen.add(parentId);
        parentId = byId.get(parentId)?.parentItemId ?? null;
      }
    }
    return { valid: true, value: { ...value, items: [...byId.values()], provenance: "AI_PROPOSED" }, issues: [] };
  }
}

export class GoalProposalDomainApplier implements AIProposalDomainApplier {
  constructor(private readonly app: Pick<ApplicationContainer, "goalService" | "milestoneService" | "actionService" | "measurementService">) {}

  async validateProposal(_userId: string, proposal: AIProposal, selection: AIProposalSelection): Promise<void> {
    const rawData = selection.editedData ?? proposal.proposalData;
    const validation = await new GoalDiscoveryOutputValidator(18).validate("GOAL_TREE_PROPOSAL", rawData);
    if (!validation.valid) throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_VALIDATION_FAILED", "Edited proposal does not pass validation.");
    const data = record(rawData);
    const all = data?.items;
    if (!Array.isArray(all) || !selection.selectedItemIds.length) throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_APPROVAL_REQUIRED", "Select at least one proposal item.");
    const items = all.map((item) => record(item) as unknown as ProposalItem);
    const chosen = new Set(selection.selectedItemIds);
    const previouslyApplied = new Set(((proposal.proposalData as { applicationResults?: { operation: string; success: boolean }[] }).applicationResults ?? []).filter((item) => item.success).map((item) => item.operation));
    const itemIds = new Set(items.map((item) => item.id));
    const byId = new Map(items.map((item) => [item.id, item]));
    const missingDependency = selection.selectedItemIds.some((id) => {
      let parentId = byId.get(id)?.parentItemId ?? null;
      while (parentId) {
        if (!chosen.has(parentId) && !previouslyApplied.has(parentId)) return true;
        parentId = byId.get(parentId)?.parentItemId ?? null;
      }
      return false;
    });
    if (chosen.size !== selection.selectedItemIds.length || selection.selectedItemIds.some((id) => !itemIds.has(id)) || missingDependency) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_VALIDATION_FAILED", "Selected items must be unique and include their required parents.");
    }
    if (items.some((item) => chosen.has(item.id) && !["GOAL", "MILESTONE", "ACTION", "MEASUREMENT"].includes(item.type))) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_VALIDATION_FAILED", "Proposal contains an unsupported item.");
    }
  }

  async applyApprovedProposal(userId: string, proposal: AIProposal, selection: AIProposalSelection): Promise<AIProposalApplicationResult> {
    const data = record(selection.editedData ?? proposal.proposalData)!;
    const items = (data.items as ProposalItem[]).filter((item) => selection.selectedItemIds.includes(item.id));
    const user = { userId, email: null, isAuthenticated: true as const };
    const ids = new Map<string, { type: ProposalItem["type"]; id: string }>();
    const processed = new Set<string>();
    for (const result of ((proposal.proposalData as { applicationResults?: { operation: string; success: boolean; entityId?: string }[] }).applicationResults ?? [])) {
      const original = (data.items as ProposalItem[]).find((item) => item.id === result.operation);
      if (result.success && original && result.entityId) ids.set(result.operation, { type: original.type, id: result.entityId });
    }
    const operations: AIProposalApplicationResult["operations"] = [];
    const remaining = [...items];
    while (remaining.length) {
      const index = remaining.findIndex((item) => item.parentItemId === null || ids.has(item.parentItemId) || processed.has(item.parentItemId));
      if (index < 0) throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_VALIDATION_FAILED", "Selected proposal items contain a dependency cycle.");
      const [item] = remaining.splice(index, 1);
      processed.add(item.id);
      const parent = item.parentItemId ? ids.get(item.parentItemId) : undefined;
      try {
        if (item.type === "GOAL") {
          if (item.parentItemId && parent?.type !== "GOAL") throw new Error("GOAL_PARENT_NOT_APPLIED");
          const result = parent
            ? await this.app.goalService.addChildGoal(user, parent.id, { title: item.title, description: item.description, targetDate: item.targetDate })
            : await this.app.goalService.createGoal(user, { title: item.title, description: item.description, targetDate: item.targetDate });
          if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.error.code);
          ids.set(item.id, { type: "GOAL", id: result.data.id });
          operations.push({ operation: item.id, success: true, entityId: result.data.id });
        } else if (item.type === "MILESTONE") {
          if (parent?.type !== "GOAL") throw new Error("MILESTONE_GOAL_NOT_APPLIED");
          const result = await this.app.milestoneService.createMilestone(user, { goalId: parent.id, title: item.title, description: item.description, targetDate: item.targetDate });
          if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.error.code);
          ids.set(item.id, { type: "MILESTONE", id: result.data.id });
          operations.push({ operation: item.id, success: true, entityId: result.data.id });
        } else if (item.type === "ACTION") {
          const goalParent = parent?.type === "GOAL" ? parent : item.parentItemId ? ids.get(items.find((candidate) => candidate.id === item.parentItemId)?.parentItemId ?? "") : undefined;
          if (goalParent?.type !== "GOAL") throw new Error("ACTION_GOAL_PARENT_REQUIRED");
          const milestoneId = parent?.type === "MILESTONE" ? parent.id : null;
          const result = await this.app.actionService.createAction(user, { goalId: goalParent.id, milestoneId, title: item.title, description: item.description });
          if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.error.code);
          ids.set(item.id, { type: "ACTION", id: result.data.id });
          operations.push({ operation: item.id, success: true, entityId: result.data.id });
        } else if (item.type === "MEASUREMENT") {
          if (parent?.type !== "GOAL") throw new Error("MEASUREMENT_GOAL_PARENT_REQUIRED");
          const result = await this.app.measurementService.createMeasurement(user, {
            goalId: parent.id, label: item.title, description: item.description,
            valueType: item.valueType!, unit: item.unit ?? null, targetValue: item.targetValue ?? null,
          });
          ids.set(item.id, { type: "MEASUREMENT", id: result.id });
          operations.push({ operation: item.id, success: true, entityId: result.id });
        }
      } catch (error) {
        operations.push({ operation: item.id, success: false, errorCode: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "APPLICATION_FAILED" });
      }
    }
    const successCount = operations.filter((operation) => operation.success).length;
    return { proposalId: proposal.id, status: successCount === operations.length ? "APPLIED" : successCount ? "PARTIALLY_APPLIED" : "FAILED", operations };
  }
}

export class AIUsageLimiter {
  constructor(private readonly connection: DatabaseConnection, private readonly perUserDaily = 12, private readonly globalDaily = 90) {}

  reserve(userId: string): boolean {
    const since = new Date(); since.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    const cutoff = since.toISOString();
    return this.connection.runInTransaction(() => {
      const retentionCutoff = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
      this.connection.db.prepare("DELETE FROM ai_usage WHERE created_at < ?").run(retentionCutoff);
      const counts = this.connection.db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS total,
        SUM(CASE WHEN user_id = ? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS user_total
        FROM ai_usage WHERE created_at >= ?`).get(userId, cutoff) as { total: number; user_total: number | null };
      if (counts.total >= this.globalDaily || (counts.user_total ?? 0) >= this.perUserDaily) return false;
      this.connection.db.prepare("INSERT INTO ai_usage(user_id, created_at) VALUES (?, ?)").run(userId, new Date().toISOString());
      return true;
    });
  }
}

export type AIErrorCode =
  | "AI_UNAUTHENTICATED" | "AI_ACCESS_DENIED" | "AI_INVALID_REQUEST"
  | "AI_INVALID_OPERATION" | "AI_CONTEXT_NOT_FOUND" | "AI_CONTEXT_ACCESS_DENIED"
  | "AI_CONTEXT_INSUFFICIENT" | "AI_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE" | "AI_PROVIDER_AUTH_FAILED"
  | "AI_PROVIDER_RATE_LIMITED" | "AI_USAGE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED" | "AI_REQUEST_TIMEOUT"
  | "AI_PROVIDER_ERROR" | "AI_EMPTY_RESPONSE" | "AI_OUTPUT_INVALID"
  | "AI_OUTPUT_TRUNCATED" | "AI_OUTPUT_SEMANTIC_INVALID" | "AI_PROPOSAL_NOT_FOUND"
  | "AI_PROPOSAL_ACCESS_DENIED" | "AI_PROPOSAL_INVALID_STATE" | "AI_APPROVAL_REQUIRED"
  | "AI_PROPOSAL_VALIDATION_FAILED" | "AI_PROPOSAL_APPLICATION_FAILED"
  | "AI_PROPOSAL_ALREADY_APPLIED" | "AI_PERSISTENCE_ERROR" | "AI_UNKNOWN_ERROR";

export class AIOrchestrationError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly code: AIErrorCode,
    message: string,
    public readonly cause?: unknown,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "AIOrchestrationError";
  }
}

FILE: backend\src\modules\ai-orchestration\ai-orchestration.service.ts

import { AIOrchestrationError } from "./ai-orchestration.errors";
import type {
  AIUserContext, AIOperationType, AIProposal, AIProposalSelection,
  AIProposalApplicationResult, AIRequestContext, AIProviderRequest,
} from "./ai-orchestration.types";
import type {
  AIContextReader, AIOrchestrationRules, AIOutputValidator, AIProposalDomainApplier,
  AIProposalRepository, AIProvider,
  AIUsageReservation,
} from "./ai-orchestration.repository";
import { validateAIRequest, validateProviderResponse } from "./ai-orchestration.validation";

const PROPOSAL_OPERATIONS: Partial<Record<AIOperationType, "GOAL_TREE" | "DAILY_PLAN">> = {
  GOAL_TREE_PROPOSAL: "GOAL_TREE",
  DAILY_PLAN_PROPOSAL: "DAILY_PLAN",
};

export class AIOrchestrationService {
  constructor(
    private readonly contextReader: AIContextReader,
    private readonly provider: AIProvider,
    private readonly outputValidator: AIOutputValidator,
    private readonly proposals: AIProposalRepository,
    private readonly domainApplier: AIProposalDomainApplier,
    private readonly rules: AIOrchestrationRules,
    private readonly usage?: AIUsageReservation,
  ) {}

  async execute(
    user: AIUserContext,
    operation: AIOperationType,
    userInput: string,
    references?: unknown,
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    const context = await this.loadContext(user.userId, operation, userInput, references);
    if (this.usage && !this.usage.reserve(user.userId)) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_USAGE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED", "The daily AI usage limit has been reached. Continue manually or try again tomorrow.");
    }
    const providerRequest: AIProviderRequest = {
      operationType: operation,
      input: userInput,
      context: {
        goalContext: context.goalContext,
        projectContext: context.projectContext,
        actionContext: context.actionContext,
        measurementContext: context.measurementContext,
        planningContext: context.planningContext,
        progressContext: context.progressContext,
        constraints: context.constraints,
      },
      outputSchemaKey: operation,
    };

    let response;
    try {
      response = await this.provider.generate(providerRequest);
    } catch (error) {
      throw this.normalizeProviderError(error);
    }
    validateProviderResponse(response);

    const validation = await this.outputValidator.validate(operation, response.content);
    if (!validation.valid) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError(
        "AI_OUTPUT_SEMANTIC_INVALID",
        "AI output did not pass the configured structural and semantic validation.",
      );
    }

    const proposalType = PROPOSAL_OPERATIONS[operation];
    if (!proposalType) return validation.value;

    if (!this.rules.proposalPersistenceEnabled) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError(
        "AI_PERSISTENCE_ERROR",
        "Proposal persistence is disabled until its model and lifecycle are approved.",
      );
    }

    const now = this.rules.now();
    const proposal: AIProposal = {
      id: this.rules.createProposalId(),
      userId: user.userId,
      operationType: operation,
      proposalType,
      status: "PENDING_REVIEW",
      proposalData: validation.value,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
    };
    try {
      return await this.proposals.create(proposal);
    } catch (error) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PERSISTENCE_ERROR", "Unable to save AI proposal.", error);
    }
  }

  async getProposal(user: AIUserContext, proposalId: string): Promise<AIProposal> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    const proposal = await this.proposals.getById(proposalId);
    if (!proposal) throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_NOT_FOUND", "Proposal was not found.");
    if (proposal.userId !== user.userId) throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_ACCESS_DENIED", "Proposal access denied.");
    return proposal;
  }

  async updateProposal(
    user: AIUserContext,
    proposalId: string,
    proposalData: unknown,
  ): Promise<AIProposal> {
    const current = await this.getProposal(user, proposalId);
    if (!["PENDING_REVIEW", "EDITED"].includes(current.status)) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_INVALID_STATE", "Proposal cannot be edited in its current state.");
    }
    const updated: AIProposal = {
      ...current,
      proposalData,
      status: "EDITED",
      updatedAt: this.rules.now(),
    };
    try {
      return await this.proposals.update(updated, current.status);
    } catch (error) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PERSISTENCE_ERROR", "Unable to update proposal.", error);
    }
  }

  async rejectProposal(user: AIUserContext, proposalId: string): Promise<AIProposal> {
    const current = await this.getProposal(user, proposalId);
    if (!["PENDING_REVIEW", "EDITED", "PARTIALLY_APPROVED", "FAILED"].includes(current.status)) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_INVALID_STATE", "Proposal cannot be rejected in its current state.");
    }
    return this.saveProposal({ ...current, status: "REJECTED", updatedAt: this.rules.now() }, current.status);
  }

  async approveAndApplyProposal(
    user: AIUserContext,
    proposalId: string,
    selection: AIProposalSelection,
  ): Promise<AIProposalApplicationResult> {
    const current = await this.getProposal(user, proposalId);
    if (!["PENDING_REVIEW", "EDITED", "PARTIALLY_APPROVED", "FAILED"].includes(current.status)) {
      if (current.status === "APPLIED") throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_ALREADY_APPLIED", "Proposal has already been applied.");
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_INVALID_STATE", "Proposal is not eligible for approval.");
    }
    if (!selection || !Array.isArray(selection.selectedItemIds) || selection.selectedItemIds.length === 0) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_APPROVAL_REQUIRED", "An explicit item selection is required.");
    }

    const previous = current.proposalData as { applicationResults?: AIProposalApplicationResult["operations"] };
    const previouslyApplied = new Set((previous?.applicationResults ?? [])
      .filter((item) => item.success).map((item) => item.operation));
    if (selection.selectedItemIds.some((id) => previouslyApplied.has(id))) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_ALREADY_APPLIED", "Previously applied proposal items cannot be applied twice.");
    }

    try {
      await this.domainApplier.validateProposal(user.userId, current, selection);
    } catch (error) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_VALIDATION_FAILED", "Approved proposal items failed domain validation.", error);
    }

    const claimed = await this.proposals.claimForApplication(current.id, user.userId, this.rules.now());
    if (!claimed) throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_INVALID_STATE", "Proposal application is already running or no longer available.");

    let result: AIProposalApplicationResult;
    try {
      result = await this.domainApplier.applyApprovedProposal(user.userId, current, selection);
    } catch (error) {
      await this.saveProposal({ ...current, status: previouslyApplied.size ? "PARTIALLY_APPROVED" : "FAILED", updatedAt: this.rules.now() }, "APPLYING");
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_APPLICATION_FAILED", "Applying approved proposal failed.", error);
    }
    const combined = [...(previous?.applicationResults ?? []), ...result.operations];
    const allItemIds = Array.isArray((current.proposalData as { items?: unknown[] })?.items)
      ? (current.proposalData as { items: { id: string }[] }).items.map((item) => item.id) : [];
    const complete = allItemIds.length > 0 && allItemIds.every((id) => combined.some((item) => item.operation === id && item.success));
    const hasSuccess = combined.some((item) => item.success);
    const nextStatus = complete ? "APPLIED" : hasSuccess ? "PARTIALLY_APPROVED" : "FAILED";
    await this.saveProposal({
      ...current, status: nextStatus,
      proposalData: { ...(current.proposalData as Record<string, unknown>), applicationResults: combined },
      updatedAt: this.rules.now(),
    }, "APPLYING");
    return { ...result, status: complete ? "APPLIED" : hasSuccess ? "PARTIALLY_APPLIED" : "FAILED", operations: combined };
  }

  private async loadContext(
    userId: string,
    operation: AIOperationType,
    userInput: string,
    references?: unknown,
  ): Promise<AIRequestContext> {
    try {
      const context = await this.contextReader.buildContext(userId, operation, userInput, references);
      validateAIRequest(context, this.rules.validation);
      if (context.userId !== userId || context.operationType !== operation) {
        throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_CONTEXT_ACCESS_DENIED", "Context does not match the authenticated user or operation.");
      }
      return context;
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof AIOrchestrationError) throw error;
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_CONTEXT_NOT_FOUND", "Unable to build authorized AI context.", error);
    }
  }

  private async saveProposal(proposal: AIProposal, expectedStatus?: AIProposal["status"]): Promise<AIProposal> {
    try { return await this.proposals.update(proposal, expectedStatus); }
    catch (error) { throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PERSISTENCE_ERROR", "Unable to update proposal state.", error); }
  }

  private assertAuthenticated(user: AIUserContext): void {
    if (!user?.isAuthenticated || !user.userId) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_UNAUTHENTICATED", "Authentication is required.");
    }
  }

  private normalizeProviderError(error: unknown): AIOrchestrationError {
    if (error instanceof AIOrchestrationError) return error;
    const candidate = error as { code?: string; status?: number; name?: string };
    if (candidate?.code === "TIMEOUT" || candidate?.name === "TimeoutError") {
      return new AIOrchestrationError("AI_REQUEST_TIMEOUT", "AI provider request timed out.", error);
    }
    if (candidate?.status === 429) {
      return new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROVIDER_RATE_LIMITED", "AI provider rate limit reached.", error);
    }
    if (candidate?.status === 401 || candidate?.status === 403) {
      return new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROVIDER_AUTH_FAILED", "AI provider authentication failed.", error);
    }
    if (candidate?.status === 503 || candidate?.status === 502) {
      return new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE", "AI provider is unavailable.", error);
    }
    return new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROVIDER_ERROR", "AI provider request failed.", error);
  }
}

export type AIUserContext = { userId: string; isAuthenticated: true };

export type AIOperationType =
  | "GOAL_CLARIFICATION"
  | "GOAL_FEASIBILITY"
  | "GOAL_TREE_PROPOSAL"
  | "DAILY_PLAN_PROPOSAL";

export type AIRequestContext = {
  userId: string;
  operationType: AIOperationType;
  userInput: string;
  goalContext?: unknown;
  projectContext?: unknown;
  actionContext?: unknown;
  measurementContext?: unknown;
  planningContext?: unknown;
  progressContext?: unknown;
  constraints?: unknown;
};

export type AIProviderRequest = {
  operationType: AIOperationType;
  input: string;
  context: Omit<AIRequestContext, "userId" | "userInput" | "operationType">;
  outputSchemaKey: string;
};

export type AIProviderResponse = {
  content: unknown;
  finishReason?: string;
  truncated?: boolean;
  providerRequestId?: string;
};

export type AIProposalType = "GOAL_TREE" | "DAILY_PLAN";
export type AIProposalStatus =
  | "PENDING_REVIEW"
  | "EDITED"
  | "APPROVED"
  | "APPLYING"
  | "PARTIALLY_APPROVED"
  | "REJECTED"
  | "APPLIED"
  | "FAILED"
  | "EXPIRED";

  id: string;
  userId: string;
  operationType: AIOperationType;
  proposalType: AIProposalType;
  status: AIProposalStatus;
  proposalData: unknown;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
};

  path: string;
  code: string;
  message: string;
};

export type AIOutputValidationResult<T = unknown> = {
  valid: boolean;
  value?: T;
  issues: AIValidationIssue[];
};

export type AIProposalSelection = {
  selectedItemIds: string[];
  editedData?: unknown;
};

export type AIApplicationOperationResult = {
  operation: string;
  success: boolean;
  entityId?: string;
  errorCode?: string;
};

export type AIProposalApplicationResult = {
  proposalId: string;
  status: "APPLIED" | "PARTIALLY_APPLIED" | "FAILED";
  operations: AIApplicationOperationResult[];
};

FILE: backend\src\modules\ai-orchestration\ai-orchestration.validation.ts
============================================================
import { AIOrchestrationError } from "./ai-orchestration.errors";
import type { AIRequestContext, AIOperationType, AIProviderResponse } from "./ai-orchestration.types";

const OPERATIONS: AIOperationType[] = [
  "GOAL_CLARIFICATION", "GOAL_FEASIBILITY", "GOAL_TREE_PROPOSAL", "DAILY_PLAN_PROPOSAL",
];

export type AIValidationRules = {
  maxInputLength: number;
  maxContextBytes: number;
  maxProposalItems: number;
};

export function validateAIRequest(
  input: AIRequestContext,
  rules: AIValidationRules,
): void {
  if (!input || typeof input !== "object" || !input.userId || !input.userInput?.trim()) {
    throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_INVALID_REQUEST", "A valid authenticated request and user input are required.");
  }
  if (!OPERATIONS.includes(input.operationType)) {
    throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_INVALID_OPERATION", "Unsupported AI operation.");
  }
  if (input.userInput.trim().length > rules.maxInputLength) {
    throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_INVALID_REQUEST", "AI input exceeds the configured limit.");
  }
  let bytes: number;
  try {
    bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify({
      goalContext: input.goalContext, projectContext: input.projectContext,
      actionContext: input.actionContext, measurementContext: input.measurementContext,
      planningContext: input.planningContext, progressContext: input.progressContext,
      constraints: input.constraints,
    })).length;
  } catch {
    throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_INVALID_REQUEST", "AI context must be serializable.");
  }
  if (bytes > rules.maxContextBytes) {
    throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_INVALID_REQUEST", "AI context exceeds the configured limit.");
  }
}

export function validateProviderResponse(response: AIProviderResponse): void {
  if (!response || response.content === null || response.content === undefined || response.content === "") {
    throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_EMPTY_RESPONSE", "The AI provider returned no usable content.");
  }
  if (response.truncated || response.finishReason === "length") {
    throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_OUTPUT_TRUNCATED", "The AI provider response was truncated.");
  }
}

export function validateProposalItemCount(
  items: unknown[],
  rules: AIValidationRules,
): void {
  if (items.length > rules.maxProposalItems) {
    throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_OUTPUT_INVALID", "Proposal contains too many items.");
  }
}

FILE: backend\src\modules\ai-orchestration\gemini.provider.ts
