import { GoogleGenAI, type GenerateContentResponse } from "@google/genai";
import type { AIProvider } from "./ai-orchestration.repository";
import type { AIProviderRequest, AIProviderResponse } from "./ai-orchestration.types";
import { AIOrchestrationError } from "./ai-orchestration.errors";

const CLARIFICATION_SCHEMA = {
  type: "object",
  properties: {
    outcome: { type: "string" },
    successCriteria: { type: "array", items: { type: "string" } },
    knownFacts: { type: "array", items: { type: "string" } },
    assumptions: { type: "array", items: { type: "string" } },
    missingInformation: { type: "array", items: { type: "string" } },
    questions: { type: "array", items: { type: "object", properties: {
      id: { type: "string" }, prompt: { type: "string" }, why: { type: "string" },
      answerType: { type: "string", enum: ["TEXT", "NUMBER", "DATE", "CHOICE"] },
      required: { type: "boolean" }, options: { type: "array", items: { type: "string" } },
    }, required: ["id", "prompt", "why", "answerType", "required", "options"] } },
  }, required: ["outcome", "successCriteria", "knownFacts", "assumptions", "missingInformation", "questions"],
} as const;

const FEASIBILITY_SCHEMA = {
  type: "object",
  properties: {
    assessment: { type: "string", enum: ["FEASIBLE", "CONCERN", "INSUFFICIENT_INFORMATION"] },
    summary: { type: "string" },
    concerns: { type: "array", items: { type: "string" } },
    alternatives: { type: "array", items: { type: "string" } },
    assumptions: { type: "array", items: { type: "string" } },
    missingInformation: { type: "array", items: { type: "string" } },
  }, required: ["assessment", "summary", "concerns", "alternatives", "assumptions", "missingInformation"],
} as const;

const PROPOSAL_SCHEMA = {
  type: "object",
  properties: {
    summary: { type: "string" },
    items: { type: "array", items: { type: "object", properties: {
      id: { type: "string" }, type: { type: "string", enum: ["GOAL", "MILESTONE", "ACTION", "MEASUREMENT"] },
      title: { type: "string" }, description: { type: "string" }, targetDate: { type: ["string", "null"] },
      parentItemId: { type: ["string", "null"] }, rationale: { type: "string" },
      valueType: { type: ["string", "null"], enum: ["NUMBER", "TEXT", "BOOLEAN", null] },
      unit: { type: ["string", "null"] }, targetValue: { type: ["number", "string", "boolean", "null"] },
    }, required: ["id", "type", "title", "description", "targetDate", "parentItemId", "rationale"] } },
  }, required: ["summary", "items"],
} as const;

const SCHEMAS = {
  GOAL_CLARIFICATION: CLARIFICATION_SCHEMA,
  GOAL_FEASIBILITY: FEASIBILITY_SCHEMA,
  GOAL_TREE_PROPOSAL: PROPOSAL_SCHEMA,
  DAILY_PLAN_PROPOSAL: PROPOSAL_SCHEMA,
} as const;

const INSTRUCTIONS: Record<AIProviderRequest["operationType"], string> = {
  GOAL_CLARIFICATION: "Understand the user's goal. Separate explicit facts from assumptions. Ask only questions whose answers may change the plan. Do not invent facts. Return concise JSON matching the schema.",
  GOAL_FEASIBILITY: "Assess feasibility using only the supplied information. State uncertainty and missing prerequisites. Do not replace the user's intended outcome or invent resources, dates, or commitments.",
  GOAL_TREE_PROPOSAL: "Propose a small useful structure using Goals, Milestones, Actions, and only when a measurable outcome is useful, a Measurement. Use flexible depth and no arbitrary minimum item count. Include a single root GOAL. IDs must be unique strings; parentItemId must reference another proposed item or be null. A Measurement must be a child of a Goal, use NUMBER, TEXT, or BOOLEAN, and include a target only when explicitly supported by the input; otherwise set targetValue to null. Do not invent dates, resources, or facts. All content is proposed, not confirmed.",
  DAILY_PLAN_PROPOSAL: "Propose a bounded daily plan using only supplied actions, times, and commitments. Do not invent availability. Return only supported plan items.",
};

export class GeminiAIProvider implements AIProvider {
  private readonly client: GoogleGenAI | null;
  private readonly model: string;

  constructor(apiKey = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY, model = process.env.GEMINI_MODEL || "gemini-3.8-flash", timeoutMs = 20_000) {
    this.client = apiKey ? new GoogleGenAI({ apiKey, httpOptions: { timeout: timeoutMs } }) : null;
    this.model = model;
  }

  async generate(request: AIProviderRequest): Promise<AIProviderResponse> {
    if (!this.client) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE", "AI assistance is unavailable. Add GEMINI_API_KEY to the backend environment or continue manually.");
    }
    const prompt = [
      INSTRUCTIONS[request.operationType],
      "Return only the requested structured result. Keep output concise.",
      `User input:\n${request.input}`,
      `Authorized context:\n${JSON.stringify(request.context)}`,
    ].join("\n\n");
    let response: GenerateContentResponse;
    try {
      response = await this.client.models.generateContent({
        model: this.model,
        contents: prompt,
        config: {
          responseMimeType: "application/json",
          responseJsonSchema: SCHEMAS[request.operationType],
          maxOutputTokens: 1800,
        },
      });
    } catch (error) {
      const candidate = error as { status?: number; code?: number; name?: string };
      if (candidate?.name === "TimeoutError" || candidate?.name === "AbortError") {
        throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_REQUEST_TIMEOUT", "The AI request timed out. You can retry or continue manually.", error);
      }
      if (candidate?.status === 429 || candidate?.code === 429) {
        throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROVIDER_RATE_LIMITED", "The AI service is at its current quota. Try again later or continue manually.", error);
      }
      if (candidate?.status === 401 || candidate?.status === 403 || candidate?.code === 401 || candidate?.code === 403) {
        throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROVIDER_AUTH_FAILED", "The configured AI key was rejected.", error);
      }
      throw error;
    }
    const text = response.text;
    let content: unknown;
    try {
      content = text ? JSON.parse(text) as unknown : null;
    } catch (error) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_OUTPUT_INVALID", "The AI service returned invalid structured data.", error);
    }
    return {
      content,
      finishReason: response.candidates?.[0]?.finishReason,
      truncated: response.candidates?.[0]?.finishReason === "MAX_TOKENS",
    };
  }
}

export const geminiSchemas = SCHEMAS;

FILE: backend\src\modules\ai-orchestration\sqlite-ai.repository.ts

import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../../infrastructure/database/sqlite.ts";
import type { AIProposalRepository } from "./ai-orchestration.repository";
import type { AIProposal, AIProposal as Proposal, AIProposalStatus } from "./ai-orchestration.types";
import { AIOrchestrationError } from "./ai-orchestration.errors";

interface ProposalRow {
  id: string; user_id: string; operation_type: Proposal["operationType"];
  proposal_type: Proposal["proposalType"]; status: Proposal["status"];
  proposal_data: string; created_at: string; updated_at: string;
}
function mapRow(row: ProposalRow): Proposal {
  return {
    id: row.id, userId: row.user_id, operationType: row.operation_type,
    proposalType: row.proposal_type, status: row.status,
    proposalData: JSON.parse(row.proposal_data), createdAt: row.created_at, updatedAt: row.updated_at,
  };
}

export class SqliteAIProposalRepository implements AIProposalRepository {
  constructor(private readonly connection: DatabaseConnection) {}

  async create(proposal: AIProposal): Promise<AIProposal> {
    this.connection.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO ai_proposals
      (id,user_id,operation_type,proposal_type,status,proposal_data,created_at,updated_at)
      VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`).run(proposal.id, proposal.userId, proposal.operationType,
      proposal.proposalType, proposal.status, JSON.stringify(proposal.proposalData), proposal.createdAt, proposal.updatedAt);
    return proposal;
  }

  async getById(proposalId: string): Promise<AIProposal | null> {
    const row = this.connection.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM ai_proposals WHERE id = ?")
      .get(proposalId) as ProposalRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapRow(row) : null;
  }

  async update(proposal: AIProposal, expectedStatus?: AIProposalStatus): Promise<AIProposal> {
    const statusClause = expectedStatus ? " AND status = ?" : "";
    const result = this.connection.db.prepare(`UPDATE ai_proposals SET status=?, proposal_data=?, updated_at=?
      WHERE id=? AND user_id=?${statusClause}`).run(proposal.status, JSON.stringify(proposal.proposalData),
      proposal.updatedAt, proposal.id, proposal.userId, ...(expectedStatus ? [expectedStatus] : []));
    if (Number(result.changes) !== 1) {
      throw new AIOrchestrationError("AI_PROPOSAL_NOT_FOUND", "Proposal was not found.");
    }
    return proposal;
  }

  async claimForApplication(proposalId: string, userId: string, updatedAt: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const result = this.connection.db.prepare(`UPDATE ai_proposals SET status='APPLYING', updated_at=?
      WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status IN ('PENDING_REVIEW','EDITED','PARTIALLY_APPROVED','FAILED')`)
      .run(updatedAt, proposalId, userId);
    return Number(result.changes) === 1;
  }
}

FILE: backend\src\modules\auth\auth.errors.ts

import type { AuthError, AuthErrorCode } from "./auth.types";

const ERROR_MESSAGES: Record<AuthErrorCode, string> = {
  AUTH_INVALID_INPUT: "Email hoặc mật khẩu không hợp lệ.",
  AUTH_INVALID_CREDENTIALS: "Email hoặc mật khẩu không chính xác.",
  AUTH_EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS: "Email này đã được đăng ký.",
  AUTH_EMAIL_NOT_CONFIRMED: "Email chưa được xác minh.",
  AUTH_SESSION_EXPIRED: "Phiên đăng nhập đã hết hạn. Vui lòng đăng nhập lại.",
  AUTH_RATE_LIMITED: "Có quá nhiều yêu cầu. Vui lòng thử lại sau.",
  AUTH_NETWORK_ERROR: "Không thể kết nối mạng. Vui lòng thử lại.",
  AUTH_PROVIDER_ERROR: "Dịch vụ xác thực đang gặp sự cố.",
  AUTH_UNAUTHENTICATED: "Bạn cần đăng nhập để tiếp tục."
};

export function createAuthError(code: AuthErrorCode): AuthError {
  return { code, message: ERROR_MESSAGES[code] };
}

export function normalizeProviderError(error: unknown): AuthError {
  const message =
    typeof error === "object" && error !== null && "message" in error
      ? String((error as { message: unknown }).message).toLowerCase()
      : "";

  const status =
    typeof error === "object" && error !== null && "status" in error
      ? Number((error as { status: unknown }).status)
      : 0;

  if (
    message.includes("already registered") ||
    message.includes("user already exists") ||
    message.includes("already been registered")
  ) return createAuthError("AUTH_EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS");

  if (
    message.includes("invalid login credentials") ||
    message.includes("invalid credentials")
  ) return createAuthError("AUTH_INVALID_CREDENTIALS");

  if (
    message.includes("email not confirmed") ||
    message.includes("email_not_confirmed")
  ) return createAuthError("AUTH_EMAIL_NOT_CONFIRMED");

  if (
    message.includes("rate limit") ||
    message.includes("too many requests") ||
    status === 429
  ) return createAuthError("AUTH_RATE_LIMITED");

  if (
    message.includes("network") ||
    message.includes("fetch failed") ||
    message.includes("failed to fetch")
  ) return createAuthError("AUTH_NETWORK_ERROR");

  if (status === 401) return createAuthError("AUTH_INVALID_CREDENTIALS");

  return createAuthError("AUTH_PROVIDER_ERROR");
}

export interface ProviderUser {
  id: string;
  email: string | null;
}

export interface ProviderSession {
  user: ProviderUser;
  accessToken?: string;
  refreshToken?: string;
  expiresAt?: number;
}

export type ProviderAuthEvent =
  | "INITIAL_SESSION"
  | "SIGNED_IN"
  | "SIGNED_OUT"
  | "TOKEN_REFRESHED"
  | "USER_UPDATED"
  | "PASSWORD_RECOVERY"
  | "MFA_CHALLENGE_VERIFIED";

export interface ProviderResult<T> {
  data: T;
  error: unknown | null;
}

export interface AuthProvider {
  register(
    email: string,
    password: string
  ): Promise<
    ProviderResult<{
      user: ProviderUser | null;
      session: ProviderSession | null;
    }>
  >;

  signIn(
    email: string,
    password: string
  ): Promise<
    ProviderResult<{
      user: ProviderUser | null;
      session: ProviderSession | null;
    }>
  >;

  signOut(): Promise<ProviderResult<null>>;

  getSession(): Promise<
    ProviderResult<{
      session: ProviderSession | null;
    }>
  >;

  onAuthStateChange(
    callback: (
      event: ProviderAuthEvent,
      session: ProviderSession | null
    ) => void
  ): () => void;
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\modules\auth\auth.service.ts
============================================================

import type { AuthProvider } from "./auth.provider";
import { createAuthError, normalizeProviderError } from "./auth.errors";
import type {
  AuthenticationService,
  AuthenticationState,
  AuthenticatedUserContext,
  OperationResult,
  RegistrationResult,
  RegisterInput,
  SignInInput
} from "./auth.types";
import { validateRegisterInput, validateSignInInput } from "./auth.validation";

export interface AuthenticationApiService {
  register(
    input: RegisterInput
  ): Promise<
    OperationResult<{
      registration: RegistrationResult;
      accessToken: string | null;
    }>
  >;

  signIn(
    input: SignInInput
  ): Promise<
    OperationResult<{
      user: AuthenticatedUserContext;
      accessToken: string | null;
    }>
  >;
}

function toUserContext(user: {
  id: string;
  email: string | null;
}): AuthenticatedUserContext {
  return {
    userId: user.id,
    email: user.email,
    isAuthenticated: true
  };
}

function success<T>(data: T): OperationResult<T> {
  return { success: true, data };
}

function failure<T>(
  code: Parameters<typeof createAuthError>[0]
): OperationResult<T> {
  return {
    success: false,
    error: createAuthError(code)
  };
}

export function createAuthenticationService(
  provider: AuthProvider
): AuthenticationService {
  return {
    async register(
      input: RegisterInput
    ): Promise<OperationResult<RegistrationResult>> {
      if (!validateRegisterInput(input).valid) {
        return failure("AUTH_INVALID_INPUT");
      }

      try {
        const result = await provider.register(
          input.email.trim(),
          input.password
        );

        if (result.error) {
          return {
            success: false,
            error: normalizeProviderError(result.error)
          };
        }

        if (result.data.session && result.data.user) {
          return success({
            status: "authenticated",
            user: toUserContext(result.data.user)
          });
        }

        return success({
          status: "confirmation_required",
          email: input.email.trim()
        });
      } catch (error) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: normalizeProviderError(error)
        };
      }
    },

    async signIn(
      input: SignInInput
    ): Promise<OperationResult<AuthenticatedUserContext>> {
      if (!validateSignInInput(input).valid) {
        return failure("AUTH_INVALID_INPUT");
      }

      try {
        const result = await provider.signIn(
          input.email.trim(),
          input.password
        );

        if (result.error) {
          return {
            success: false,
            error: normalizeProviderError(result.error)
          };
        }

        if (!result.data.session || !result.data.user) {
          return failure("AUTH_INVALID_CREDENTIALS");
        }

        return success(toUserContext(result.data.user));
      } catch (error) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: normalizeProviderError(error)
        };
      }
    },

    async signOut(): Promise<OperationResult<void>> {
      try {
        const result = await provider.signOut();

        if (result.error) {
          return {
            success: false,
            error: normalizeProviderError(result.error)
          };
        }

        return success(undefined);
      } catch (error) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: normalizeProviderError(error)
        };
      }
    },

    async getAuthenticationState(): Promise<AuthenticationState> {
      try {
        const result = await provider.getSession();

        if (result.error || !result.data.session) {
          return {
            status: "unauthenticated",
            user: null
          };
        }

        return {
          status: "authenticated",
          user: toUserContext(result.data.session.user)
        };
      } catch {
        return {
          status: "unauthenticated",
          user: null
        };
      }
    },

    subscribeToAuthenticationState(listener) {
      return provider.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {
        if (session) {
          listener({
            status: "authenticated",
            user: toUserContext(session.user)
          });
        } else {
          listener({
            status: "unauthenticated",
            user: null
          });
        }
      });
    }
  };
}

/**
 * API-specific authentication service.
 * Returns the access token supplied by the authentication provider.
 * Does not change the B01 AuthenticationService contract.
 */
export function createAuthenticationApiService(
  provider: AuthProvider
): AuthenticationApiService {
  return {
    async register(input: RegisterInput) {
      if (!validateRegisterInput(input).valid) {
        return failure("AUTH_INVALID_INPUT");
      }

      try {
        const result = await provider.register(
          input.email.trim(),
          input.password
        );

        if (result.error) {
          return {
            success: false,
            error: normalizeProviderError(result.error)
          };
        }

        if (result.data.session && result.data.user) {
          return success({
            registration: {
              status: "authenticated",
              user: toUserContext(result.data.user)
            },
            accessToken: result.data.session.accessToken ?? null
          });
        }

        return success({
          registration: {
            status: "confirmation_required",
            email: input.email.trim()
          },
          accessToken: null
        });
      } catch (error) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: normalizeProviderError(error)
        };
      }
    },

    async signIn(input: SignInInput) {
      if (!validateSignInInput(input).valid) {
        return failure("AUTH_INVALID_INPUT");
      }

      try {
        const result = await provider.signIn(
          input.email.trim(),
          input.password
        );

        if (result.error) {
          return {
            success: false,
            error: normalizeProviderError(result.error)
          };
        }

        if (!result.data.session || !result.data.user) {
          return failure("AUTH_INVALID_CREDENTIALS");
        }

        return success({
          user: toUserContext(result.data.user),
          accessToken: result.data.session.accessToken ?? null
        });
      } catch (error) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: normalizeProviderError(error)
        };
      }
    }
  };
}

export interface RegisterInput {
  email: string;
  password: string;
}

export interface SignInInput {
  email: string;
  password: string;
}

export interface AuthenticatedUserContext {
  userId: string;
  email: string | null;
  isAuthenticated: true;
  /** Selected by the client; every use must still verify workspace ownership. */
  workspaceId?: string;
}

export type AuthenticationState =
  | { status: "loading"; user: null }
  | { status: "unauthenticated"; user: null }
  | { status: "authenticated"; user: AuthenticatedUserContext };

export type RegistrationResult =
  | { status: "confirmation_required"; email: string }
  | { status: "authenticated"; user: AuthenticatedUserContext };

export type AuthErrorCode =
  | "AUTH_INVALID_INPUT"
  | "AUTH_INVALID_CREDENTIALS"
  | "AUTH_EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS"
  | "AUTH_EMAIL_NOT_CONFIRMED"
  | "AUTH_SESSION_EXPIRED"
  | "AUTH_RATE_LIMITED"
  | "AUTH_NETWORK_ERROR"
  | "AUTH_PROVIDER_ERROR"
  | "AUTH_UNAUTHENTICATED";

export interface AuthError {
  code: AuthErrorCode;
  message: string;
}

export type OperationResult<T> =
  | { success: true; data: T }
  | { success: false; error: AuthError };

export interface AuthenticationService {
  register(input: RegisterInput): Promise<OperationResult<RegistrationResult>>;
  signIn(input: SignInInput): Promise<OperationResult<AuthenticatedUserContext>>;
  signOut(): Promise<OperationResult<void>>;
  getAuthenticationState(): Promise<AuthenticationState>;
  subscribeToAuthenticationState(
    listener: (state: AuthenticationState) => void
  ): () => void;
}

FILE: backend\src\modules\auth\auth.validation.ts
============================================================
import type { RegisterInput, SignInInput } from "./auth.types";

export interface ValidationResult {
  valid: boolean;
  errors: Partial<Record<"email" | "password", string>>;
}

const EMAIL_PATTERN = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

export function validateRegisterInput(input: RegisterInput): ValidationResult {
  const errors: ValidationResult["errors"] = {};
  const email = input.email.trim();

  if (!email || !EMAIL_PATTERN.test(email)) {
    errors.email = "Email không hợp lệ.";
  }

  if (!input.password || input.password.trim().length === 0) {
    errors.password = "Mật khẩu không được để trống.";
  }

  return { valid: Object.keys(errors).length === 0, errors };
}

export function validateSignInInput(input: SignInInput): ValidationResult {
  const errors: ValidationResult["errors"] = {};
  const email = input.email.trim();

  if (!email || !EMAIL_PATTERN.test(email)) {
    errors.email = "Email không hợp lệ.";
  }

  if (!input.password) {
    errors.password = "Mật khẩu không được để trống.";
  }

  return { valid: Object.keys(errors).length === 0, errors };
}

FILE: backend\src\modules\auth\supabase.provider.ts
============================================================

import { createClient, type SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import type {
  AuthProvider,
  ProviderAuthEvent,
  ProviderSession,
  ProviderUser
} from "./auth.provider";

function mapUser(user: {
  id: string;
  email?: string;
}): ProviderUser {
  return {
    id: user.id,
    email: user.email ?? null
  };
}

function mapSession(
  session: {
    user: {
      id: string;
      email?: string;
    };
    access_token: string;
    refresh_token: string;
    expires_at?: number;
  } | null
): ProviderSession | null {
  if (!session) return null;

  return {
    user: mapUser(session.user),
    accessToken: session.access_token,
    refreshToken: session.refresh_token,
    expiresAt: session.expires_at
  };
}

/**
 * Server-side Supabase Auth adapter.
 *
 * Each authentication operation gets its own client.
 * No shared persisted session is kept between API requests.
 */
export function createSupabaseAuthProvider(
  supabaseUrl: string,
  publishableKey: string
): AuthProvider {
  if (!supabaseUrl || !publishableKey) {
    throw new Error("Missing Supabase public configuration.");
  }

  function createAuthClient(): SupabaseClient {
    return createClient(supabaseUrl, publishableKey, {
      auth: {
        persistSession: false,
        autoRefreshToken: false,
        detectSessionInUrl: false
      }
    });
  }

  return {
    async register(email, password) {
      const client = createAuthClient();

      const { data, error } = await client.auth.signUp({
        email,
        password
      });

      return {
        data: {
          user: data.user ? mapUser(data.user) : null,
          session: mapSession(data.session)
        },
        error
      };
    },

    async signIn(email, password) {
      const client = createAuthClient();

      const { data, error } =
        await client.auth.signInWithPassword({
          email,
          password
        });

      return {
        data: {
          user: data.user ? mapUser(data.user) : null,
          session: mapSession(data.session)
        },
        error
      };
    },

    async signOut() {
      // The backend does not keep a shared Supabase session.
      // The client must discard its locally stored access token.
      return {
        data: null,
        error: null
      };
    },

    async getSession() {
      // Authentication state is verified per request by TokenVerifier.
      return {
        data: {
          session: null
        },
        error: null
      };
    },

    onAuthStateChange(
      _callback: (
        event: ProviderAuthEvent,
        session: ProviderSession | null
      ) => void
    ) {
      // There is no persistent server-side session to subscribe to.
      return () => {};
    }
  };
}

  GOAL_PARENT_INVALID: "Quan hệ Parent–Child không hợp lệ.",
  GOAL_CIRCULAR_REFERENCE: "Thao tác sẽ tạo circular reference.",

import type {
  CreateGoalInput,
  Goal,
  ListGoalsFilter,
  UpdateGoalInput
} from "./goal.types";

/**
 * D01 persistence boundary.
 * Implementations must enforce foreign keys and atomic writes for hierarchy changes.
 * Workspace/project ownership must not be inferred from IDs supplied by the client.
 */
export interface GoalRepository {
  createGoal(workspaceId: string, input: CreateGoalInput): Promise<Goal>;
  findGoalById(goalId: string): Promise<Goal | null>;
  listGoalsByWorkspaceId(
    workspaceId: string,
    filter?: ListGoalsFilter
  ): Promise<Goal[]>;
  updateGoal(goalId: string, input: UpdateGoalInput): Promise<Goal>;
  findDescendantIds(goalId: string): Promise<string[]>;
}

/**
 * B02 boundary. Implement this adapter using B02 ownership validation operations.
 */
export interface GoalWorkspaceAccess {
  getCurrentWorkspaceId(userId: string, selectedWorkspaceId?: string): Promise<string | null>;
  validateProjectInWorkspace(
    userId: string,
    workspaceId: string,
    projectId: string
  ): Promise<boolean>;
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\modules\goal\goal.service.ts
============================================================
import { createGoalError } from "./goal.errors";
import type { GoalRepository, GoalWorkspaceAccess } from "./goal.repository";
import type {
  AuthenticatedUserContext,
  CreateGoalInput,
  Goal,
  GoalHierarchyNode,
  GoalResult,
  GoalScope,
  GoalService,
  ListGoalsFilter,
  UpdateGoalInput
} from "./goal.types";
import {
  validateCreateGoalInput,
  validateUpdateGoalInput
} from "./goal.validation";

function success<T>(data: T): GoalResult<T> {
  return { success: true, data };
}

function failure<T>(
  code: Parameters<typeof createGoalError>[0]
): GoalResult<T> {
  return { success: false, error: createGoalError(code) };
}

function isTrustedUser(
  user: AuthenticatedUserContext | null | undefined
): user is AuthenticatedUserContext {
  return Boolean(
    user &&
    user.isAuthenticated === true &&
    typeof user.userId === "string" &&
    user.userId.trim().length > 0
  );
}

function normalizeError<T>(error: unknown): GoalResult<T> {
  const message =
    typeof error === "object" && error !== null && "message" in error
      ? String((error as { message: unknown }).message).toLowerCase()
      : "";
  if (message.includes("not found")) return failure("GOAL_NOT_FOUND");
  return failure("GOAL_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
}

function buildHierarchy(goals: Goal[]): GoalResult<GoalHierarchyNode[]> {
  const byId = new Map<string, GoalHierarchyNode>();
  for (const goal of goals) {
    if (byId.has(goal.id)) return failure("GOAL_INVALID_HIERARCHY");
    byId.set(goal.id, { goal, children: [] });
  }

  const roots: GoalHierarchyNode[] = [];
  for (const goal of goals) {
    const node = byId.get(goal.id)!;
    if (goal.parentGoalId === null) {
      roots.push(node);
      continue;
    }

    const parent = byId.get(goal.parentGoalId);
    if (!parent) return failure("GOAL_INVALID_HIERARCHY");
    parent.children.push(node);
  }

  const visiting = new Set<string>();
  const visited = new Set<string>();

  function visit(node: GoalHierarchyNode): boolean {
    if (visiting.has(node.goal.id)) return false;
    if (visited.has(node.goal.id)) return true;
    visiting.add(node.goal.id);
    for (const child of node.children) {
      if (!visit(child)) return false;
    }
    visiting.delete(node.goal.id);
    visited.add(node.goal.id);
    return true;
  }

  for (const root of roots) {
    if (!visit(root)) return failure("GOAL_INVALID_HIERARCHY");
  }
  if (visited.size !== goals.length) {
    return failure("GOAL_INVALID_HIERARCHY");
  }

  return success(roots);
}

export function createGoalService(
  repository: GoalRepository,
  workspaceAccess: GoalWorkspaceAccess
): GoalService {
  async function resolveWorkspace(user: AuthenticatedUserContext) {
    if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure<string>("GOAL_UNAUTHENTICATED");
    try {
      const workspaceId = await workspaceAccess.getCurrentWorkspaceId(user.userId, user.workspaceId);
      return workspaceId
        ? success(workspaceId)
        : failure<string>("GOAL_ACCESS_DENIED");
    } catch {
      return failure<string>("GOAL_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
    }
  }

  async function resolveOwnedGoal(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    goalId: string
  ): Promise<GoalResult<{ goal: Goal; workspaceId: string }>> {
    if (!isTrustedUser(user)) {
      return failure("GOAL_UNAUTHENTICATED");
    }
    if (!goalId?.trim()) return failure("GOAL_NOT_FOUND");

    try {
      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;

      const goal = await repository.findGoalById(goalId);
      if (!goal) return failure("GOAL_NOT_FOUND");
      if (goal.workspaceId !== workspaceResult.data) {
        return failure("GOAL_ACCESS_DENIED");
      }
      return success({ goal, workspaceId: workspaceResult.data });
    } catch (error) {
      return normalizeError(error);
    }
  }

  async function validateParent(
    workspaceId: string,
    parentGoalId: string,
    childProjectId: string | null
  ): Promise<GoalResult<Goal>> {
    try {
      const parent = await repository.findGoalById(parentGoalId);
      if (!parent) return failure("GOAL_PARENT_NOT_FOUND");
      if (parent.workspaceId !== workspaceId) {
        return failure("GOAL_OWNERSHIP_MISMATCH");
      }
      if (parent.archivedAt !== null) return failure("GOAL_PARENT_INVALID");
      if (parent.projectId !== childProjectId) {
        return failure("GOAL_INVALID_PLACEMENT");
      }
      return success(parent);
    } catch (error) {
      return normalizeError(error);
    }
  }

  async function wouldCreateCycle(
    parentGoalId: string,
    childGoalId: string
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    if (parentGoalId === childGoalId) return true;
    const descendants = await repository.findDescendantIds(childGoalId);
    return descendants.includes(parentGoalId);
  }

  return {
    async createGoal(user, input: CreateGoalInput) {
      if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("GOAL_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      const validation = validateCreateGoalInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return failure(validation.code ?? "GOAL_INVALID_INPUT");
      }

      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;
      const workspaceId = workspaceResult.data;

      try {
        const projectId = input.projectId ?? null;
        if (projectId) {
          const allowed = await workspaceAccess.validateProjectInWorkspace(
            user.userId, workspaceId, projectId
          );
          if (!allowed) return failure("GOAL_INVALID_PLACEMENT");
        }

        const parentGoalId = input.parentGoalId ?? null;
        if (parentGoalId) {
          const parentResult = await validateParent(
            workspaceId, parentGoalId, projectId
          );
          if (!parentResult.success) return parentResult;
        }

        const goal = await repository.createGoal(workspaceId, {
          title: input.title.trim(),
          description:
            input.description === undefined || input.description === null
              ? null
              : input.description.trim() || null,
          targetDate: input.targetDate ?? null,
          projectId,
          parentGoalId
        });

        if (
          goal.workspaceId !== workspaceId ||
          goal.projectId !== projectId ||
          goal.parentGoalId !== parentGoalId
        ) {
          return failure("GOAL_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
        }
        return success(goal);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizeError(error);
      }
    },

    async getGoal(user, goalId) {
      const result = await resolveOwnedGoal(user, goalId);
      return result.success ? success(result.data.goal) : result;
    },

    async listGoals(user, filter: ListGoalsFilter = {}) {
      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;

      try {
        if (filter.projectId) {
          const allowed = await workspaceAccess.validateProjectInWorkspace(
            user.userId, workspaceResult.data, filter.projectId
          );
          if (!allowed) return failure("GOAL_INVALID_PLACEMENT");
        }

        const goals = await repository.listGoalsByWorkspaceId(
          workspaceResult.data,
          filter
        );
        return success(goals);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizeError(error);
      }
    },

    async updateGoal(user, goalId, input: UpdateGoalInput) {
      const owned = await resolveOwnedGoal(user, goalId);
      if (!owned.success) return owned;

      const validation = validateUpdateGoalInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return failure(validation.code ?? "GOAL_INVALID_INPUT");
      }

      const safeInput: UpdateGoalInput = {};
      if (input.title !== undefined) safeInput.title = input.title.trim();
      if (input.description !== undefined) {
        safeInput.description =
          input.description === null ? null : input.description.trim() || null;
      }
      if (input.targetDate !== undefined) safeInput.targetDate = input.targetDate;

      try {
        const updated = await repository.updateGoal(goalId, safeInput);
        if (
          updated.id !== owned.data.goal.id ||
          updated.workspaceId !== owned.data.goal.workspaceId ||
          updated.projectId !== owned.data.goal.projectId ||
          updated.parentGoalId !== owned.data.goal.parentGoalId
        ) {
          return failure("GOAL_INVALID_HIERARCHY");
        }
        return success(updated);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizeError(error);
      }
    },

    async addChildGoal(user, parentGoalId, input) {
      const parentResult = await resolveOwnedGoal(user, parentGoalId);
      if (!parentResult.success) return parentResult;
      return this.createGoal(user, {
        ...input,
        projectId: parentResult.data.goal.projectId,
        parentGoalId
      });
    },

    async getGoalHierarchy(user, scope: GoalScope = {}) {
      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;

      try {
        if (scope.projectId) {
          const allowed = await workspaceAccess.validateProjectInWorkspace(
            user.userId, workspaceResult.data, scope.projectId
          );
          if (!allowed) return failure("GOAL_INVALID_PLACEMENT");
        }

        if (scope.rootGoalId) {
          const rootResult = await resolveOwnedGoal(user, scope.rootGoalId);
          if (!rootResult.success) return rootResult;
        }

        const goals = await repository.listGoalsByWorkspaceId(
          workspaceResult.data,
          {
            projectId: scope.projectId,
            includeArchived: scope.includeArchived
          }
        );

        const scopedGoals = scope.rootGoalId
          ? goals.filter((goal) => goal.id === scope.rootGoalId ||
              goal.parentGoalId === scope.rootGoalId ||
              goals.some((candidate) =>
                candidate.id === goal.parentGoalId &&
                candidate.parentGoalId === scope.rootGoalId
              ))
          : goals;

        return buildHierarchy(scopedGoals);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizeError(error);
      }
    },

    async validateParentChild(user, parentGoalId, childGoalId) {
      const parentResult = await resolveOwnedGoal(user, parentGoalId);
      if (!parentResult.success) return parentResult;
      const childResult = await resolveOwnedGoal(user, childGoalId);
      if (!childResult.success) return childResult;

      const parent = parentResult.data.goal;
      const child = childResult.data.goal;

      if (parent.id === child.id) return failure("GOAL_CIRCULAR_REFERENCE");
      if (parent.workspaceId !== child.workspaceId) {
        return failure("GOAL_OWNERSHIP_MISMATCH");
      }
      if (parent.projectId !== child.projectId) {
        return failure("GOAL_INVALID_PLACEMENT");
      }

      try {
        if (await wouldCreateCycle(parent.id, child.id)) {
          return failure("GOAL_CIRCULAR_REFERENCE");
        }
        return success(true);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizeError(error);
      }
    }
  };
}

import type { CreateGoalInput, UpdateGoalInput } from "./goal.types";

const MAX_TITLE_LENGTH = 160;
const MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH = 5000;

export interface GoalValidationResult {
  valid: boolean;
  code?: "GOAL_INVALID_TITLE" | "GOAL_INVALID_INPUT";
}

function validOptionalText(value: unknown, maxLength: number): boolean {
  return value === undefined || value === null ||
    (typeof value === "string" && value.length <= maxLength);
}

function validDate(value: unknown): boolean {
  if (value === undefined || value === null) return true;
  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.trim() === "") return false;
  return !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(value));
}

export function validateCreateGoalInput(
  input: CreateGoalInput
): GoalValidationResult {
  if (
    typeof input.title !== "string" ||
    input.title.trim().length === 0 ||
    input.title.trim().length > MAX_TITLE_LENGTH
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_TITLE" };
  }

  if (
    !validOptionalText(input.description, MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH) ||
    !validDate(input.targetDate) ||
    (input.projectId !== undefined && input.projectId !== null &&
      (typeof input.projectId !== "string" || !input.projectId.trim())) ||
    (input.parentGoalId !== undefined && input.parentGoalId !== null &&
      (typeof input.parentGoalId !== "string" || !input.parentGoalId.trim()))
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_INPUT" };
  }

  return { valid: true };
}

export function validateUpdateGoalInput(
  input: UpdateGoalInput
): GoalValidationResult {
  if (Object.keys(input).length === 0) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_INPUT" };
  }

  if (
    input.title !== undefined &&
    (typeof input.title !== "string" ||
      input.title.trim().length === 0 ||
      input.title.trim().length > MAX_TITLE_LENGTH)
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_TITLE" };
  }

  if (
    !validOptionalText(input.description, MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH) ||
    !validDate(input.targetDate)
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_INPUT" };
  }

  return { valid: true };
}

FILE: backend\src\modules\measurement\measurement-rules.ts

import { MeasurementError } from "./measurement.errors.ts";
import type { MeasurementRules } from "./measurement.repository.ts";
import type { Measurement, MeasurementRecord, MeasurementValue } from "./measurement.types.ts";

const VALUE_TYPES = new Set(["NUMBER", "TEXT", "BOOLEAN"]);
function matchesType(type: string, value: unknown): value is MeasurementValue {
  if (type === "NUMBER") return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value);
  if (type === "TEXT") return typeof value === "string";
  if (type === "BOOLEAN") return typeof value === "boolean";
  return false;
}

/** Bounded MVP type policy; C06 remains the owner and can version this explicitly later. */
export class MVPMeasurementRules implements MeasurementRules {
  validateDefinition(input: { valueType: string; unit: string | null; targetValue: unknown }): void {
    if (!VALUE_TYPES.has(input.valueType)) {
      throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_VALUE_TYPE", "Choose Number, Text, or Yes/No.");
    }
    if (input.valueType !== "NUMBER" && input.unit !== null) {
      throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_UNIT", "Units are supported for numeric measurements only.");
    }
    if (input.targetValue !== null && !matchesType(input.valueType, input.targetValue)) {
      throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_TARGET", "Target must match the measurement type.");
    }
  }

  validateValue(definition: Measurement, value: unknown): void {
    if (!matchesType(definition.valueType, value)) {
      throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_VALUE", "Value must match the measurement type.");
    }
  }

  assertCanRecord(definition: Measurement): void {
    if (definition.archivedAt) throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_ARCHIVED", "Archived measurements cannot receive new values.");
  }

  resolveCurrent(records: MeasurementRecord[]): MeasurementRecord | null {
    return records[0] ?? null;
  }

  assertCanUpdate(definition: Measurement, patch: { unit?: string | null; targetValue?: unknown }, hasRecords: boolean): void {
    if (definition.archivedAt) throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_ARCHIVED", "Archived measurements cannot be edited.");
    if (hasRecords && patch.unit !== undefined && patch.unit !== definition.unit) {
      throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_UPDATE", "Unit cannot change after values have been recorded.");
    }
  }

  assertCanArchive(definition: Measurement): void {
    if (definition.archivedAt) throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_UPDATE", "Measurement is already archived.");
  }
}

export type MeasurementErrorCode =

  | "MEASUREMENT_INVALID_UNIT"
  | "MEASUREMENT_INVALID_TARGET"
  | "MEASUREMENT_INVALID_TIMESTAMP"
  | "MEASUREMENT_RECORD_NOT_FOUND"
  | "MEASUREMENT_ARCHIVED"
  | "MEASUREMENT_INVALID_UPDATE"
  | "MEASUREMENT_PERSISTENCE_ERROR";

export class MeasurementError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly code: MeasurementErrorCode,
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "MeasurementError";
  }

import type {
  GoalId, Measurement, MeasurementId, MeasurementRecord, MeasurementRecordId,
  MeasurementHistoryQuery, AuthenticatedUserContext,
} from "./measurement.types";

export interface MeasurementRepository {
  createMeasurement(input: Omit<Measurement, "id">): Promise<Measurement>;
  getMeasurement(id: MeasurementId): Promise<Measurement | null>;
  listMeasurementsByGoal(goalId: GoalId, includeArchived: boolean): Promise<Measurement[]>;
  updateMeasurement(id: MeasurementId, patch: Partial<Pick<Measurement,
    "label" | "description" | "unit" | "targetValue" | "updatedAt">>): Promise<Measurement>;
  archiveMeasurement(id: MeasurementId, archivedAt: string): Promise<Measurement>;
  createRecord(input: Omit<MeasurementRecord, "id">): Promise<MeasurementRecord>;
  listRecords(measurementId: MeasurementId, query: MeasurementHistoryQuery): Promise<MeasurementRecord[]>;
}

export interface GoalAccess {
  assertCanAccessGoal(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: GoalId): Promise<void>;
}

export interface MeasurementRules {
  /** Domain policy supplied by C06: approved value types, unit and target compatibility. */
  validateDefinition(input: {
    valueType: string; unit: string | null; targetValue: unknown;
  }): void;
  /** Domain policy supplied by C06; must reject values incompatible with the definition. */
  validateValue(definition: Measurement, value: unknown): void;
  /** C06 decides whether archived definitions accept new records. */
  assertCanRecord(definition: Measurement): void;
  /** C06 decides whether/how current record is resolved; no ordering is assumed here. */
  resolveCurrent(records: MeasurementRecord[]): MeasurementRecord | null;
  /** C06 decides whether an existing definition may be updated, especially unit changes. */
  assertCanUpdate(definition: Measurement, patch: {
    label?: string; description?: string | null; unit?: string | null; targetValue?: unknown;
  }, hasRecords: boolean): void;
  /** C06 decides whether repeated archive requests are permitted. */
  assertCanArchive(definition: Measurement): void;
}

FILE: backend\src\modules\measurement\measurement.service.ts
============================================================
import { MeasurementError } from "./measurement.errors";
import type { GoalAccess, MeasurementRepository, MeasurementRules } from "./measurement.repository";
import type {
  AuthenticatedUserContext, CreateMeasurementInput, Measurement, MeasurementHistoryQuery,
  MeasurementRecord, RecordMeasurementValueInput, UpdateMeasurementInput,
} from "./measurement.types";
import {
  validateCreateMeasurementInput, validateHistoryQuery, validateRecordInput,
  validateUpdateMeasurementInput,
} from "./measurement.validation";

export class MeasurementService {
  constructor(
    private readonly repository: MeasurementRepository,
    private readonly goalAccess: GoalAccess,
    private readonly rules: MeasurementRules,
    private readonly now: () => Date = () => new Date(),
  ) {}

  async createMeasurement(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: CreateMeasurementInput): Promise<Measurement> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateCreateMeasurementInput(input);
    await this.goalAccess.assertCanAccessGoal(user, input.goalId);
    this.rules.validateDefinition({
      valueType: input.valueType,
      unit: input.unit ?? null,
      targetValue: input.targetValue ?? null,
    });
    const timestamp = this.now().toISOString();
    return this.repository.createMeasurement({
      goalId: input.goalId,
      label: input.label.trim(),
      description: input.description ?? null,
      valueType: input.valueType,
      unit: input.unit ?? null,
      targetValue: input.targetValue ?? null,
      createdAt: timestamp,
      updatedAt: timestamp,
      archivedAt: null,
    });
  }

  async getMeasurement(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string): Promise<Measurement> {
    const measurement = await this.getOwnedMeasurement(user, id);
    return measurement;
  }

  async listMeasurementsByGoal(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: string, includeArchived = false,
  ): Promise<Measurement[]> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    await this.goalAccess.assertCanAccessGoal(user, goalId);
    return this.repository.listMeasurementsByGoal(goalId, includeArchived);
  }

  async updateMeasurement(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string, patch: UpdateMeasurementInput,
  ): Promise<Measurement> {
    const definition = await this.getOwnedMeasurement(user, id);
    validateUpdateMeasurementInput(patch);
    const history = await this.repository.listRecords(id, {});
    this.rules.assertCanUpdate(definition, patch, history.length > 0);
    const candidate = {
      valueType: definition.valueType,
      unit: patch.unit === undefined ? definition.unit : patch.unit,
      targetValue: patch.targetValue === undefined ? definition.targetValue : patch.targetValue,
    };
    this.rules.validateDefinition(candidate);
    return this.repository.updateMeasurement(id, {
      ...patch,
      ...(patch.label !== undefined ? { label: patch.label.trim() } : {}),
      updatedAt: this.now().toISOString(),
    });
  }

  async recordMeasurementValue(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: RecordMeasurementValueInput,
  ): Promise<MeasurementRecord> {
    const definition = await this.getOwnedMeasurement(user, input.measurementId);
    validateRecordInput(input);
    this.rules.assertCanRecord(definition);
    this.rules.validateValue(definition, input.value);
    const createdAt = this.now().toISOString();
    return this.repository.createRecord({
      measurementId: definition.id,
      value: input.value,
      recordedAt: input.recordedAt ?? createdAt,
      createdAt,
      note: input.note ?? null,
    });
  }

  async getCurrentMeasurementValue(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string,
  ): Promise<MeasurementRecord | null> {
    await this.getOwnedMeasurement(user, id);
    const records = await this.repository.listRecords(id, {});
    return this.rules.resolveCurrent(records);
  }

  async getMeasurementHistory(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string, query: MeasurementHistoryQuery = {},
  ): Promise<MeasurementRecord[]> {
    await this.getOwnedMeasurement(user, id);
    validateHistoryQuery(query);
    return this.repository.listRecords(id, query);
  }

  async archiveMeasurement(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string): Promise<Measurement> {
    const definition = await this.getOwnedMeasurement(user, id);
    this.rules.assertCanArchive(definition);
    return this.repository.archiveMeasurement(id, this.now().toISOString());
  }

  private async getOwnedMeasurement(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string): Promise<Measurement> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    if (!id?.trim()) throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_NOT_FOUND", "Measurement ID is required.");
    const measurement = await this.repository.getMeasurement(id);
    if (!measurement) throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_NOT_FOUND", "Measurement was not found.");
    await this.goalAccess.assertCanAccessGoal(user, measurement.goalId);
    return measurement;
  }

  private assertAuthenticated(user: AuthenticatedUserContext): void {
    if (!user || user.isAuthenticated !== true || !user.userId) {
      throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_ACCESS_DENIED", "Authenticated user context is required.");
    }
  }
}

FILE: backend\src\modules\measurement\measurement.validation.ts

import { MeasurementError } from "./measurement.errors";
import type {
  CreateMeasurementInput,
  MeasurementHistoryQuery,
  RecordMeasurementValueInput,
  UpdateMeasurementInput,
} from "./measurement.types";

export function validateCreateMeasurementInput(input: CreateMeasurementInput): void {
  if (typeof input.goalId !== "string" || !input.goalId.trim()) throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_GOAL_NOT_FOUND", "Goal ID is required.");
  if (typeof input.label !== "string" || !input.label.trim()) throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_LABEL", "Label is required.");
  if (typeof input.valueType !== "string" || !input.valueType.trim()) throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_VALUE_TYPE", "Value type is required.");
  if (input.label.trim().length > 120) throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_LABEL", "Label must be 120 characters or fewer.");
  if (input.description != null && (typeof input.description !== "string" || input.description.length > 2000)) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_LABEL", "Description must be text up to 2,000 characters.");
  }
  if (input.unit != null && (typeof input.unit !== "string" || !input.unit.trim() || input.unit.length > 40)) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_UNIT", "Unit must be omitted, null, or non-empty text.");
  }
}

export function validateUpdateMeasurementInput(input: UpdateMeasurementInput): void {
  if (Object.keys(input).length === 0) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_UPDATE", "At least one field must be supplied.");
  }
  if (input.label !== undefined && (typeof input.label !== "string" || !input.label.trim())) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_LABEL", "Label cannot be empty.");
  }
  if (input.label !== undefined && input.label.trim().length > 120) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_LABEL", "Label must be 120 characters or fewer.");
  }
  if (input.unit !== undefined && input.unit !== null &&
      (typeof input.unit !== "string" || !input.unit.trim() || input.unit.length > 40)) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_UNIT", "Unit must be null or non-empty text.");
  }
  if (input.description !== undefined && input.description !== null &&
      (typeof input.description !== "string" || input.description.length > 2000)) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_UPDATE", "Description must be text up to 2,000 characters.");
  }
}

export function validateRecordInput(input: RecordMeasurementValueInput): void {
  if (!input.measurementId?.trim()) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_NOT_FOUND", "Measurement ID is required.");
  }
  if (input.value === undefined || input.value === null ||
      !["number", "string", "boolean"].includes(typeof input.value) ||
      (typeof input.value === "number" && !Number.isFinite(input.value))) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_VALUE", "Value must be a finite number, string, or boolean.");
  }
  if (input.recordedAt !== undefined && Number.isNaN(Date.parse(input.recordedAt))) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_TIMESTAMP", "Recorded At must be a valid date.");
  }
  if (input.note != null && typeof input.note !== "string") {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_VALUE", "Note must be text.");
  }
  if (input.note != null && input.note.length > 1000) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_VALUE", "Note must be 1,000 characters or fewer.");
  }
  if (typeof input.value === "string" && input.value.length > 2000) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_VALUE", "Text values must be 2,000 characters or fewer.");
  }
}

export function validateHistoryQuery(query: MeasurementHistoryQuery): void {
  if (query.from && Number.isNaN(Date.parse(query.from))) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_TIMESTAMP", "History start date is invalid.");
  }
  if (query.to && Number.isNaN(Date.parse(query.to))) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_TIMESTAMP", "History end date is invalid.");
  }
  if (query.from && query.to && Date.parse(query.from) > Date.parse(query.to)) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_TIMESTAMP", "History date range is invalid.");
  }
  if (query.offset !== undefined && (!Number.isInteger(query.offset) || query.offset < 0)) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_VALUE", "Offset must be a non-negative integer.");
  }
  if (query.limit !== undefined && (!Number.isInteger(query.limit) || query.limit < 1 || query.limit > 200)) {
    throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_INVALID_VALUE", "Limit must be between 1 and 200.");
  }
}

import type {
  MilestoneErrorCode
} from "./milestone.types";

const ERROR_MESSAGES: Record<MilestoneErrorCode, string> = {
  MILESTONE_NOT_FOUND: "Không tìm thấy Milestone.",
  MILESTONE_ACCESS_DENIED: "Bạn không có quyền truy cập Milestone này.",
  MILESTONE_UNAUTHENTICATED: "Bạn cần đăng nhập để tiếp tục.",
  MILESTONE_INVALID_TITLE: "Tiêu đề Milestone không hợp lệ.",
  MILESTONE_INVALID_INPUT: "Dữ liệu Milestone không hợp lệ.",
  MILESTONE_GOAL_NOT_FOUND: "Không tìm thấy Goal sở hữu Milestone.",
  MILESTONE_GOAL_ACCESS_DENIED: "Bạn không có quyền truy cập Goal này.",
  MILESTONE_GOAL_INVALID: "Goal không hợp lệ để tạo Milestone.",
  MILESTONE_ACTION_NOT_FOUND: "Không tìm thấy Action liên quan.",
  MILESTONE_ACTION_RELATION_INVALID: "Quan hệ Milestone–Action không hợp lệ.",
  MILESTONE_ALREADY_COMPLETED: "Milestone đã được hoàn thành.",
  MILESTONE_COMPLETION_NOT_ALLOWED: "Không được phép hoàn thành Milestone.",
  MILESTONE_UPDATE_NOT_ALLOWED: "Không được phép cập nhật Milestone.",
  MILESTONE_PERSISTENCE_ERROR: "Không thể lưu hoặc truy xuất Milestone.",
  MILESTONE_UNKNOWN_ERROR: "Đã xảy ra lỗi không xác định."
};

export function createMilestoneError(code: MilestoneErrorCode) {
  return { code, message: ERROR_MESSAGES[code] };
}

import type {
  CreateMilestoneInput,
  Milestone,
  UpdateMilestoneInput
} from "./milestone.types";

/**
 * D01 boundary. A production adapter must enforce the Goal foreign key
 * and persist completion timestamps consistently.
 */
export interface MilestoneRepository {
  createMilestone(input: {
    goalId: string;
    title: string;
    description: string | null;
    targetDate: string | null;
  }): Promise<Milestone>;

  findMilestoneById(milestoneId: string): Promise<Milestone | null>;

  listMilestonesByGoal(goalId: string): Promise<Milestone[]>;

  updateMilestone(
    milestoneId: string,
    input: UpdateMilestoneInput
  ): Promise<Milestone>;

  completeMilestone(
    milestoneId: string,
    completedAt: string
  ): Promise<Milestone>;
}

/**
 * B03 boundary. Use B03's GoalService.getGoal() to resolve the Goal
 * through the authenticated user context. Do not query B03 tables directly.
 */
export interface MilestoneGoalAccess {
  getGoalForUser(
    user: { userId: string; email: string | null; isAuthenticated: true },
    goalId: string
  ): Promise<
    | { success: true; data: { id: string; status?: string; archivedAt?: string | null } }
    | { success: false; error: { code: string; message: string } }
  >;
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\modules\milestone\milestone.service.ts
============================================================
import { createMilestoneError } from "./milestone.errors";
import type {
  AuthenticatedUserContext,
  CreateMilestoneInput,
  Milestone,
  MilestoneResult,
  UpdateMilestoneInput
} from "./milestone.types";
import type {
  MilestoneGoalAccess,
  MilestoneRepository
} from "./milestone.repository";
import {
  validateCreateMilestoneInput,
  validateUpdateMilestoneInput
} from "./milestone.validation";

function success<T>(data: T): MilestoneResult<T> {
  return { success: true, data };
}

function failure<T>(
  code: Parameters<typeof createMilestoneError>[0]
): MilestoneResult<T> {
  return { success: false, error: createMilestoneError(code) };
}

function isAuthenticated(
  user: AuthenticatedUserContext | null | undefined
): user is AuthenticatedUserContext {
  return Boolean(
    user &&
    user.isAuthenticated === true &&
    typeof user.userId === "string" &&
    user.userId.trim().length > 0
  );
}

function normalizePersistenceError<T>(): MilestoneResult<T> {
  return failure("MILESTONE_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
}

export interface MilestoneService {
  createMilestone(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    input: CreateMilestoneInput
  ): Promise<MilestoneResult<Milestone>>;

  getMilestone(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    milestoneId: string
  ): Promise<MilestoneResult<Milestone>>;

  listMilestonesByGoal(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    goalId: string
  ): Promise<MilestoneResult<Milestone[]>>;

  updateMilestone(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    milestoneId: string,
    input: UpdateMilestoneInput
  ): Promise<MilestoneResult<Milestone>>;

  completeMilestone(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    milestoneId: string
  ): Promise<MilestoneResult<Milestone>>;
}

export function createMilestoneService(
  repository: MilestoneRepository,
  goalAccess: MilestoneGoalAccess,
  now: () => string = () => new Date().toISOString()
): MilestoneService {
  async function resolveMilestone(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    milestoneId: string
  ): Promise<MilestoneResult<Milestone>> {
    if (!isAuthenticated(user)) {
      return failure("MILESTONE_UNAUTHENTICATED");
    }
    if (!milestoneId?.trim()) return failure("MILESTONE_NOT_FOUND");

    try {
      const milestone = await repository.findMilestoneById(milestoneId);
      if (!milestone) return failure("MILESTONE_NOT_FOUND");

      const goalResult = await goalAccess.getGoalForUser(user, milestone.goalId);
      if (!goalResult.success) {
        if (goalResult.error.code === "GOAL_NOT_FOUND") {
          return failure("MILESTONE_GOAL_NOT_FOUND");
        }
        return failure("MILESTONE_ACCESS_DENIED");
      }

      return success(milestone);
    } catch {
      return normalizePersistenceError();
    }
  }

  async function validateGoalAccess(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    goalId: string
  ): Promise<MilestoneResult<true>> {
    try {
      const result = await goalAccess.getGoalForUser(user, goalId);
      if (result.success) return success(true);
      if (result.error.code === "GOAL_NOT_FOUND") {
        return failure("MILESTONE_GOAL_NOT_FOUND");
      }
      if (result.error.code === "GOAL_ACCESS_DENIED") {
        return failure("MILESTONE_GOAL_ACCESS_DENIED");
      }
      return failure("MILESTONE_GOAL_INVALID");
    } catch {
      return normalizePersistenceError();
    }
  }

  return {
    async createMilestone(user, input) {
      if (!isAuthenticated(user)) {
        return failure("MILESTONE_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      }

      const validation = validateCreateMilestoneInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return failure(validation.code ?? "MILESTONE_INVALID_INPUT");
      }

      const goalResult = await validateGoalAccess(user, input.goalId);
      if (!goalResult.success) return goalResult;

      try {
        const milestone = await repository.createMilestone({
          goalId: input.goalId,
          title: input.title.trim(),
          description:
            input.description === undefined || input.description === null
              ? null
              : input.description.trim() || null,
          targetDate: input.targetDate ?? null
        });
        if (milestone.goalId !== input.goalId) {
          return failure("MILESTONE_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
        }
        return success(milestone);
      } catch {
        return normalizePersistenceError();
      }
    },

    async getMilestone(user, milestoneId) {
      return resolveMilestone(user, milestoneId);
    },

    async listMilestonesByGoal(user, goalId) {
      if (!isAuthenticated(user)) {
        return failure("MILESTONE_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      }
      if (!goalId?.trim()) return failure("MILESTONE_GOAL_NOT_FOUND");

      const goalResult = await validateGoalAccess(user, goalId);
      if (!goalResult.success) return goalResult;

      try {
        return success(await repository.listMilestonesByGoal(goalId));
      } catch {
        return normalizePersistenceError();
      }
    },

    async updateMilestone(user, milestoneId, input) {
      const existing = await resolveMilestone(user, milestoneId);
      if (!existing.success) return existing;

      const validation = validateUpdateMilestoneInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return failure(validation.code ?? "MILESTONE_INVALID_INPUT");
      }
      if (existing.data.status === "COMPLETED") {
        return failure("MILESTONE_UPDATE_NOT_ALLOWED");
      }

      const safeInput: UpdateMilestoneInput = {};
      if (input.title !== undefined) safeInput.title = input.title.trim();
      if (input.description !== undefined) {
        safeInput.description =
          input.description === null ? null : input.description.trim() || null;
      }
      if (input.targetDate !== undefined) safeInput.targetDate = input.targetDate;

      try {
        const updated = await repository.updateMilestone(milestoneId, safeInput);
        if (
          updated.id !== existing.data.id ||
          updated.goalId !== existing.data.goalId
        ) {
          return failure("MILESTONE_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
        }
        return success(updated);
      } catch {
        return normalizePersistenceError();
      }
    },

    async completeMilestone(user, milestoneId) {
      const existing = await resolveMilestone(user, milestoneId);
      if (!existing.success) return existing;
      if (existing.data.status === "COMPLETED") {
        return failure("MILESTONE_ALREADY_COMPLETED");
      }

      try {
        const completed = await repository.completeMilestone(
          milestoneId,
          now()
        );
        if (
          completed.id !== existing.data.id ||
          completed.goalId !== existing.data.goalId ||
          completed.status !== "COMPLETED" ||
          completed.completedAt === null
        ) {
          return failure("MILESTONE_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
        }
        return success(completed);
      } catch {
        return normalizePersistenceError();
      }
    }
  };
}

import type { AuthenticatedUserContext } from "../auth/auth.types";

export type { AuthenticatedUserContext };

/**
 * PENDING / COMPLETED are the proposed MVP values in the B04 specification.
 * Final domain approval is still required (C04).
 */
export type MilestoneStatus = "PENDING" | "COMPLETED";

export interface Milestone {
  id: string;
  goalId: string;
  title: string;
  description: string | null;
  targetDate: string | null;
  status: MilestoneStatus;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  completedAt: string | null;
}

export interface CreateMilestoneInput {
  goalId: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string | null;
  targetDate?: string | null;
}

export interface UpdateMilestoneInput {
  title?: string;
  description?: string | null;
  targetDate?: string | null;
}

export type MilestoneResult<T> =
  | { success: true; data: T }
  | {
      success: false;
      error: {
        code: MilestoneErrorCode;
        message: string;
      };
    };

export type MilestoneErrorCode =
  | "MILESTONE_NOT_FOUND"
  | "MILESTONE_ACCESS_DENIED"
  | "MILESTONE_UNAUTHENTICATED"
  | "MILESTONE_INVALID_TITLE"
  | "MILESTONE_INVALID_INPUT"
  | "MILESTONE_GOAL_NOT_FOUND"
  | "MILESTONE_GOAL_ACCESS_DENIED"
  | "MILESTONE_GOAL_INVALID"
  | "MILESTONE_ACTION_NOT_FOUND"
  | "MILESTONE_ACTION_RELATION_INVALID"
  | "MILESTONE_ALREADY_COMPLETED"
  | "MILESTONE_COMPLETION_NOT_ALLOWED"
  | "MILESTONE_UPDATE_NOT_ALLOWED"
  | "MILESTONE_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"
  | "MILESTONE_UNKNOWN_ERROR";

FILE: backend\src\modules\milestone\milestone.validation.ts
============================================================
import type {
  CreateMilestoneInput,
  UpdateMilestoneInput
} from "./milestone.types";

const MAX_TITLE_LENGTH = 160;
const MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH = 5000;

export interface MilestoneValidationResult {
  valid: boolean;
  code?: "MILESTONE_INVALID_TITLE" | "MILESTONE_INVALID_INPUT";
}

function validOptionalText(value: unknown, maxLength: number): boolean {
  return value === undefined || value === null ||
    (typeof value === "string" && value.length <= maxLength);
}

function validDate(value: unknown): boolean {
  if (value === undefined || value === null) return true;
  return typeof value === "string" &&
    value.trim().length > 0 &&
    !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(value));
}

export function validateCreateMilestoneInput(
  input: CreateMilestoneInput
): MilestoneValidationResult {
  if (
    typeof input.title !== "string" ||
    input.title.trim().length === 0 ||
    input.title.trim().length > MAX_TITLE_LENGTH
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "MILESTONE_INVALID_TITLE" };
  }

  if (
    typeof input.goalId !== "string" ||
    input.goalId.trim().length === 0 ||
    !validOptionalText(input.description, MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH) ||
    !validDate(input.targetDate)
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "MILESTONE_INVALID_INPUT" };
  }

  return { valid: true };
}

export function validateUpdateMilestoneInput(
  input: UpdateMilestoneInput
): MilestoneValidationResult {
  if (Object.keys(input).length === 0) {
    return { valid: false, code: "MILESTONE_INVALID_INPUT" };
  }

  if (
    input.title !== undefined &&
    (typeof input.title !== "string" ||
      input.title.trim().length === 0 ||
      input.title.trim().length > MAX_TITLE_LENGTH)
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "MILESTONE_INVALID_TITLE" };
  }

  if (
    !validOptionalText(input.description, MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH) ||
    !validDate(input.targetDate)
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "MILESTONE_INVALID_INPUT" };
  }

  return { valid: true };
}

import type {
  ActionId, AuthenticatedUserContext, DailyPlan, DailyPlanId, PlanningDateRange,
  ScheduleEntry, ScheduleId,
} from "./planning.types";

export interface PlanningRepository {
  createDailyPlan(input: Omit<DailyPlan, "id">): Promise<DailyPlan>;
  getDailyPlan(id: DailyPlanId): Promise<DailyPlan | null>;
  findDailyPlanByUserAndDate(userId: string, date: string): Promise<DailyPlan | null>;
  updateDailyPlan(id: DailyPlanId, patch: Partial<Pick<DailyPlan, "date" | "updatedAt">>): Promise<DailyPlan>;
  listSchedulesByDailyPlan(dailyPlanId: DailyPlanId): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]>;
  createSchedule(input: Omit<ScheduleEntry, "id">): Promise<ScheduleEntry>;
  getSchedule(id: ScheduleId): Promise<ScheduleEntry | null>;
  updateSchedule(id: ScheduleId, patch: Partial<Pick<ScheduleEntry,
    "startAt" | "endAt" | "dailyPlanId" | "updatedAt">>): Promise<ScheduleEntry>;
  deleteSchedule(id: ScheduleId): Promise<void>;
  listSchedulesByDateRange(userId: string, range: PlanningDateRange, workspaceId?: string): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]>;
  findOverlappingSchedules(userId: string, startAt: string, endAt: string, excludeScheduleId?: ScheduleId): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]>;
}

export interface ActionAccess {
  assertCanAccessAction(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, actionId: ActionId): Promise<void>;
}

export interface PlanningAccess {
  assertCanAccessDailyPlan(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, plan: DailyPlan): Promise<void>;
  assertCanAccessSchedule(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, schedule: ScheduleEntry): Promise<void>;
}

export interface PlanningRules {
  /** C07 / DEC-017 supplies approved conflict and relationship behavior. */
  assertScheduleAllowed(input: {
    userId: string; actionId: ActionId; dailyPlan: DailyPlan | null; startAt: string; endAt: string;
    excludeScheduleId?: ScheduleId;
  }): Promise<void>;
  /** C07 supplies the approved date/time-zone and date-boundary interpretation. */
  assertScheduleMatchesPlanDate(schedule: {
    startAt: string; endAt: string; dailyPlan: DailyPlan;
  }): void;
}

export interface PlanningProposalApproval {
  assertApprovedByUser(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, proposalId: string): Promise<void>;
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\modules\planning\planning.service.ts
============================================================
import { PlanningError } from "./planning.errors";
import type {
  ActionAccess, PlanningAccess, PlanningProposalApproval, PlanningRepository, PlanningRules,
} from "./planning.repository";
import type {
  ApprovedPlanningProposal, AuthenticatedUserContext, CreateDailyPlanInput, CreateScheduleInput,
  DailyPlan, PlanningDateRange, ScheduleEntry, UpdateDailyPlanInput, UpdateScheduleInput,
} from "./planning.types";
import {
  validateCreateScheduleInput, validateDailyPlanInput, validateDateRange,
  validateScheduleTimes, validateUpdateScheduleInput,
} from "./planning.validation";

export class PlanningService {
  constructor(
    private readonly repository: PlanningRepository,
    private readonly actionAccess: ActionAccess,
    private readonly access: PlanningAccess,
    private readonly rules: PlanningRules,
    private readonly proposalApproval: PlanningProposalApproval,
    private readonly now: () => Date = () => new Date(),
  ) {}

  async createDailyPlan(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: CreateDailyPlanInput): Promise<DailyPlan> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateDailyPlanInput(input);
    const existing = await this.repository.findDailyPlanByUserAndDate(user.userId, input.date);
    if (existing) throw new PlanningError("DAILY_PLAN_ALREADY_EXISTS", "A Daily Plan already exists for this user and date.");
    const timestamp = this.now().toISOString();
    return this.repository.createDailyPlan({
      userId: user.userId, date: input.date, createdAt: timestamp, updatedAt: timestamp,
    });
  }

  async getDailyPlan(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, date: string): Promise<{
    dailyPlan: DailyPlan; schedules: ScheduleEntry[];
  } | null> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateDailyPlanInput({ date });
    const plan = await this.repository.findDailyPlanByUserAndDate(user.userId, date);
    if (!plan) return null;
    await this.access.assertCanAccessDailyPlan(user, plan);
    return { dailyPlan: plan, schedules: await this.repository.listSchedulesByDailyPlan(plan.id) };
  }

  async updateDailyPlan(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string, patch: UpdateDailyPlanInput,
  ): Promise<DailyPlan> {
    const plan = await this.getOwnedPlan(user, id);
    validateDailyPlanInput(patch);
    if (patch.date !== undefined && patch.date !== plan.date) {
      const existing = await this.repository.findDailyPlanByUserAndDate(user.userId, patch.date);
      if (existing && existing.id !== id) {
        throw new PlanningError("DAILY_PLAN_ALREADY_EXISTS", "A Daily Plan already exists for the target date.");
      }
      const schedules = await this.repository.listSchedulesByDailyPlan(id);
      for (const schedule of schedules) {
        this.rules.assertScheduleMatchesPlanDate({ ...schedule, dailyPlan: { ...plan, date: patch.date } });
      }
    }
    return this.repository.updateDailyPlan(id, { ...patch, updatedAt: this.now().toISOString() });
  }

  async addActionToDailyPlan(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, dailyPlanId: string, input: Omit<CreateScheduleInput, "dailyPlanId">,
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    const plan = await this.getOwnedPlan(user, dailyPlanId);
    return this.createSchedule(user, { ...input, dailyPlanId: plan.id });
  }

  async removeActionFromDailyPlan(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, dailyPlanId: string, scheduleId: string,
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    const plan = await this.getOwnedPlan(user, dailyPlanId);
    const schedule = await this.getOwnedSchedule(user, scheduleId);
    if (schedule.dailyPlanId !== plan.id) {
      throw new PlanningError("SCHEDULE_INVALID_RELATIONSHIP", "Schedule does not belong to the requested Daily Plan.");
    }
    return this.repository.updateSchedule(schedule.id, {
      dailyPlanId: null, updatedAt: this.now().toISOString(),
    });
  }

  async createSchedule(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: CreateScheduleInput): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateCreateScheduleInput(input);
    await this.actionAccess.assertCanAccessAction(user, input.actionId);
    const plan = input.dailyPlanId ? await this.getOwnedPlan(user, input.dailyPlanId) : null;
    if (plan) this.rules.assertScheduleMatchesPlanDate({ startAt: input.startAt, endAt: input.endAt, dailyPlan: plan });
    await this.rules.assertScheduleAllowed({
      userId: user.userId, actionId: input.actionId, dailyPlan: plan, startAt: input.startAt, endAt: input.endAt,
    });
    const timestamp = this.now().toISOString();
    return this.repository.createSchedule({
      actionId: input.actionId, dailyPlanId: input.dailyPlanId ?? null,
      startAt: input.startAt, endAt: input.endAt, createdAt: timestamp, updatedAt: timestamp,
    });
  }

  async getSchedule(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    return this.getOwnedSchedule(user, id);
  }

  async updateSchedule(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string, patch: UpdateScheduleInput,
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    const schedule = await this.getOwnedSchedule(user, id);
    validateUpdateScheduleInput(patch);
    const startAt = patch.startAt ?? schedule.startAt;
    const endAt = patch.endAt ?? schedule.endAt;
    validateScheduleTimes(startAt, endAt);
    const dailyPlanId = patch.dailyPlanId === undefined ? schedule.dailyPlanId : patch.dailyPlanId;
    const plan = dailyPlanId ? await this.getOwnedPlan(user, dailyPlanId) : null;
    if (plan) this.rules.assertScheduleMatchesPlanDate({ startAt, endAt, dailyPlan: plan });
    await this.actionAccess.assertCanAccessAction(user, schedule.actionId);
    await this.rules.assertScheduleAllowed({
      userId: user.userId, actionId: schedule.actionId, dailyPlan: plan, startAt, endAt, excludeScheduleId: schedule.id,
    });
    return this.repository.updateSchedule(id, { ...patch, updatedAt: this.now().toISOString() });
  }

  async deleteSchedule(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.getOwnedSchedule(user, id);
    await this.repository.deleteSchedule(id);
  }

  async getSchedulesByDateRange(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, range: PlanningDateRange,
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateDateRange(range);
    return this.repository.listSchedulesByDateRange(user.userId, range, user.workspaceId);
  }

  async applyApprovedPlanningProposal(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, proposal: ApprovedPlanningProposal,
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    if (!proposal || proposal.approved !== true || !proposal.proposalId ||
        proposal.userId !== user.userId || !Array.isArray(proposal.schedules)) {
      throw new PlanningError("PLANNING_PROPOSAL_INVALID", "Proposal is invalid or not approved for this user.");
    }
    await this.proposalApproval.assertApprovedByUser(user, proposal.proposalId);
    const created: ScheduleEntry[] = [];
    // This starter applies through the same createSchedule operation; transaction/atomic batch
    // behavior must be supplied by D01 when persistence is integrated.
    for (const schedule of proposal.schedules) created.push(await this.createSchedule(user, schedule));
    return created;
  }

  private async getOwnedPlan(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string): Promise<DailyPlan> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    const plan = await this.repository.getDailyPlan(id);
    if (!plan) throw new PlanningError("DAILY_PLAN_NOT_FOUND", "Daily Plan was not found.");
    await this.access.assertCanAccessDailyPlan(user, plan);
    return plan;
  }

  private async getOwnedSchedule(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    const schedule = await this.repository.getSchedule(id);
    if (!schedule) throw new PlanningError("SCHEDULE_NOT_FOUND", "Schedule was not found.");
    await this.access.assertCanAccessSchedule(user, schedule);
    await this.actionAccess.assertCanAccessAction(user, schedule.actionId);
    return schedule;
  }

  private assertAuthenticated(user: AuthenticatedUserContext): void {
    if (!user || user.isAuthenticated !== true || !user.userId) {
      throw new PlanningError("DAILY_PLAN_ACCESS_DENIED", "Authenticated user context is required.");
    }
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\modules\planning\planning.types.ts
============================================================
export type DailyPlanId = string;
export type ScheduleId = string;
export type ActionId = string;

export interface AuthenticatedUserContext {
  userId: string;
  email: string | null;
  isAuthenticated: true;
  workspaceId?: string;
}

export interface DailyPlan {
  id: DailyPlanId;
  userId: string;
  date: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface ScheduleEntry {
  id: ScheduleId;
  actionId: ActionId;
  dailyPlanId: DailyPlanId | null;
  startAt: string;
  endAt: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface CreateDailyPlanInput { date: string; }
export interface UpdateDailyPlanInput { date?: string; }

export interface CreateScheduleInput {
  actionId: ActionId;

  endDate: string;
