FILE: backend\src\modules\planning\planning.validation.ts
============================================================
import { PlanningError } from "./planning.errors";
import type {
  CreateDailyPlanInput, CreateScheduleInput, PlanningDateRange, UpdateDailyPlanInput,
  UpdateScheduleInput,
} from "./planning.types";

function validDate(value: string): boolean {
  return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value) && !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(`${value}T00:00:00.000Z`))
    && new Date(`${value}T00:00:00.000Z`).toISOString().slice(0, 10) === value;
}

function validDateTime(value: string): boolean {
  return !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(value));
}

export function validateDailyPlanInput(input: CreateDailyPlanInput | UpdateDailyPlanInput): void {
  if (input.date !== undefined && !validDate(input.date)) {
    throw new PlanningError("DAILY_PLAN_INVALID_DATE", "Date must be a valid YYYY-MM-DD date.");
  }
  if (Object.keys(input).length === 0) {
    throw new PlanningError("PLANNING_VALIDATION_FAILED", "At least one field must be supplied.");
  }
}

export function validateScheduleTimes(startAt: string, endAt: string): void {
  if (!startAt || !validDateTime(startAt)) {
    throw new PlanningError("SCHEDULE_INVALID_START_TIME", "Start time must be a valid datetime.");
  }
  if (!endAt || !validDateTime(endAt)) {
    throw new PlanningError("SCHEDULE_INVALID_END_TIME", "End time must be a valid datetime.");
  }
  if (Date.parse(endAt) <= Date.parse(startAt)) {
    throw new PlanningError("SCHEDULE_INVALID_TIME_RANGE", "End time must be after start time.");
  }
}

export function validateCreateScheduleInput(input: CreateScheduleInput): void {
  if (!input.actionId?.trim()) {
    throw new PlanningError("SCHEDULE_ACTION_NOT_FOUND", "Action ID is required.");
  }
  validateScheduleTimes(input.startAt, input.endAt);
}

export function validateUpdateScheduleInput(input: UpdateScheduleInput): void {
  if (Object.keys(input).length === 0) {
    throw new PlanningError("PLANNING_VALIDATION_FAILED", "At least one field must be supplied.");
  }
  if (input.startAt !== undefined && !validDateTime(input.startAt)) {
    throw new PlanningError("SCHEDULE_INVALID_START_TIME", "Start time must be a valid datetime.");
  }
  if (input.endAt !== undefined && !validDateTime(input.endAt)) {
    throw new PlanningError("SCHEDULE_INVALID_END_TIME", "End time must be a valid datetime.");
  }
}

export function validateDateRange(range: PlanningDateRange): void {
  if (!validDate(range.startDate) || !validDate(range.endDate)) {
    throw new PlanningError("SCHEDULE_INVALID_DATE_RANGE", "Date range must contain valid dates.");
  }
  if (range.endDate < range.startDate) {
    throw new PlanningError("SCHEDULE_INVALID_DATE_RANGE", "End date cannot be before start date.");
  }
}

import type {
  ActionCompletionResult, AuthenticatedUserContext, GoalId, GoalProgressResult,
  MeasurementProgressResult, ProgressSummary,
} from "./progress.types";

export interface ProgressSourceAccess {
  assertCanAccessGoal(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: GoalId): Promise<void>;
  getMeasurementProgressInputs(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: GoalId): Promise<unknown[]>;
  getActionProgressInput(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: GoalId): Promise<unknown>;
  getChildGoalIds(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: GoalId): Promise<GoalId[]>;
  getGoalProgressInputs(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: GoalId): Promise<{
    measurementResults: MeasurementProgressResult[];
    actionResult: ActionCompletionResult | null;
    childGoalResults: GoalProgressResult[];
  }>;
}

export interface ProgressRules {
  calculateMeasurement(input: unknown): MeasurementProgressResult;
  calculateActionCompletion(input: unknown): ActionCompletionResult;
  calculateGoal(input: unknown): GoalProgressResult;
  aggregateChildGoals(input: unknown): GoalProgressResult;
  compareMeasurement(input: unknown): {
    difference: unknown | null; comparisonStatus: string; progressValue: number | null;
  };
  combineStatuses(statuses: string[]): "CALCULATED" | "NOT_APPLICABLE" | "INSUFFICIENT_DATA" | "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED";
}

export interface ProgressHistoryRepository {
  /** Optional persistence contract. Do not use until C08/C09 approves snapshots/history. */
  saveSnapshot?(userId: string, goalId: GoalId, summary: ProgressSummary): Promise<void>;
}

FILE: backend\src\modules\progress\progress.service.ts
============================================================
import { ProgressError } from "./progress.errors";
import type { ProgressRules, ProgressSourceAccess, ProgressHistoryRepository } from "./progress.repository";
import type {
  ActionCompletionResult, ActionProgressInput, AuthenticatedUserContext, ChildGoalAggregationInput,
  GoalProgressInput, GoalProgressResult, MeasurementProgressInput, MeasurementProgressResult,
  MeasurementTargetComparison, ProgressSummary,
} from "./progress.types";
import {
  validateActionProgressInput, validateChildAggregationInput, validateGoalProgressInput,
  validateMeasurementProgressInput,
} from "./progress.validation";

export class ProgressService {
  constructor(
    private readonly sources: ProgressSourceAccess,
    private readonly rules: ProgressRules,
    private readonly history?: ProgressHistoryRepository,
  ) {}

  calculateMeasurementProgress(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: MeasurementProgressInput,
  ): MeasurementProgressResult {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateMeasurementProgressInput(input);
    return this.rules.calculateMeasurement(input);
  }

  calculateActionCompletion(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: ActionProgressInput,
  ): ActionCompletionResult {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateActionProgressInput(input);
    return this.rules.calculateActionCompletion(input);
  }

  calculateGoalProgress(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: GoalProgressInput): GoalProgressResult {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateGoalProgressInput(input);
    return this.rules.calculateGoal(input);
  }

  aggregateChildGoalProgress(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: ChildGoalAggregationInput,
  ): GoalProgressResult {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateChildAggregationInput(input);
    return this.rules.aggregateChildGoals(input);
  }

  compareMeasurementWithTarget(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: MeasurementProgressInput,
  ): MeasurementTargetComparison {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateMeasurementProgressInput(input);
    if (input.targetValue === null) {
      throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_INSUFFICIENT_DATA", "Measurement has no Target Value.");
    }
    const comparison = this.rules.compareMeasurement(input);
    return {
      measurementId: input.measurementId,
      currentValue: input.currentValue,
      targetValue: input.targetValue,
      ...comparison,
    };
  }

  async getGoalProgressSummary(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: string): Promise<ProgressSummary> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    await this.sources.assertCanAccessGoal(user, goalId);
    const inputs = await this.sources.getGoalProgressInputs(user, goalId);
    const goalInput: GoalProgressInput = {
      goalId,
      measurementResults: inputs.measurementResults,
      actionResult: inputs.actionResult,
      childGoalResults: inputs.childGoalResults,
    };
    const goalProgress = this.rules.calculateGoal(goalInput);
    return {
      goalId,
      goalProgress,
      measurementProgress: inputs.measurementResults,
      actionCompletion: inputs.actionResult,
      childGoalProgress: inputs.childGoalResults,
      calculationStatus: this.rules.combineStatuses([
        goalProgress.calculationStatus,
        ...inputs.measurementResults.map((item) => item.calculationStatus),
        ...(inputs.actionResult ? [inputs.actionResult.calculationStatus] : []),
        ...inputs.childGoalResults.map((item) => item.calculationStatus),
      ]),
    };
  }

  async getProgressSummary(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalIds: string[]): Promise<ProgressSummary[]> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    if (!Array.isArray(goalIds) || goalIds.some((id) => !id?.trim())) {
      throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_INVALID_INPUT", "Goal IDs must be a list of non-empty IDs.");
    }
    const summaries: ProgressSummary[] = [];
    for (const goalId of goalIds) summaries.push(await this.getGoalProgressSummary(user, goalId));
    return summaries;
  }

  async getProgressDataForReview(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: string,
  ): Promise<ProgressSummary> {
    return this.getGoalProgressSummary(user, goalId);
  }

  async saveProgressSnapshot(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, summary: ProgressSummary): Promise<void> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    if (!this.history?.saveSnapshot) {
      throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED", "Progress snapshot persistence is not configured.");
    }
    await this.sources.assertCanAccessGoal(user, summary.goalId);
    await this.history.saveSnapshot(user.userId, summary.goalId, summary);
  }

  private assertAuthenticated(user: AuthenticatedUserContext): void {
    if (!user || user.isAuthenticated !== true || !user.userId) {
      throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_ACCESS_DENIED", "Authenticated user context is required.");
    }
  }
}

FILE: backend\src\modules\progress\progress.validation.ts
============================================================
import { ProgressError } from "./progress.errors";
import type {
  ActionProgressInput, ChildGoalAggregationInput, GoalProgressInput, MeasurementProgressInput,
} from "./progress.types";

export function validateMeasurementProgressInput(input: MeasurementProgressInput): void {
  if (!input.measurementId?.trim() || !input.valueType?.trim()) {
    throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_INVALID_INPUT", "Measurement ID and value type are required.");
  }
  if (input.currentValue === undefined || input.currentValue === null) {
    throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_INSUFFICIENT_DATA", "Current Measurement Value is unavailable.");
  }
  if (input.targetValue === undefined) {
    throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_INVALID_TARGET", "Target Value must be supplied explicitly, or null.");
  }
  if (!input.unit && input.unit !== null) {
    throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_INVALID_UNIT", "Unit must be supplied explicitly, or null.");
  }
}

export function validateActionProgressInput(input: ActionProgressInput): void {
  if (!input.goalId?.trim() || !Array.isArray(input.actions)) {
    throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_INVALID_INPUT", "Goal ID and Action collection are required.");
  }
  for (const action of input.actions) {
    if (!action.id?.trim() || !action.status?.trim()) {
      throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_INVALID_INPUT", "Every Action requires an ID and status.");
    }
  }
}

export function validateGoalProgressInput(input: GoalProgressInput): void {
  if (!input.goalId?.trim() || !Array.isArray(input.measurementResults) ||
      !Array.isArray(input.childGoalResults)) {
    throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_INVALID_INPUT", "Goal progress input is invalid.");
  }
}

export function validateChildAggregationInput(input: ChildGoalAggregationInput): void {
  if (!input.parentGoalId?.trim() || !Array.isArray(input.childGoalResults)) {
    throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_INVALID_INPUT", "Parent Goal and child results are required.");
  }
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  for (const result of input.childGoalResults) {
    if (!result.goalId?.trim() || seen.has(result.goalId)) {
      throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_INVALID_HIERARCHY", "Child Goal IDs must be present and unique.");
    }
    seen.add(result.goalId);
  }
}

import type { ReviewRecord, ReviewScope, PlannedWorkItem, ActualExecutionItem, PlannedActualComparison, ReviewSummary } from "./review.types";
import type { ReviewValidationRules } from "./review.validation";
export interface ReviewRepository {
  create(record: ReviewRecord): Promise<ReviewRecord>;
  getById(reviewId: string): Promise<ReviewRecord | null>;
  updateNote(reviewId: string, userId: string, note: string, updatedAt: string): Promise<ReviewRecord | null>;
  updateSummary(reviewId: string, userId: string, summary: ReviewSummary, updatedAt: string): Promise<ReviewRecord | null>;
}
export interface ReviewAccess { assertScopeAccess(userId: string, scope: ReviewScope): Promise<void>; }
export interface ReviewSourceReader {
  getPlannedWork(userId: string, scope: ReviewScope): Promise<{ data: PlannedWorkItem[]; limitations?: string[] }>;
  getActualExecution(userId: string, scope: ReviewScope): Promise<{ data: ActualExecutionItem[]; limitations?: string[] }>;
  getProgress(userId: string, scope: ReviewScope): Promise<{ data: unknown | null; status: "AVAILABLE" | "EMPTY" | "UNAVAILABLE"; limitations?: string[] }>;
  getMeasurement(userId: string, scope: ReviewScope): Promise<{ data: unknown | null; status: "AVAILABLE" | "EMPTY" | "UNAVAILABLE" | "NOT_REQUESTED"; limitations?: string[] }>;
}
export interface ReviewRules {
  validation: ReviewValidationRules;
  matchPlannedToActual(planned: PlannedWorkItem[], actual: ActualExecutionItem[]): PlannedActualComparison;
  generateSummary(input: Omit<ReviewSummary, "limitations">): ReviewSummary;
  now(): string;
  createReviewRecord: boolean;
  persistSummary: boolean;
}

FILE: backend\src\modules\review\review.service.ts
============================================================
import { ReviewError } from "./review.errors";
import type { CreateReviewInput, ReviewRecord, ReviewScope, ReviewSummary, ReviewUserContext, UpdateReviewNoteInput } from "./review.types";
import type { ReviewAccess, ReviewRepository, ReviewRules, ReviewSourceReader } from "./review.repository";
import { validateReviewNote, validateReviewScope } from "./review.validation";
export class ReviewService {
  constructor(private readonly repository: ReviewRepository, private readonly access: ReviewAccess,
    private readonly sources: ReviewSourceReader, private readonly rules: ReviewRules) {}
  async getReviewData(user: ReviewUserContext, scope: ReviewScope): Promise<ReviewSummary> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user); validateReviewScope(scope, this.rules.validation);
    await this.access.assertScopeAccess(user.userId, scope);
    let results: Awaited<ReturnType<ReviewSourceReader["getPlannedWork"]>> extends never ? never : any;
    try {
      results = await Promise.all([
        this.sources.getPlannedWork(user.userId, scope), this.sources.getActualExecution(user.userId, scope),
        this.sources.getProgress(user.userId, scope), this.sources.getMeasurement(user.userId, scope),
      ]);
    } catch (error) { throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE", "A Review source dependency is unavailable.", error); }
    const [p, a, g, m] = results;
    const plannedWork = { status: p.data.length ? "AVAILABLE" as const : "EMPTY" as const, data: p.data, limitations: p.limitations ?? [] };
    const actualExecution = { status: a.data.length ? "AVAILABLE" as const : "EMPTY" as const, data: a.data, limitations: a.limitations ?? [] };
    const progress = { status: g.status, data: g.data, limitations: g.limitations ?? [] };
    const measurement = { status: m.status, data: m.data, limitations: m.limitations ?? [] };
    let comparison;
    try {
      const compared = this.rules.matchPlannedToActual(p.data, a.data);
      comparison = { status: compared.items.length ? "AVAILABLE" as const : "EMPTY" as const, data: compared, limitations: compared.limitations };
    } catch (error) { throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_COMPARISON_FAILED", "Planned and actual comparison failed.", error); }
    try {
      return this.rules.generateSummary({ scope, plannedWork, actualExecution, comparison, progress, measurement, generatedAt: this.rules.now() });
    } catch (error) { throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_SUMMARY_GENERATION_FAILED", "Review summary generation failed.", error); }
  }
  async createReview(user: ReviewUserContext, input: CreateReviewInput): Promise<ReviewRecord> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user); validateReviewScope(input.scope, this.rules.validation);
    await this.access.assertScopeAccess(user.userId, input.scope);
    if (!this.rules.createReviewRecord) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_INVALID_STATE", "Review persistence is not enabled until its model is approved.");
    const note = input.note?.trim() || null;
    if (note && note.length > this.rules.validation.maxNoteLength) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_NOTE_INVALID", "Review note exceeds configured length.");
    const now = this.rules.now();
    const record: ReviewRecord = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), userId: user.userId, scope: input.scope, summary: null, note, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now };
    try { return await this.repository.create(record); }
    catch (error) { throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_PERSISTENCE_ERROR", "Unable to create Review.", error); }
  }
  async getReview(user: ReviewUserContext, reviewId: string): Promise<ReviewRecord> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    const record = await this.repository.getById(reviewId);
    if (!record) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_NOT_FOUND", "Review was not found.");
    if (record.userId !== user.userId) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_ACCESS_DENIED", "Review access denied.");
    await this.access.assertScopeAccess(user.userId, record.scope);
    return record;
  }
  async updateReviewNote(user: ReviewUserContext, reviewId: string, input: UpdateReviewNoteInput): Promise<ReviewRecord> {
    await this.getReview(user, reviewId);
    const note = validateReviewNote(input, this.rules.validation);
    try {
      const updated = await this.repository.updateNote(reviewId, user.userId, note, this.rules.now());
      if (!updated) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_NOT_FOUND", "Review was not found.");
      return updated;
    } catch (error) { if (error instanceof ReviewError) throw error; throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_PERSISTENCE_ERROR", "Unable to update Review note.", error); }
  }
  async refreshReviewSummary(user: ReviewUserContext, reviewId: string): Promise<ReviewRecord> {
    const record = await this.getReview(user, reviewId);
    const summary = await this.getReviewData(user, record.scope);
    if (!this.rules.persistSummary) return { ...record, summary };
    try {
      const updated = await this.repository.updateSummary(reviewId, user.userId, summary, this.rules.now());
      if (!updated) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_NOT_FOUND", "Review was not found.");
      return updated;
    } catch (error) { if (error instanceof ReviewError) throw error; throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_PERSISTENCE_ERROR", "Unable to refresh Review summary.", error); }
  }
  private assertAuthenticated(user: ReviewUserContext): void {
    if (!user?.isAuthenticated || !user.userId) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_ACCESS_DENIED", "Authentication is required.");
  }
}

FILE: backend\src\modules\review\review.types.ts

export type ReviewUserContext = { userId: string; isAuthenticated: true };
export type ReviewScope = { goalId?: string; periodStart?: string; periodEnd?: string; dailyPlanId?: string };
export type ReviewSourceStatus = "AVAILABLE" | "EMPTY" | "PARTIAL" | "UNAVAILABLE" | "NOT_REQUESTED";
export type ReviewSourceSection<T> = { status: ReviewSourceStatus; data: T | null; limitations: string[] };
export type PlannedWorkItem = { actionId: string; scheduleId?: string; plannedStartAt?: string | null; plannedEndAt?: string | null };
export type ActualExecutionItem = { actionId: string; status?: string; actualStartAt?: string | null; completedAt?: string | null; actualDuration?: number | null; executionRecordId?: string };
export type PlannedActualMatchStatus = "MATCHED" | "PLANNED_WITHOUT_EXECUTION_RECORD" | "EXECUTION_WITHOUT_PLAN_MATCH" | "UNMATCHED";
export type PlannedActualComparisonItem = { actionId: string; planned: PlannedWorkItem | null; actual: ActualExecutionItem | null; matchStatus: PlannedActualMatchStatus };
export type PlannedActualComparison = { items: PlannedActualComparisonItem[]; limitations: string[] };
export type ReviewSummary = {
  scope: ReviewScope;
  plannedWork: ReviewSourceSection<PlannedWorkItem[]>;
  actualExecution: ReviewSourceSection<ActualExecutionItem[]>;
  comparison: ReviewSourceSection<PlannedActualComparison>;
  progress: ReviewSourceSection<unknown>;
  measurement: ReviewSourceSection<unknown>;
  limitations: string[];
  generatedAt: string;
};
export type ReviewRecord = { id: string; userId: string; scope: ReviewScope; summary: ReviewSummary | null; note: string | null; createdAt: string; updatedAt: string };
export type CreateReviewInput = { scope: ReviewScope; note?: string | null };
export type UpdateReviewNoteInput = { content: string };

FILE: backend\src\modules\review\review.validation.ts
============================================================
import { ReviewError } from "./review.errors";
import type { ReviewScope, UpdateReviewNoteInput } from "./review.types";
export type ReviewValidationRules = { maxNoteLength: number; requireGoalId: boolean; allowPeriodOnlyScope: boolean };
export function validateReviewScope(scope: ReviewScope, rules: ReviewValidationRules): void {
  const goal = Boolean(scope.goalId?.trim()), period = Boolean(scope.periodStart || scope.periodEnd), daily = Boolean(scope.dailyPlanId?.trim());
  if (!goal && !period && !daily) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_INVALID_SCOPE", "A review scope is required.");
  if (rules.requireGoalId && !goal) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_INVALID_SCOPE", "A Goal ID is required.");
  if (!rules.allowPeriodOnlyScope && period && !goal && !daily) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_INVALID_SCOPE", "Period-only scope is disabled.");
  if (scope.periodStart && Number.isNaN(Date.parse(scope.periodStart))) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_INVALID_DATE_RANGE", "Invalid periodStart.");
  if (scope.periodEnd && Number.isNaN(Date.parse(scope.periodEnd))) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_INVALID_DATE_RANGE", "Invalid periodEnd.");
  if (scope.periodStart && scope.periodEnd && Date.parse(scope.periodStart) > Date.parse(scope.periodEnd))
    throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_INVALID_DATE_RANGE", "periodStart must not be after periodEnd.");
}
export function validateReviewNote(input: UpdateReviewNoteInput, rules: ReviewValidationRules): string {
  if (typeof input.content !== "string") throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_NOTE_INVALID", "Note content must be a string.");
  const content = input.content.trim();
  if (!content) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_NOTE_EMPTY", "Review note cannot be empty.");
  if (content.length > rules.maxNoteLength) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_NOTE_INVALID", "Review note exceeds configured length.");
  return content;
}

const ERROR_MESSAGES: Record<WorkspaceErrorCode, string> = {

  WORKSPACE_ALREADY_EXISTS: "Personal Workspace đã tồn tại.",
  WORKSPACE_ACCESS_DENIED: "Bạn không có quyền truy cập Workspace này.",
  WORKSPACE_INITIALIZATION_FAILED: "Không thể khởi tạo Workspace.",
  WORKSPACE_INVALID_OWNER: "Danh tính chủ sở hữu Workspace không hợp lệ.",
  WORKSPACE_INVALID_NAME: "Workspace name is required and must be 80 characters or fewer.",
  PROJECT_NOT_FOUND: "Không tìm thấy Project.",
  PROJECT_ACCESS_DENIED: "Bạn không có quyền truy cập Project này.",
  PROJECT_INVALID_NAME: "Tên Project không hợp lệ.",
  PROJECT_INVALID_INPUT: "Dữ liệu Project không hợp lệ.",

  PROJECT_UPDATE_FAILED: "Không thể cập nhật Project.",

import type {
  CreateProjectInput,
  Project,
  UpdateProjectInput,
  Workspace
} from "./workspace.types";

/**
 * D01 persistence boundary.
 *
 * getOrCreatePersonalWorkspace MUST be atomic and return a deterministic existing
 * personal workspace or create one when the owner has no workspace yet.
 */
export interface WorkspaceRepository {
  getOrCreatePersonalWorkspace(userId: string): Promise<Workspace>;
  findWorkspaceByUserId(userId: string): Promise<Workspace | null>;
  findWorkspaceById(workspaceId: string): Promise<Workspace | null>;
  listWorkspacesByUserId(userId: string): Promise<Workspace[]>;
  createWorkspace(userId: string, name: string): Promise<Workspace>;
  deleteWorkspace(workspaceId: string, userId: string): Promise<boolean>;

  createProject(
    workspaceId: string,
    input: CreateProjectInput
  ): Promise<Project>;

  findProjectById(projectId: string): Promise<Project | null>;
  listProjectsByWorkspaceId(workspaceId: string): Promise<Project[]>;

  updateProject(
    projectId: string,
    input: UpdateProjectInput
  ): Promise<Project>;
}

/**
 * B03 read-only boundary. B02 must not own or mutate Goal entities.
 * Return null from the service overview when B03 has not been connected yet.
 */
export interface GoalSummaryReader {
  getIndependentGoalCount(workspaceId: string): Promise<number>;
  getProjectGoalCount(projectId: string): Promise<number>;
}

============================================================
FILE: backend\src\modules\workspace\workspace.service.ts
============================================================
import { createWorkspaceError } from "./workspace.errors";
import type { GoalSummaryReader, WorkspaceRepository } from "./workspace.repository";
import type {
  AuthenticatedUserContext,
  CreateProjectInput,
  Project,
  ProjectOverview,
  UpdateProjectInput,
  Workspace,
  WorkspaceOverview,
  WorkspaceResult,
  WorkspaceService
} from "./workspace.types";
import {
  validateCreateProjectInput,
  validateUpdateProjectInput
} from "./workspace.validation";

function success<T>(data: T): WorkspaceResult<T> {
  return { success: true, data };
}

function failure<T>(
  code: Parameters<typeof createWorkspaceError>[0]
): WorkspaceResult<T> {
  return { success: false, error: createWorkspaceError(code) };
}

function isTrustedUser(user: AuthenticatedUserContext | null | undefined): user is AuthenticatedUserContext {
  return Boolean(
    user &&
    user.isAuthenticated === true &&
    typeof user.userId === "string" &&
    user.userId.trim().length > 0
  );
}

function normalizePersistenceError<T>(
  error: unknown,
  fallback: Parameters<typeof createWorkspaceError>[0]
): WorkspaceResult<T> {
  const message =
    typeof error === "object" && error !== null && "message" in error
      ? String((error as { message: unknown }).message).toLowerCase()
      : "";

  if (message.includes("unique") || message.includes("duplicate")) {
    return failure("WORKSPACE_ALREADY_EXISTS");
  }

  return failure(fallback);
}

export function createWorkspaceService(
  repository: WorkspaceRepository,
  goalReader?: GoalSummaryReader
): WorkspaceService {
  async function resolveWorkspace(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext
  ): Promise<WorkspaceResult<Workspace>> {
    if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");

    try {
      const workspace = user.workspaceId
        ? await repository.findWorkspaceById(user.workspaceId)
        : await repository.findWorkspaceByUserId(user.userId);
      if (workspace && workspace.userId !== user.userId) {
        return failure("WORKSPACE_ACCESS_DENIED");
      }
      return workspace
        ? success(workspace)
        : failure("WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND");
    } catch (error) {
      return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
    }
  }

  async function resolveOwnedProject(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    projectId: string
  ): Promise<WorkspaceResult<Project>> {
    if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");
    if (!projectId || !projectId.trim()) return failure("PROJECT_NOT_FOUND");

    try {
      const project = await repository.findProjectById(projectId);
      if (!project) return failure("PROJECT_NOT_FOUND");

      const workspace = user.workspaceId
        ? await repository.findWorkspaceById(user.workspaceId)
        : await repository.findWorkspaceByUserId(user.userId);
      if (!workspace) return failure("WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND");
      if (workspace.userId !== user.userId) return failure("WORKSPACE_ACCESS_DENIED");

      if (project.workspaceId !== workspace.id) {
        return failure("PROJECT_ACCESS_DENIED");
      }

      return success(project);
    } catch (error) {
      return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
    }
  }

  return {
    async listWorkspaces(user) {
      if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      try {
        return success(await repository.listWorkspacesByUserId(user.userId));
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
      }
    },

    async createWorkspace(user, name) {
      if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      const normalizedName = typeof name === "string" ? name.trim() : "";
      if (!normalizedName || normalizedName.length > 80) return failure("WORKSPACE_INVALID_NAME");
      try {
        return success(await repository.createWorkspace(user.userId, normalizedName));
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
      }
    },

    async deleteWorkspace(user, workspaceId) {
      if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      if (!workspaceId?.trim()) return failure("WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND");
      try {
        const workspace = await repository.findWorkspaceById(workspaceId);
        if (!workspace) return failure("WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND");
        if (workspace.userId !== user.userId) return failure("WORKSPACE_ACCESS_DENIED");
        return success(await repository.deleteWorkspace(workspaceId, user.userId));
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
      }
    },

    async initializePersonalWorkspace(user) {
      if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");

      try {
        // Atomic get-or-create is delegated to D01; B02 does not trust client owner IDs.
        const workspace = await repository.getOrCreatePersonalWorkspace(user.userId);
        if (workspace.userId !== user.userId) {
          return failure("WORKSPACE_INVALID_OWNER");
        }
        return success(workspace);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_INITIALIZATION_FAILED");
      }
    },

    async getCurrentWorkspace(user) {
      return resolveWorkspace(user);
    },

    async getWorkspaceOverview(user): Promise<WorkspaceResult<WorkspaceOverview>> {
      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;

      try {
        const projects = await repository.listProjectsByWorkspaceId(
          workspaceResult.data.id
        );

        const goalSummary = goalReader
          ? {
              independentGoalCount: await goalReader.getIndependentGoalCount(
                workspaceResult.data.id
              )
            }
          : null;

        return success({
          workspace: workspaceResult.data,
          projects,
          goalSummary
        });
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
      }
    },

    async createProject(user, input: CreateProjectInput) {
      if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");

      const validation = validateCreateProjectInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return failure(validation.code ?? "PROJECT_INVALID_INPUT");
      }

      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;

      try {
        const project = await repository.createProject(
          workspaceResult.data.id,
          {
            name: input.name.trim(),
            description:
              input.description === undefined || input.description === null
                ? null
                : input.description.trim() || null
          }
        );

        if (project.workspaceId !== workspaceResult.data.id) {
          return failure("WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
        }

        return success(project);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "PROJECT_CREATION_FAILED");
      }
    },

    async getProject(user, projectId) {
      return resolveOwnedProject(user, projectId);
    },

    async listProjects(user) {
      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;

      try {
        const projects = await repository.listProjectsByWorkspaceId(
          workspaceResult.data.id
        );

        return success(
          [...projects].sort((a, b) =>
            b.updatedAt.localeCompare(a.updatedAt)
          )
        );
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
      }
    },

    async updateProject(user, projectId, input: UpdateProjectInput) {
      const ownedProject = await resolveOwnedProject(user, projectId);
      if (!ownedProject.success) return ownedProject;

      const validation = validateUpdateProjectInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return failure(validation.code ?? "PROJECT_INVALID_INPUT");
      }

      const safeInput: UpdateProjectInput = {};
      if (input.name !== undefined) safeInput.name = input.name.trim();
      if (input.description !== undefined) {
        safeInput.description =
          input.description === null ? null : input.description.trim() || null;
      }

      try {
        const updated = await repository.updateProject(projectId, safeInput);

        // A repository implementation must not permit workspace_id or id updates.
        if (
          updated.id !== ownedProject.data.id ||
          updated.workspaceId !== ownedProject.data.workspaceId
        ) {
          return failure("PROJECT_UPDATE_FAILED");
        }

        return success(updated);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "PROJECT_UPDATE_FAILED");
      }
    },

    async getProjectOverview(user, projectId): Promise<WorkspaceResult<ProjectOverview>> {
      const projectResult = await resolveOwnedProject(user, projectId);
      if (!projectResult.success) return projectResult;

      try {
        const goalSummary = goalReader
          ? {
              goalCount: await goalReader.getProjectGoalCount(projectId)
            }
          : null;

        return success({
          project: projectResult.data,
          goalSummary
        });
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
      }
    },

    async validateWorkspaceOwnership(user, workspaceId) {
      if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");

      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;

      if (workspaceResult.data.id !== workspaceId) {
        return failure("WORKSPACE_ACCESS_DENIED");
      }

      return success(true);
    },

    async validateProjectOwnership(user, projectId) {
      const result = await resolveOwnedProject(user, projectId);
      if (!result.success) return result;
      return success(true);
    }
  };
}

FILE: backend\src\modules\workspace\workspace.validation.ts

import type { CreateProjectInput, UpdateProjectInput } from "./workspace.types";

const MAX_PROJECT_NAME_LENGTH = 120;
const MAX_PROJECT_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH = 2000;

export interface WorkspaceValidationResult {
  valid: boolean;
  code?: "PROJECT_INVALID_NAME" | "PROJECT_INVALID_INPUT";
}

export function validateCreateProjectInput(
  input: CreateProjectInput
): WorkspaceValidationResult {
  if (typeof input.name !== "string" || input.name.trim().length === 0) {
    return { valid: false, code: "PROJECT_INVALID_NAME" };
  }

  if (input.name.trim().length > MAX_PROJECT_NAME_LENGTH) {
    return { valid: false, code: "PROJECT_INVALID_NAME" };
  }

  if (
    input.description !== undefined &&
    input.description !== null &&
    (typeof input.description !== "string" ||
      input.description.length > MAX_PROJECT_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH)
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "PROJECT_INVALID_INPUT" };
  }

  return { valid: true };
}

export function validateUpdateProjectInput(
  input: UpdateProjectInput
): WorkspaceValidationResult {
  if (Object.keys(input).length === 0) {
    return { valid: false, code: "PROJECT_INVALID_INPUT" };
  }

  if (
    input.name !== undefined &&
    (typeof input.name !== "string" ||
      input.name.trim().length === 0 ||
      input.name.trim().length > MAX_PROJECT_NAME_LENGTH)
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "PROJECT_INVALID_NAME" };
  }

  if (
    input.description !== undefined &&
    input.description !== null &&
    (typeof input.description !== "string" ||
      input.description.length > MAX_PROJECT_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH)
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "PROJECT_INVALID_INPUT" };
  }

  return { valid: true };
}

FILE: backend\src\server\app.ts

import http from "node:http";
import { createSqliteDatabase, type DatabaseConnection } from "../infrastructure/database/sqlite.ts";
import {
  createApplicationContainer,
  type ApplicationContainer,
  type ContainerConfig
} from "../application/container.ts";
import { createApiHandler } from "../api/index.ts";

export interface AppOptions {
  dbPath?: string;
  connection?: DatabaseConnection;
  config?: ContainerConfig;
  corsOrigin?: string;
}

export interface AppInstance {
  server: http.Server;
  container: ApplicationContainer;
  close: () => Promise<void>;
}

export function createApp(options: AppOptions = {}): AppInstance {
  const connection = options.connection ?? createSqliteDatabase({ dbPath: options.dbPath });
  const container = createApplicationContainer(connection, options.config);
  const corsOrigin = options.corsOrigin ?? "*";

  const apiHandler = createApiHandler(container, corsOrigin);
  const server = http.createServer(apiHandler);

  return {
    server,
    container,
    async close() {
      return new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        if (!server.listening) {
          try {
            connection.close();
          } catch {
            // ignore if already closed
          }
          resolve();
          return;
        }

        server.close((err) => {
          try {
            connection.close();
          } catch {
            // ignore if already closed
          }
          if (err) reject(err);
          else resolve();
        });
      });
    }
  };
}

FILE: backend\src\server\index.ts

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { createApp } from "./app.ts";

// Simple .env reader if .env file exists
function loadEnv(): void {
  const envPath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), ".env");

  if (fs.existsSync(envPath)) {
    try {
      const content = fs.readFileSync(envPath, "utf-8");

      for (const line of content.split("\n")) {
        const trimmed = line.trim();

        if (!trimmed || trimmed.startsWith("#")) continue;

        const eqIdx = trimmed.indexOf("=");

        if (eqIdx !== -1) {
          const key = trimmed.slice(0, eqIdx).trim();
          const val = trimmed
            .slice(eqIdx + 1)
            .trim()
            .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "");

          if (!(key in process.env)) {
            process.env[key] = val;
          }
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }
}

loadEnv();

function positiveLimit(name: string, fallback: number, max: number): number {
  const value = Number(process.env[name] ?? fallback);
  return Number.isFinite(value) ? Math.max(1, Math.min(max, Math.trunc(value))) : fallback;
}

const port = Number(process.env.PORT || 3001);
const dbPath = process.env.DATABASE_PATH || "./data/flow-up.db";
const corsOrigin = process.env.CORS_ORIGIN || "*";

const nodeEnv = process.env.NODE_ENV || "development";
const isProduction = nodeEnv === "production";

const authDevMode = process.env.AUTH_DEV_MODE;

if (
  authDevMode !== undefined &&
  authDevMode !== "true" &&
  authDevMode !== "false"
) {
  throw new Error(
    "AUTH_DEV_MODE must be either 'true' or 'false'."
  );
}

const devMode = !isProduction && authDevMode === "true";

if (isProduction && authDevMode === "true") {
  throw new Error(
    "AUTH_DEV_MODE cannot be enabled in production."
  );
}

if (isProduction && (!process.env.SUPABASE_URL || !process.env.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY)) {
  throw new Error(
    "SUPABASE_URL and SUPABASE_ANON_KEY are required in production."
  );
}

const app = createApp({
  dbPath,
  corsOrigin,
  config: {
    auth: {
      supabaseUrl: process.env.SUPABASE_URL,
      supabaseAnonKey: process.env.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY,
      devMode
    },
    ai: {
      apiKey: process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY,
      model: process.env.GEMINI_MODEL || "gemini-3.8-flash",
      timeoutMs: positiveLimit("AI_TIMEOUT_MS", 20000, 60000),
      perUserDaily: positiveLimit("AI_DAILY_USER_LIMIT", 12, 100),
      globalDaily: positiveLimit("AI_DAILY_GLOBAL_LIMIT", 90, 1000),
    },
  }
});

app.server.listen(port, () => {
  console.log("====================================================");
  console.log(`  FLOW-UP Backend Server running on port ${port}`);
  console.log(`  Health Check: http://localhost:${port}/health`);
  console.log(`  API Base:     http://localhost:${port}/api`);
  console.log(`  Database:     ${dbPath}`);
  console.log(`  Environment:  ${nodeEnv}`);
  console.log(`  Dev Mode:     ${devMode ? "ENABLED" : "DISABLED"}`);
  console.log("====================================================");
});

const shutdown = async () => {
  console.log("\nStopping FLOW-UP server...");

  try {
    await app.close();
    console.log("FLOW-UP server gracefully stopped.");
    process.exit(0);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Error during shutdown:", err);
    process.exit(1);
  }
};

process.on("SIGINT", shutdown);
process.on("SIGTERM", shutdown);

FILE: backend\src\server\init-db.ts

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { createSqliteDatabase } from "../infrastructure/database/sqlite.ts";

function loadEnv(): void {
  const envPath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), ".env");
  if (fs.existsSync(envPath)) {
    try {
      const content = fs.readFileSync(envPath, "utf-8");
      for (const line of content.split("\n")) {
        const trimmed = line.trim();
        if (!trimmed || trimmed.startsWith("#")) continue;
        const eqIdx = trimmed.indexOf("=");
        if (eqIdx !== -1) {
          const key = trimmed.slice(0, eqIdx).trim();
          const val = trimmed.slice(eqIdx + 1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "");
          if (!(key in process.env)) {
            process.env[key] = val;
          }
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }
}

loadEnv();

const dbPath = process.env.DATABASE_PATH || "./data/flow-up.db";
console.log(`Initializing SQLite database at: ${dbPath}...`);

const db = createSqliteDatabase({ dbPath });
console.log("Database schema initialized and foreign keys enabled successfully!");
db.close();
console.log("Database connection closed.");
